# 更新日志 / Changelog

本文件记录 Cebian 的所有重要变更。
All notable changes to Cebian are documented in this file.

格式参考 [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/zh-CN/1.1.0/)，版本号遵循 [语义化版本](https://semver.org/lang/zh-CN/)。
The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/), and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/).

约定 / Conventions

- 所有新条目先写入 `## [Unreleased]`，发版时再整体落成版本节。
- 已发布的版本节不可修改。
- 每个小节正文先列中文、后列英文；来自 issue 的变更附 `(#编号)`。

- New entries go under `## [Unreleased]` first; they are promoted to a version section at release time.
- Released version sections are immutable.
- Each section lists Chinese bullets first, then English bullets; issue-driven changes link `(#number)`.

## [Unreleased]

### 新增 / Added

- 新增「设置 → 对话 → 自动压缩超长对话」：可开关自动压缩，并用滑杆调整触发阈值（占模型上下文窗口的百分比，默认 80%）。此前触发点写死为「窗口 − 16384 token」，换算成百分比会随窗口漂移——128k 模型 87%、1M 模型要到 98.4% 才压，基本等于压不到就已经撑爆 ([#72](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/72))
- 输入框右下角新增上下文用量环：一眼看出当前会话占了模型上下文窗口的多少，点开显示已用 / 总量、自动压缩的触发比例；用量逼近压缩点时环会变色。数字与自动压缩用的是同一套估算，界面上的占用就是压缩判据比较的那个值
- 新增「设置 → 外观」：可调整对话字号（80%–150%）和对话字体（默认 / 衬线 / 等宽，浏览器支持读取本机字体时还可从本机已安装的字体中选择），只作用于消息与输入框，工具卡片等其余界面保持原样，并带实时预览；主题也可在这里直接选择跟随系统、浅色或深色 ([#75](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/75))
- 新增拖拽添加附件：把文件拖到侧边栏的聊天页（消息区或输入框都可以）即可添加，校验规则与点击上传一致，拖入文件夹会提示不支持；同时，在侧边栏其他位置松开文件也不会再被浏览器直接打开、把整个 Cebian 界面替换掉 ([#55](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/55))
- 新增模型：Claude Opus 5.5、Claude Fable 5.1（Anthropic、GitHub Copilot），GPT-6 Sol / Luna / Astra（OpenAI、OpenAI Codex、GitHub Copilot），Grok 4.7（xAI、GitHub Copilot），Gemini 3.8 Flash（Google、GitHub Copilot），DeepSeek V4.1 Flash（DeepSeek）。OpenAI Codex 下的 GPT-6 Sol / Luna 暂不提供「关闭思考」档

- Added Settings → Chat → Auto-compact long chats: a switch for automatic compaction plus a slider for the trigger threshold (a percentage of the model's context window, 80% by default). The trigger used to be hard-coded at "window − 16384 tokens", which drifts with window size — 87% on a 128k model but 98.4% on a 1M one, so large-window models would blow past the limit before compaction ever ran ([#72](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/72))
- Added a context-usage ring next to the composer: see at a glance how much of the model's context window the current chat is using, and click it for the exact amount and the threshold at which earlier history is compacted. The ring changes colour as usage approaches that threshold. It reports the same estimate automatic compaction uses, so the number on screen is exactly the one the threshold is compared against
- Added Settings → Appearance: adjust the chat font size (80%–150%) and chat font (default / serif / monospace, or — where the browser can read installed fonts — any font installed on your device). It applies to messages and the input box only, leaving tool cards and the rest of the interface unchanged, and comes with a live preview. The theme can also be picked here directly: system, light or dark ([#75](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/75))
- Added drag-and-drop attachments: drop files onto the sidepanel chat page — anywhere over the messages or the input box — to attach them, with the same checks as the upload button; dropped folders are reported as unsupported. Dropping a file anywhere else in the sidepanel no longer makes the browser open it in place of the whole Cebian interface ([#55](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/55))
- Added models: Claude Opus 5.5 and Claude Fable 5.1 (Anthropic, GitHub Copilot), GPT-6 Sol / Luna / Astra (OpenAI, OpenAI Codex, GitHub Copilot), Grok 4.7 (xAI, GitHub Copilot), Gemini 3.8 Flash (Google, GitHub Copilot) and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash (DeepSeek). Under OpenAI Codex, GPT-6 Sol and Luna don't offer the "off" thinking level for now

- 新增 Persona / SOUL layer：按 `sahajamit/openclaw-deep-dive/06-soul-persona-system-prompt.md` 的「recency injection」思路，把 persona 副本同时注入两处——(1) `composeSystemPrompt` 在 `<available-workers>` 与 `<user-instructions>` 之间拼出 `<persona>` 块（name + vibe + tone + emoji 头行 + SOUL 副本 + closing directive），(2) `composeUserMessage` 在每条 user message 的 `<reminder-instructions>` 块内追加 1-line recap（紧邻 `<user-request>`，解决「跨多轮后 persona 漂到 context 远处导致 LLM 忽略」问题）。Settings → Persona 页首新增「Enable persona (OpenClaw mode)」Switch（与 authoring 区同页），chat composer 同步加 `<PersonaChip />`（⚡Normal / 🦞OpenClaw 切换，与 Switch 共享 `local:personaEnabled` 通过 `useStorageItem` 的 watch）。Authoring 区含 1 个 SOUL Textarea（2000-char cap）+ 4 个 identity input（name / vibe / tone / emoji）。OFF 时 `<persona>` 块、recap 1-line 都为空，`{{PERSONA_BLOCK}}` / `{{PERSONA_BINDING}}` 占位符替换为空字符串 + `collapseTripleNewlines` → prompt byte-shape 与 pre-Subtask-2 完全一致，Anthropic prompt cache 仍可命中。Recap 用例：Worker Team 提醒在前，persona recap 在后，两者 OFF 时都为空，wrapper 仍输出旧 byte shape。`rewindAndResume()`（retry/edit）路径与 `composeSystemPrompt` 在 `normal send` 路径共用同一份 `personaOn` snapshot，确保 retry 后工具 / system prompt / user reminder 三块同步 persona-on/off。Storage: `local:personaEnabled` / `local:persona` / `local:personaIdentity`。新 i18n keys: `settings.persona.*` (title/enabled/enabledHint/label/hint/5 字段 placeholder), `chat.composer.persona.{normal,openclaw,toggleHint}`, `settings.nav.persona`, `agent.persona.{closingDirective,recap.{youAre,vibe,tone,emoji}}`（三语 en/zh_CN/zh_TW 对齐）。复用已有 `useStorageItem` 双向同步、Worker Team chip + Settings card layout、prompt-envelope 的 `wrapReminderInstructions` helper。
- New Persona / SOUL layer. Following `sahajamit/openclaw-deep-dive/06-soul-persona-system-prompt.md`'s recency-injection pattern, the persona copy is injected in two places: (1) `composeSystemPrompt` renders a `<persona>` block (name + vibe + tone + emoji header line + SOUL copy + closing directive) between `<available-workers>` and `<user-instructions>`; (2) `composeUserMessage` appends a 1-line recap inside `<reminder-instructions>` adjacent to `<user-request>`, fixing the drift complaint where persona sits thousands of tokens away from the current question after turn 30. The "Enable persona (OpenClaw mode)" switch sits at the top of Settings → Persona, on the same page as the authoring surface; the chat composer mirrors the toggle via a new `<PersonaChip />` (⚡Normal / 🦞OpenClaw). The authoring surface holds 1 SOUL Textarea at 2000-char cap + 4 identity inputs: name / vibe / tone / emoji. When OFF, the `<persona>` block and 1-line recap are both empty; `{{PERSONA_BLOCK}}` / `{{PERSONA_BINDING}}` placeholders resolve to '' + `collapseTripleNewlines` keeps prompt byte-shape identical to pre-Subtask-2 baseline so Anthropic prompt caching still hits. Recap stacks after the Worker Team reminder inside the same `<reminder-instructions>` block; both OFF yields the old byte shape. `rewindAndResume()` retry/edit path shares the same `personaOn` snapshot with `composeSystemPrompt`'s normal-send path so tools / system prompt / user reminder flip in lockstep when the user toggles the persona switch. Storage: `local:personaEnabled` / `local:persona` / `local:personaIdentity`. New i18n keys: `settings.persona.*` (title/enabled/enabledHint/label/hint/5 field placeholders), `chat.composer.persona.{normal,openclaw,toggleHint}`, `settings.nav.persona`, `agent.persona.{closingDirective,recap.{youAre,vibe,tone,emoji}}` (parity across en/zh_CN/zh_TW). Reuses existing `useStorageItem` two-way sync, Worker Team chip + Settings card layout, and the prompt-envelope `wrapReminderInstructions` helper.
- `/vfs` tab now ships a per-row action menu: hovering a row reveals a `MoreVertical` kebab menu with Copy name / Copy path / Copy / Cut / Download / Edit / Rename / Delete. Copy / Cut populate a module-level `lib/clipboard-buffer.ts` (in-tab across directories; `cut` auto-clears on successful paste). The dir Toolbar grows New File / New Folder / Paste buttons (Paste auto-disables when the buffer is empty). DirView gets a multi-select banner + checkbox column: Ctrl+A in a dir view selects every entry, the banner reads `N selected · Select all · Cancel`, selection auto-resets when the user navigates to a different directory. Rename / Delete refuse paths that match a structural VFS root (`/`, `/workspaces`, `~/.cebian/skills`, `~/.cebian/prompts`); Delete recursively walks `rmRecursive` to clear subtrees; New File / Folder reject names containing `/` or `..`. Reuses the existing `lib/ui/clipboard.ts` `copyText` helper and `lib/persistence/vfs.ts` mutation surface; no new IPC. New i18n keys: `vfs.action.{copy,cut,copyName,copyPath}`, `vfs.prompt.rename`, `vfs.error.{invalidName,cannotDeleteProtected}` (parity across en/zh_CN/zh_TW).

- 新增 worker stuck-loop 防御：L2 stuck detector 在 `frontend_coder` worker 触发「同 path 读 ≥4 次」或「写后连续读 ≥6 次」时立刻 `composedController.abort()` 并打 `sub_agent:worker:stuck_loop:trigger` 事件；过去这种「Minimax-M3 / vilao.ai / 9Router 等 local OpenAI-compatible proxy」确定会卡住的循环只能等满 5 分钟 `per-role ceiling`，现在 abort 时间降到 ≤60s；researcher / reviewer 等没有 write tool 的 role 不会触发 post-write threshold B（`hasWrittenThisAttempt` 永远 false），同 path threshold A 只在 `fs_read_file` 同 path 字面相等时才累加，`fs_list` 无 path 会 reset 计数器，避免误抓合法多文件浏览流程
- 新增 worker file-rescue 兜底路径：L2 stuck_loop abort 后若 `outputFileExists === true`（worker 在 loop 前已经写完文件），用单独的 `synthesizeStuckLoopHandoff` helper 合成最小 success handoff（`status:'success'` + `output_file` + 标注「stuck-loop detected, output file authoritative」），让 `assembleHandoff` 走 branch 6 success 路径。Summary / handoff_notes 文案与 `synthesizeSilentWriteHandoff`（Subtask 8.8 Fix Y）刻意区分，方便 debug log grep 时区分两种 fallback 路径；`failureReason='stuck_loop'` 在 `assembleHandoff` branch 1 retryable override 里被显式排除（deterministic 行为，retry 不会改变结果）——`shouldRetry` 因 gated on `retryable !== true` 零改动自动 false。L3 rescue 路径同时把 `readOutputIfAny` 拿到的 `r.content` 挂到 `output_content`（之前只读 `r.exists` 把 `content` 丢掉了），主代理 `--- output_content (...) ---` marker 能渲染出 worker 实际写的产物，与 silent-write fallback 行为对齐。若 loop 在写之前就触发（无 file 可救），catch block 文案改为「Worker aborted: stuck-loop detected …」而不是误导性的「Worker timed out after Xms」，看 debug log 时一眼能区分是 model loop 触发 detector 而不是 ceiling 烧满
- New `stuck_loop` defense for workers: the L2 stuck detector aborts the `frontend_coder` runner and logs `sub_agent:worker:stuck_loop:trigger` the moment it sees either "≥4 reads of the same path" or "≥6 consecutive reads after a write". Previously this deterministic loop pattern seen with local OpenAI-compatible proxies (Minimax-M3 via vilao.ai / 9Router) had to burn the full 5-minute `per-role ceiling`; abort now lands within ≤60s. Researcher / reviewer never trip the post-write threshold B (their `hasWrittenThisAttempt` stays `false`); the same-path threshold A only counts literal `fs_read_file` path equality, and `fs_list` without a path resets the counter — legal multi-file browsing flows are not falsely caught
- New file-rescue fallback for stuck workers: when L2 stuck_loop aborts and the output file is already on disk (worker wrote it before looping), a dedicated `synthesizeStuckLoopHandoff` helper synthesizes a minimal success handoff (`status:'success'` + `output_file` + a note flagging "stuck-loop detected, output file authoritative") and feeds it through `assembleHandoff`'s branch 6 success path. The summary / handoff_notes text is deliberately distinct from `synthesizeSilentWriteHandoff` (Subtask 8.8 Fix Y) so debug log greps can tell the two fallback paths apart. `failureReason='stuck_loop'` is explicitly excluded from `assembleHandoff`'s branch 1 retryable override (deterministic behavior — retry would not change the outcome); `shouldRetry` is gated on `retryable !== true`, so it returns `false` automatically with zero changes. The L3 rescue path also attaches `output_content` from `readOutputIfAny` (now captures `r.content` instead of only `r.exists`), so the main agent sees the rescued file's body via the `--- output_content (...) ---` marker — symmetric with the silent-write fallback's behaviour. When the loop fires before any write (no file to rescue), the catch block now reports `"Worker aborted: stuck-loop detected …"` instead of the misleading ceiling-style "Worker timed out after Xms" message, so users looking at the debug log know the root cause is a model loop, not the role's per-attempt ceiling


- 知识库（RAG）新增 agentic `rag_search` 工具（Subtask 4，按需启用）：`RagSettings` 增加 `ragSearchEnabled` 字段（默认 OFF），开启后主代理可在对话中主动调用 `rag_search({ collection, query, limit })` 对指定 collection 跑 hybrid (vector + BM25 / RRF) 查询，补足 send-time 预注入 top-5 chunks 之外的深度检索。返回结构化 `<rag-search-result>` envelope（`collection` / `query` / `count` / `reason` + `<chunk source index context>`），LLM 可直接解析引用。`limit` 默认 5、上限 20；chunk 按相关度顺序排列，envelope 不带数值分数。读路径新增嵌入模型守卫：查询前先按 Neon 里该 collection 的 `(embedModel, embedDim)` 对账，模型不符直接报错（不再静默返回跨向量空间的无效分数），存在无法验证模型的旧数据行时告警提示重索引。空结果按成因区分——`reason="no_match"`（有数据但没命中）与 `reason="collection_not_found"`（名字不存在或 collection 为空，附本机已知名字建议，且在嵌入前直接返回、不浪费一次 API 调用）。`execute()` 内置 belt-and-braces 安全网（即便 stale session 把 tool 列进了 list，关闭开关后调用仍抛错不执行）；`lib/tools/index.ts` 在 `buildSessionToolArray` 阶段按「全局开关 OR 本会话 ghim 了解锁」条件 push——ghim 了 RAG collection 的会话即使全局开关关闭也会自动获得 `rag_search`（证据 = 每轮 send 的 attachment 里的 pinned rag-context，tool list 与 system prompt 由同一 snapshot 驱动），既没 ghim 也没开开关时该工具既不在 tool list 也不在 system prompt 出现，LLM 不会幻觉调用。系统提示注入走 `{{RAG_SEARCH_TOOL_LINE}}` 与 `{{RAG_SEARCH_WORKFLOW_STEP}}` 两个占位符（`entrypoints/background/agent/prompt-composer.ts` 装配），关闭时占位符折叠为空字符串 + `collapseTripleNewlines` 后处理，使 OFF 状态 system prompt 逐字节与 Subtask 4 前相同（保住 Anthropic prompt cache）
- Knowledge (RAG): New agentic `rag_search` tool (Subtask 4, opt-in). `RagSettings` gains `ragSearchEnabled` (default OFF). When on, the main agent can call `rag_search({ collection, query, limit })` mid-conversation to run a hybrid (vector + BM25 / RRF) query against the named collection — complementing the send-time pre-injected top-5 chunks when the user asks for a specific section number, function name, or code identifier that the envelope does not cover. The tool returns a structured `<rag-search-result>` envelope (`collection` / `query` / `count` / `reason` + `<chunk source index context>`), XML-escaped for parseable downstream consumption. `limit` defaults to 5 and caps at 20; chunks arrive in relevance order and the envelope carries no numeric score. The read path gains an embedder guard: before querying it reconciles the collection's `(embedModel, embedDim)` from Neon, erroring on a model mismatch instead of silently returning meaningless cross-space scores, and warning (with a re-index hint) when rows predate model tracking. An empty result is split by cause — `reason="no_match"` (data exists, nothing matched) vs `reason="collection_not_found"` (name missing / collection empty, with on-device known-name suggestions, returned before embedding so no API call is wasted). Belt-and-braces safety: `execute()` re-reads settings and throws if disabled, so a stale session whose tool list still includes `rag_search` cannot actually invoke it after the user toggles off; `lib/tools/index.ts` pushes the tool when the global switch is on OR the session has a pinned RAG collection — pinning a collection auto-enables `rag_search` for that conversation even with the switch off (evidence = the pinned rag-context attachment on every send; tool list and system prompt are driven by the same snapshot). With neither a pin nor the switch, the tool is absent from both the tool list and the system prompt — the LLM has no way to hallucinate a call. System-prompt augmentation uses `{{RAG_SEARCH_TOOL_LINE}}` and `{{RAG_SEARCH_WORKFLOW_STEP}}` placeholders, wired through `entrypoints/background/agent/prompt-composer.ts`. When off, both resolve to `''` and `collapseTripleNewlines` collapses the resulting triple-newline runs so the prompt is byte-identical to pre-Subtask-4, preserving Anthropic prompt caching
- 新增 Worker Team 总开关（Fast / Team 模式切换）：在 Settings → Advanced → Worker team 卡片新增「启用 Worker 团队」开关，并在聊天输入框工具栏加入 ⚡Fast / 👥Team 切换芯片——两处共享同一个 storage 标识（`local:workerTeamEnabled`），通过 `useStorageItem` 的 watch 自动双向同步。默认关闭（Team 为 opt-in 进阶能力——未开启时主代理直接走原生 `fs_*` 工具、系统提示不注入 `<available-workers>` 块）；关闭时主代理不再暴露 `delegate_task` 工具、系统提示也不再注入 `<available-workers>` L1 块，主代理改用原生 `fs_create_file` / `fs_edit_file` / `fs_read_file` 等工具直接产出 artifact，整体产出时间从 30–60s 缩短到 3–5s。两侧 gate（tool registration + system prompt block）由同一个 flag 同步驱动，与 `rag_search` 的「tool + prompt must agree」模式一致——任一侧单独撤掉都会让 LLM 幻觉调用或反之不知何时用 worker。Per-role `workerModels` 配置不受影响（OFF 状态下保留 UI 供预先配置），`delegate_dom` 独立于本开关不受波及。In-flight：用户在半轮切换时正在跑的 worker 不会被取消（YAGNI），新状态从下一轮 `composeSystemPrompt` 调用（即下一次派发）开始生效
- New Worker Team master switch (Fast / Team mode toggle). Settings → Advanced → Worker team card gains an "Enable worker team" switch, and the chat composer toolbar gains a ⚡Fast / 👥Team chip — both share the same storage key (`local:workerTeamEnabled`) and stay in lockstep via `useStorageItem`'s watch. Defaults to OFF (Team is opt-in — until enabled, the main agent works directly with the native `fs_*` tools and no `<available-workers>` block is injected). When OFF the main agent no longer sees `delegate_task` in its tool list and the system prompt no longer injects the `<available-workers>` L1 block; the agent writes artifacts directly via the native `fs_create_file` / `fs_edit_file` / `fs_read_file` family, dropping end-to-end artifact time from 30–60s down to 3–5s. The two-sided gate (tool registration + prompt block) is driven by the same flag, mirroring the `rag_search` "tool + prompt must agree" pattern — withdrawing one side alone would either let the LLM hallucinate a non-existent tool call or leave it unable to discover the capability. Per-role `workerModels` configuration is untouched (the four role pickers stay visible while OFF so users can preconfigure and flip the switch later); `delegate_dom` is independent of this switch. In-flight: a worker already running when the user toggles keeps running to completion (YAGNI); the new state takes effect on the next `composeSystemPrompt` call (the next dispatch).
- 知识库（RAG）新增 Contextual Retrieval（上下文检索，Subtask 3，按需启用）：在 `RagSettings` 增加 `contextualRetrievalEnabled` / `contextualLlmBaseUrl` / `contextualLlmApiKey` / `contextualLlmModel` 四个字段（默认全 OFF；端点默认复用本地 CLIProxyAPI）。开启后，索引流水线在「分块 → 嵌入」之间插入 `context-generation` 阶段，逐 chunk 调用轻量 LLM 生成 ≤ 40 词的上下文前缀，前缀会同时拼接到嵌入输入与 `metadata.context_prefix`（Subtask 2 的 `content_tsv` 表达式已通过 `COALESCE` 取该值，使 BM25 路径无需迁移即可吃到前缀）。长文档（> 40K chars）走「标题 + 大纲 + 前后 1–2 段」精简上下文，目标 ≤ 8K chars。LLM 调用失败/返回空均降级为空字符串（chunk 仍正常入库，仅丢失本次召回加成），并发 8。`contextualLlmApiKey` 作为密钥分类进备份系统（与 `embedderApiKey` 同形态），用户从备份恢复时按 merge/replace 策略一并还原
- Knowledge (RAG): Contextual Retrieval (Subtask 3, opt-in). `RagSettings` gains four new fields — `contextualRetrievalEnabled`, `contextualLlmBaseUrl`, `contextualLlmApiKey`, `contextualLlmModel` — all OFF by default, with the endpoint defaulting to the local CLIProxyAPI. When enabled, the indexer inserts a `context-generation` phase between chunking and embedding: each chunk is shipped to a cheap LLM that produces a ≤ 40-word context prefix. The prefix is prepended to the embed input **and** stored in `metadata.context_prefix`; the Subtask 2 `content_tsv` expression already picks it up via `COALESCE`, so the BM25 path gains the recall boost with no migration. Long documents (> 40K chars) use a slim envelope (title + outline + 1–2 surrounding paragraphs) capped at 8K chars. LLM failures / empty responses gracefully degrade to `''` — the chunk still indexes, it just loses the recall boost for that one chunk — at concurrency 8. `contextualLlmApiKey` is classified as a secret in the backup registry (same shape as `embedderApiKey`) and follows merge / replace strategy on restore
- 知识库（RAG）设置页重新组织：顶部是健康条（Database / Embedder / Collections 三枚状态指示，各自带独立的检查按钮），下面紧跟知识库列表——日常操作的对象，放在最前；再往下是检索与排序。连接串与嵌入模型收进默认折叠的「数据来源」，分块参数与 Contextual Retrieval 收进同样默认折叠的「高级」；检索与排序区只保留检索模式（Hybrid [Recommended] / Vector only，默认 Hybrid，配子标签解释 dense + sparse 融合策略）与 Rerank 开关，以及 agentic `rag_search` 工具开关。Contextual Retrieval 默认关闭，开启后展开 3 个 LLM 端点输入（Base URL / API key / model），并在索引前提示「每个 chunk 约 1 次 LLM 调用」的成本。另加三步上手指引（连接数据库 → 配置嵌入模型 → 创建知识库），有知识库后自动消失；知识库列表的表头下多一行说明「磁盘上的文件夹是事实来源，改那里再重新索引」——而不是指望在界面里直接编辑来源。新建知识库的对话框改为「先选文件夹」：主按钮不再需要先填名字才能点，选完文件夹后名称自动按文件夹名填好（仍可改），设置页与侧边栏现在共用同一个对话框（此前两份各自维护、已经出现不一致）；「重新索引」改称「从文件夹更新」，并去掉了重复的选源入口——以前同一件事有三个控件（选文件夹按钮、collection 下拉、行内 Pick folder），现在只剩一个。「知识库名称」输入框只在新建时出现：更新时名字已固定，改名会让它与磁盘上的文件夹不再对应
- Knowledge (RAG) settings reorganized: a health strip at the top (three status indicators — Database / Embedder / Collections — each with its own check button), immediately followed by the collections list, which is what you actually touch day to day, then Retrieval & Ranking. The connection string and embedding model move into a collapsed "Data sources" block, and chunking parameters plus Contextual Retrieval into an equally collapsed Advanced block; Retrieval & Ranking keeps only the retrieval mode radio (Hybrid [Recommended] / Vector only, default Hybrid, with a sub-label explaining the dense + sparse fusion) plus the Rerank toggle and the agentic `rag_search` tool toggle. Contextual Retrieval is off by default; turning it on expands three LLM endpoint inputs (Base URL / API key / model) and warns about the cost (roughly one LLM call per chunk) before indexing. Also added a three-step setup guide (connect the database → configure the embedder → create a collection) that disappears once you have one, and a line under the collections heading stating that the folder on disk is the source of truth — so the workflow reads as "edit on disk, then reindex" rather than editing sources in the UI. The new-collection dialog is now folder-first: the primary button no longer requires a name before you can click it, the collection name is filled in from the folder once picked (still editable), Settings and the sidebar now share one dialog implementation (they were two copies that had already drifted), and "Reindex" is renamed to "Update from folder" — the duplicate source pickers are gone, so one action (choose a folder) replaces the three that used to do the same thing (a folder button, a collection dropdown, and an inline Pick folder). The collection-name field now appears only when creating: on update the name is fixed, and renaming would break its correspondence with the folder on disk
- MCP 服务器卡片支持拖动排序：Settings → MCP 和 sidebar drawer 两处的卡片都新增了 grip 拖把手（仅把手接收 `useSortable` 的 pointer/keyboard 监听器，开关 / 编辑 / 删除按钮不会误触发拖动）；按住把手拖动即可调整顺序，松手后写入持久化存储并通过 `useStorageItem` 的 watch 回调推回 UI，刷新页面后顺序保持；同样支持键盘操作（Tab 聚焦把手 → Space 抓起 → 方向键移动 → Space 落下 / Escape 取消），reorder 不修改 `updatedAt`，因为记录本身没变
- 新增跨 context phase tracer 用于 send→reply 流水线的临时性能诊断：新增 `lib/debug/trace.ts` 暴露 `startTrace(source, sessionId, initialT0?)`，所有 marker 形状统一为 `{ t0, deltaMs, ...extra, sessionId }`，按 `t0`+`deltaMs` 即可重建 timeline。`performance.now()` 跨 renderer ↔ SW 的 origin 不同，因此 renderer 在 `chat:t0` 时捕获锚点并通过 IPC `prompt.t0` 透传给 BG，BG 与 hook 各自以同锚点建 handle 让 Δt 跨 context 可比。`initialT0 === undefined` 严格区分「未传」与「传 0」，mark() 内部从 `extra` 里剥掉 `t0`/`deltaMs` 防止 caller 误覆盖。覆盖阶段：`ui:chat:t0`（按下 Enter）/ `hook:recv_agent_start` / `hook:first_token`（用户感知 TTFB）/ `bg:prompt_received` / `bg:agent_ready` / `bg:prompt_composed` / `bg:system_prompt` / `bg:compaction` / `bg:agent_dispatched` / `bg:agent_start` / `bg:first_token`（模型 TTFB）/ `bg:token_n`（每 10 token 一次）/ `bg:message_end` / `bg:agent_end_pre_sync` / `bg:agent_end_post_sync` / `bg:title_gen_start` / `bg:title_gen_done`。每条 teardown 路径（`agent_end` / `cancel()` running-idle / `destroySession` / `commitCompactionCancel` / compaction-cancelled / 双 liveness guard）都通过 `releaseTrace(sessionId)` 主动释放 `pendingTraces` / `firstTokenSeen` / `tokenCounts` 三个临时表，避免 per-session Map/Set 永久占位。开关：Settings → About → Debug log 开启后才会落 IDB，UI 端可开 Live log 实时查看
- 压缩摘要结构化 (Subtask 4)：LLM 生成的压缩摘要现附带结构化 JSON（`schema_version=1`，含 `goal` / `constraints` / `progress` / `decisions` / `next_steps` / `critical_context`），原始 LLM 输出同时归档到 VFS `/workspaces/<sessionId>/compaction/<ISO>-<rand>.json` 供事后审视。UI 渲染保持 `<CompactionDivider />` 不变；JSON 解析失败时降级为纯 Markdown 摘要（与既有行为兼容，`structured` 字段缺席不显式置 null）。归档采用 `.tmp` + rename 原子写，规避 session 销毁 (`vfs.rm recursive`) 与归档写入的竞态
- 主动压缩静默失败时弹 toast (Subtask 5)：80% 预检触发后若 `findCompactionCutPoint` 返回 `cut <= 0`（典型场景：单条用户消息本身就超过 `keepRecentTokens`，前面没有可摘要的历史），BG 广播一条新的 IPC `compaction_skipped`（`reason: 'cut_no_op'` + 当前 token / 上下文窗口 / 保留预算 / 消息数），sidepanel 通过新模块 `lib/agent/compaction-sidepanel-channel.ts` + `hooks/useCompactionToasts.ts` 用 Sonner 弹一条瞬时提醒「上下文过满无法自动压缩（已用 XX%）。可尝试删除大段粘贴内容或开启新会话。」让用户在撞 400 之前知情。常规路径 `cut <= 0` 继续静默——它要么成功压缩，要么最终走 `context_overflow` 卡片，重复提醒只会干扰；事件走 fire-and-forget，不写 `AgentPortState`、不触发 trace sentinel 清理

- MCP server cards now support drag-to-reorder: both Settings → MCP and the sidebar drawer render each card with a new grip handle on the left as the drag activator (only the handle receives `useSortable` pointer/keyboard listeners, so the toggle / edit / delete buttons never accidentally start a drag); press and drag the grip to reorder, drop writes to persistent storage and the change flows back through `useStorageItem`'s watch callback, order survives reload; keyboard reorder works too (Tab to grip → Space to pick up → Arrow keys to move → Space to drop / Escape to cancel), reorder does NOT bump `updatedAt` since the records themselves are unchanged
- Added a cross-context phase tracer for temporary performance diagnosis of the send→reply pipeline: new `lib/debug/trace.ts` exposes `startTrace(source, sessionId, initialT0?)`; every marker has the uniform shape `{ t0, deltaMs, ...extra, sessionId }`, sortable on `t0`+`deltaMs` to reconstruct the timeline. Because `performance.now()` has different origins across renderer ↔ SW, the renderer captures the anchor at `chat:t0` and ships it through the IPC `prompt.t0` field, then BG and hook each create their own handle with that same anchor so Δt is comparable across contexts. `initialT0 === undefined` strictly distinguishes "not passed" from "passed 0"; `mark()` strips `t0`/`deltaMs` from `extra` to prevent caller tampering. Phases covered: `ui:chat:t0` (Enter pressed) / `hook:recv_agent_start` / `hook:first_token` (user-perceived TTFB) / `bg:prompt_received` / `bg:agent_ready` / `bg:prompt_composed` / `bg:system_prompt` / `bg:compaction` / `bg:agent_dispatched` / `bg:agent_start` / `bg:first_token` (model TTFB) / `bg:token_n` (every 10 tokens) / `bg:message_end` / `bg:agent_end_pre_sync` / `bg:agent_end_post_sync` / `bg:title_gen_start` / `bg:title_gen_done`. Every teardown path (`agent_end` / `cancel()` running-idle / `destroySession` / `commitCompactionCancel` / compaction-cancelled / both liveness guards) calls `releaseTrace(sessionId)` to actively evict the per-session `pendingTraces` / `firstTokenSeen` / `tokenCounts` and prevent permanent placeholder growth. Toggled by Settings → About → Debug log; the Live log view streams markers in real time when the dialog is open
- Compaction summary now ships a structured JSON side-channel and a per-event VFS archive (Subtask 4): the LLM is asked to append a fenced JSON block (`schema_version: 1`, fields `goal` / `constraints` / `progress` / `decisions` / `next_steps` / `critical_context`) at the end of its existing Markdown summary; the parser walks fences from last to first, runs each candidate through `JSON.parse` and a strict `isStructuredSummary` schema guard (rejects wrong types, missing fields, `decisions[i]` not an object, etc.), then falls back to whole-output parsing if no fence matches. On parse success the result is attached to the `compactionSummary` message via a new optional `structured?` field (declared via module augmentation on pi-agent-core's `CompactionSummaryMessage`); on failure the field is **absent** rather than `null`, so existing `if (msg.structured)` guards work unchanged. The full LLM output (Markdown + JSON fence), the structured JSON, model identity, token counts and the compact-event timestamp are also archived as a single JSON manifest at `/workspaces/<sessionId>/compaction/<ISO>-<rand>.json` (ISO timestamp with `:` and `.` replaced by `-`, 6-char hex random suffix; collision probability within a single ms is ~2⁻²⁴). The archive write is atomic (`.tmp` + `vfs.rename`) so a session-destroy running concurrently with the archive write never leaves a half-written file. UI rendering (`<CompactionDivider />`) is unchanged; the structured JSON is invisible to the user today and is queryable by future inspection tooling. Archive failures are best-effort (`debugLog.warn` only) and never block the compaction path
- Proactive compaction silent no-op now surfaces a Sonner toast (Subtask 5): when the 80% pre-check fires but `findCompactionCutPoint` returns `cut <= 0` (typically because a single user message alone exceeds `keepRecentTokens` and there is no earlier transcript to summarize), the BG broadcasts a new IPC `compaction_skipped` (`reason: 'cut_no_op'` + current tokens / context window / keep-recent budget / messages count); the sidepanel fans this through a new `lib/agent/compaction-sidepanel-channel.ts` pub/sub bridge and `hooks/useCompactionToasts.ts` (single-mount subscriber pattern, mirroring `useRecorder.installRejectionToastsOnce`) to toast "Context too full to compact (XX% used). Try dropping a large paste or starting a new conversation." — heads-up before the next request 400s. The regular-path `cut <= 0` stays silent: that path either compacts successfully or eventually surfaces the `context_overflow` card, so an additional toast would only duplicate the same underlying signal. The event is fire-and-forget — no `AgentPortState` mutation, no trace-sentinel cleanup, no client response message
- `/vfs` 标签页中 `.html` / `.htm` 文件现在可内嵌预览：在「Preview」模式下渲染到一个新的 MV3 sandbox entrypoint `entrypoints/html-preview.sandbox/`，由它通过 postMessage 收到的 HTML 字符串在内部沙盒 iframe 中执行（`sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-forms allow-modals"`，故意不放 `allow-popups` / `allow-top-navigation`；`allow-modals` 让 `alert()` / `confirm()` / `prompt()` 之类 modal 能弹出）。inline `<script>` / DOMContentLoaded handler 能跑是因为 sandbox 页面继承了 manifest `sandbox` CSP（包含 `'unsafe-inline'`），而 extension pages 的 `script-src 'self'` 是 MV3 强制的（Chrome 110+ 在安装时就拒绝 `'unsafe-inline'`，无法绕开）。sandbox 页面是 opaque origin，`allow-same-origin` 不会让预览内容逃逸到 sidepanel 的扩展权限域。保留「Preview / View source」切换，源码模式走 `<pre>`（iframe 仍挂在 DOM 里以保留脚本状态）。原 raw-source 渲染路径已替换——`.html` 不再走 TEXT 桶
- `.html` / `.htm` files in the `/vfs` tab can now be previewed inline: the host page embeds a dedicated MV3 sandbox entrypoint (`entrypoints/html-preview.sandbox/`) which receives the document via postMessage and renders it inside an inner iframe (`sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-forms allow-modals"`, deliberately excluding `allow-popups` / `allow-top-navigation`; `allow-modals` enables `alert()` / `confirm()` / `prompt()`). Inline `<script>` and DOMContentLoaded handlers run because the sandbox page inherits the permissive manifest `sandbox` CSP (`'unsafe-inline'`); the extension-pages CSP cannot be widened to allow inline scripts (Chrome MV3 enforces `script-src 'self'` for `extension_pages` and refuses `'unsafe-inline'` at install time). The sandbox page's opaque origin makes `allow-same-origin` harmless — there is no privileged origin for the previewed document to escape into. Preview / View source toggle is preserved; in source mode the iframe stays mounted (just hidden) so script state survives a quick source peek. The raw-source path is replaced — `.html` no longer falls into the TEXT bucket
- 新增 `delegate_task` 工具，支持把长任务委派给 4 种固定的 worker role（`content_writer` / `frontend_coder` / `reviewer` / `researcher`）；worker 跑在隔离 sub-agent 上下文里，**通过 VFS 文件而非聊天 context 传递产物**（input_files 读、output_path 写），主代理 context 不被污染——拿到的只是一份 ≤4 KB 的结构化 handoff JSON（`status` / `output_file` / `summary` / `handoff_notes`）。可选 `expected_schema` 参数支持用 TypeBox JSON Schema 校验 worker 输出，校验失败自动单次自愈重试；可选 `skills` 参数自动注入 `~/.cebian/skills/<name>/SKILL.md`（复用 mention-resolver 的 `stripFrontmatter` + 100 KB truncate）；可选 `anti_patterns` 参数包装成 `<do-not-do>` 块 prepend 到 prompt；内置单次重试覆盖机械性失败（JSON 解析失败、schema 校验失败、worker 声明成功但 output_file 缺失）— 不需要主代理介入
- Settings → Advanced 新增 Worker Models 区域（紧跟 DOM sub-agent model），可给每个 role 配置专用模型（默认继承主模型）；侧边栏同步新增可折叠 Team Roster widget 快速切换，与 Settings 共享 storage。chat 流中委派执行时新增 inline `DelegationCard`（折叠 / 展开 + status badge + role icon + 重试 badge + output 文件 VFS 预览按钮），按 role 显示专门配色与图标

- Added the `delegate_task` tool — main agent can offload long tasks to one of four fixed worker roles (`content_writer` / `frontend_coder` / `reviewer` / `researcher`). Workers run in isolated sub-agent context and **communicate via VFS files** (read input_files, write output_path) instead of polluting the main agent's context; the main agent only receives a ≤4 KB structured handoff JSON (`status` / `output_file` / `summary` / `handoff_notes`). Optional `expected_schema` parameter validates worker output against a TypeBox JSON Schema with automatic single-pass self-healing retry; optional `skills` parameter auto-injects `~/.cebian/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` (reuses mention-resolver's `stripFrontmatter` + 100 KB truncate); optional `anti_patterns` is wrapped into a `<do-not-do>` block prepended to the prompt; the built-in single-pass retry covers mechanical failures (JSON parse error, schema validation error, worker-declared success with missing output file) with no main-agent intervention
- Settings → Advanced gains a Worker Models section (right after the DOM sub-agent model) for per-role model override (default inherits the main session model); the sidebar gets a collapsible Team Roster widget mirroring the same storage for quick mid-task swaps. During delegation the chat stream renders an inline `DelegationCard` (collapsible header + status badge + role icon + retry badge + output-file VFS preview button) with role-specific colors and icons
- 主代理 system prompt 新增 `<available-workers>` L1 索引：4 种固定 worker role 各占一个 `<worker>` 子块（`<role>` / `<description>` / `<example>`），加一段 prescriptive preamble 以 **DEFAULT to `delegate_task`** 为极性——遇到「写 / build / create / generate / draft X」类会产生非平凡 artifact 的请求一律 delegate（即使主代理有原生工具能直接做），仅「短答案 Q&A / 多轮迭代 / 单次工具调用」三类例外才允许自己动手，并附具体数字（`Build a Web Studio` 走 `frontend_coder` ≈50 token handoff vs 自己动手 30+ 次工具调用），让主代理一眼看见"native tool 一旦开打，tool result 全进主代理 context、最后只产出一份文件"的代价曲线，主动把"该不该 delegate"放进决策视野，不再仅依赖 schema description 的被动描述；block 由 `lib/agent/worker-roles.ts:buildAvailableWorkersBlock` 渲染（与 `<skills>` 块同 XML 包裹、同"先扫索引再决定"协议），4 个 role key 受 `Record<WorkerRole, ...>` 编译期兜底，新增/删除 role 必须同步更新 description + example。顺序：base → skills → available-workers → user-instructions，全部紧贴 base 让 system prompt 命中 cache 前缀（registry 不变则字节不变）
- Worker schema 校验前的自动截断（Postel's Law）：在 `assembleHandoff` 调 `checkSchema` 之前，新增 pure helper `autoTruncateHandoff(parsed, schema)` 走 schema 的 `properties` 树，对每个带 `maxLength` 的 string 字段（reviewer 的是 `summary=200` / `checklist.items.evidence=200` / `checklist.items.item=64`；未来别的角色扩 schema 也自动生效）把超长值截成 `slice(0, maxLength - 3) + '...'`；归零于 schema literal，不在 runner 写死 cap；`handoff_notes` 这种 schema 没有 `maxLength` 的自由文本字段不会被截（reviewer 写 bug-report context 1000+ chars 仍合法）；schema 本身不是合法 JSON 走原本的 `Invalid expected_schema` 早返回，不走 clamp。`parsed` argument 不可变（helper 永远返回 shallow-clone）。针对 `cebian-debug-20260909-211043.json` attempt 10 复现：reviewer 给 `summary` 250 chars，Phase 1 旧逻辑 reject 后单次自愈重试仍 fail 同长度，导致 attempts 2 次全失败；现在截到 200 chars（197+`...`）后 schema 一次过、`assembleHandoff` branch 6 success path。Shape errors（type / required / pattern / enum）行为不变，仍走原本的 schema-fail + `retryable: true` 分支，clamp 不降低 schema sensitivity

- Added an `<available-workers>` L1 menu to the main agent's system prompt: each of the four fixed worker roles gets a `<worker>` sub-block carrying `<role>` / `<description>` / `<example>`, plus a prescriptive preamble that **defaults to `delegate_task`** — any request that produces a non-trivial artifact ("write / build / create / generate / draft X") delegates even when the main agent has the native tools to do it directly, with only three carve-outs (short Q&A / iterative refinement / single tool call) where doing it yourself is acceptable. The preamble backs this up with concrete numbers (e.g. `Build a Web Studio` via `frontend_coder` ≈ 50-token handoff vs ~30+ native tool calls with every tool result polluting main-agent context), making the cost curve visible up front instead of relying on the schema description's passive cue. Rendered by `buildAvailableWorkersBlock` in `lib/agent/worker-roles.ts`, mirroring the existing `<skills>` block's XML wrapping and "scan index first, then decide" protocol; the four role keys are pinned by a `Record<WorkerRole, ...>` so adding/removing a role forces a description + example update at compile time. Ordering: base → skills → available-workers → user-instructions, all glued to the base prompt so the system cache prefix hits as long as the registry is unchanged
- Worker handoff schema auto-truncate before validation (Postel's Law): a new pure helper `autoTruncateHandoff(parsed, schema)` walks the schema's `properties` tree before `checkSchema` runs, clamping every string field with `maxLength` (for reviewer: `summary=200` / `checklist.items.evidence=200` / `checklist.items.item=64`; future roles that extend the schema get auto-covered too) to `slice(0, maxLength - 3) + '...'`. Caps are read from the schema literal — the runner does not hard-code any cap; schema-bounded string fields with no `maxLength` like `handoff_notes` are skipped untouched (a reviewer's 1000-char bug-report context stays legitimate). Malformed-schema paths still take the original `Invalid expected_schema` early-return; the new clamp only runs when schema is parseable. The `parsed` argument is immutable — the helper always returns a shallow-cloned object. Regression that prompted this (visible in `cebian-debug-20260909-211043.json`, attempt 10): reviewer emitted a 250-char `summary`, the previous hard-fail + single-pass self-healing retry both failed on the same `must not have more than 200 characters` error and burned 2 attempts; now the clamp truncates to 200 chars (197 + `...`) and the schema passes on the first attempt, `assembleHandoff` branch 6 success path runs. Shape errors (type / required / pattern / enum) still flow into the existing schema-fail + `retryable: true` branch, so the clamp does not lower schema sensitivity

- 新增 worker 实时输出微流框（Live Micro-Stream Box）：`delegate_task` 的 Delegation 卡片在 worker 运行期间实时显示其 thinking / 正文 / 工具调用活动——一个最多 4 行已完成 + 1 行进行中的 mono 小框，自动滚到最新，不再只有 spinner + 计时器的空白卡。worker 一开始吐字就把 Task 正文收起保持紧凑，成功 / 部分 / 失败 / 超时后立刻隐藏并回到最终摘要。Batch 派发（多个 worker 共用同一个外层 `delegate_task` toolCallId）只在卡片顶部渲染**一个**共享流框，而非每个 item 各来一遍。高频 delta（本地 OpenAI-compatible 代理约 50–100Hz）经同 kind token 合并 + 50ms trailing-edge 节流，渲染上限约 20 次/秒且与上游速度无关；内容一律压缩后才显示——工具行只呈现 `toolName:path`，不外泄原始 tool 参数（`fs_create_file` 的 args 可达 25–135 KB）、token 速率或 debug 指标
- New live micro-stream box on worker delegation cards: while a `delegate_task` worker runs, its `DelegationCard` now shows the worker's thinking / body text / tool-call activity in real time — a mono box of up to 4 finished lines + 1 in-progress line that auto-scrolls to the newest, replacing the previous empty spinner-and-timer card. The Task body collapses to keep the card compact once the worker starts emitting, and the box disappears the moment the worker succeeds / partially succeeds / fails / times out, returning to the final summary. Batch dispatch (multiple workers sharing one outer `delegate_task` toolCallId) renders a single shared stream box at the card top rather than one per item. High-rate deltas (local OpenAI-compatible proxies emit ~50–100 Hz) are coalesced per kind and throttled on a 50 ms trailing edge, capping rendering at ~20 updates/sec regardless of upstream speed; content is always compacted before display — tool lines show only `toolName:path`, and raw tool arguments (`fs_create_file` args can reach 25–135 KB), token rates, and debug metrics are never surfaced

- 超长 user 消息气泡现在自动折叠：自然高度超过 240px（约 9 行）的气泡只露出顶部裁切高度 + 底部渐隐，气泡右下方给一个「展开更多 / 收起」小按钮（含 `aria-expanded`）；短消息与编辑态完全不受影响。折叠判定用 ResizeObserver 量内层未裁切节点的自然高度，故字号 slider、sidepanel 宽度变化后自动重新判定；裁切用 `overflow-clip` 而非 `overflow-hidden`，避免拖选文字越过裁切线时浏览器程序性滚动裁切框。展开状态是 per-bubble 本地态，不持久化——重新打开会话时长消息回到折叠默认
- Over-long user message bubbles now collapse automatically: a bubble whose natural height exceeds 240px (≈9 lines) is clipped to that height with a bottom fade, and a small "Show more / Show less" button (with `aria-expanded`) appears under its right edge; short messages and the inline edit state are unaffected. The overflow decision measures the inner unclipped node's natural height via a ResizeObserver, so font-size slider or sidepanel width changes re-decide automatically; clipping uses `overflow-clip` rather than `overflow-hidden` so dragging a text selection past the cut line cannot programmatically scroll the clip box. The expanded state is per-bubble local state and is not persisted — reopening a session returns long messages to the collapsed default
- 新增 Canvas Live Artifacts：sidepanel chat 视图左侧新增可折叠的 Canvas pane（Header Eye/EyeOff 工具栏按钮切换，开关状态走 `useStorageItem` + `session:` 持久化到浏览器会话）。Layout 由 `react-resizable-panels` 分两栏（默认 50/50，minSize 20/25，拖拽实时调整），右半保持原 chat 流不变。Agent 通过新 LLM 工具 `canvas_open` 打开 VFS 文件预览，三层隔离：外层 iframe `sandbox="allow-scripts allow-forms allow-modals allow-popups"` 不带 `allow-same-origin` 拿到不透明 origin，内层走 manifest `sandbox.pages` 的 `vfs-preview.html` broker（监听 `vfs-preview-ready` 后 postMessage `vfs-preview-render`），再嵌 srcdoc iframe 跑用户 HTML，三层 opaque origin + 已 widen 的 `wxt.config.ts` sandbox CSP 让用户 HTML 无法访问 `chrome.*` 或扩展存储。BG 域通过 VFS `onChange` 跨 context 总线（`lib/persistence/vfs.ts:132-264`，已支持 SW wake-up）订阅写入事件——正被打开的文件被改就广播 `canvas_file_changed`，sidepanel iframe 自动热重载无需用户操作。`canvasChannel` 桥接 BG 与 sidepanel（跨 capability 注入，避免 `lib → entrypoints` 阻断），由 `useBackgroundAgent` 在 subscribe 时设 `activeSessionId` 过滤跨 session 缓存污染。管理面板「空状态」展示「请让 agent 调用 canvas_open 打开一个文件」。Firefox 不支持 manifest `sandbox.pages`，Canvas pane 在 Firefox 构建上降级为无脚本 srcdoc 静态渲染。新增 i18n 命名空间 `canvas.*`（en/zh_CN/zh_TW 三语 parity 通过 `pnpm check` 强制对齐），覆盖 Header 切换按钮 aria + tooltip、pane close 按钮、empty state 两行文案。Storage: `session:canvas-panel-open`（`exclude` 分类，不进备份——pane 状态是设备本地 UI 偏好，不是用户配置）
- New Canvas Live Artifacts: sidepanel chat view gains a collapsible Canvas pane on the left edge (Header Eye/EyeOff toolbar button; open/closed state persisted via `useStorageItem` to `session:` storage scoped to the browser session). Layout uses `react-resizable-panels` for the horizontal split (default 50/50, minSize 20/25, live-resize drag), leaving the right chat stream untouched. Agent opens VFS files for live preview via a new LLM tool `canvas_open`; three-layer isolation: outer iframe `sandbox="allow-scripts allow-forms allow-modals allow-popups"` (no `allow-same-origin` → opaque origin), inner `vfs-preview.html` from `manifest.sandbox.pages` brokers via postMessage (waits for `vfs-preview-ready` then dispatches `vfs-preview-render`), innermost srcdoc iframe runs the user's HTML. The three layers of opaque origin plus the widened `wxt.config.ts` sandbox CSP prevent user HTML from accessing `chrome.*` APIs or extension storage. BG subscribes to VFS writes via the existing cross-context `onChange` bus (`lib/persistence/vfs.ts:132-264`, already wakes the SW) — when an open file is modified, BG re-reads and broadcasts `canvas_file_changed` so the iframe hot-reloads with zero user action. `canvasChannel` bridges BG and sidepanel via the channel-set-port pattern (registers `openCanvas`/`setPort` to cross the lib→entrypoints capability boundary per AGENTS.md), and `useBackgroundAgent` calls `setActiveSession` on subscribe so the cache filters foreign-session events and never pollutes the currently-viewed pane. Empty state shows "Ask the agent to canvas_open a file." Firefox lacks manifest `sandbox.pages`, so the Canvas pane degrades to a scriptless srcdoc static render on Firefox builds. New i18n namespace `canvas.*` (parity across en/zh_CN/zh_TW enforced by `pnpm check` lint pass), covering the Header toggle aria+tooltip, pane close aria, and empty-state title/hint. Storage: `session:canvas-panel-open` (`exclude` class — pane toggle is device-local UI state, not user-facing config)
- 新增 Settings → Scheduled tasks 页面：用户可 CRUD 后台定时任务（cron / interval schedule + fetch 或 webcheck action + notify 策略）。BG 域每分钟一个 `scheduler-tick` alarm（chrome.alarms 30s floor 的实际下界）拉一次任务列表，对到点的走 `lib/scheduler/runner.ts` 的 `runTask`（30s per-task timeout、CORS/network 错误转 `RunResult.ok=false`），写回 `lastRunAt` + `lastResult`。新增 IPC 路由 `scheduler_list` / `scheduler_create` / `scheduler_update` / `scheduler_delete` / `scheduler_run_now`（BG 端 `entrypoints/background/scheduler/client-handlers.ts`，sidepanel 端 `hooks/useScheduledTasks.ts`），跨 context 推送用 `scheduler_result` ServerMessage + `lib/scheduler/sidepanel-channel.ts` 的 `publishResult`，UI 端 `useBackgroundAgent.handleMessage` 桥接 → `useSchedulerNotifications` 弹 Sonner toast（区分 `source='tick'` vs `source='manual'`）。验证全在 BG 端 `lib/scheduler/validate.ts`（URL scheme / cron 表达式 / interval minutes ≥ 1 / webcheck.expected 要求等），失败回 `error` ServerMessage，UI toast 显示；不重复校验。新增 i18n 命名空间 `settings.scheduler.*`（en/zh_CN/zh_TW 三语 parity 通过 `pnpm check` 强制对齐，约 35 个 key：title / columns / scheduleType / actionType / 字段 labels / placeholders / dialog 标题按钮 / runNow toast 文案 / permissionDenied 文案）。Storage `local:scheduledTasks` 已在 Phase A 注册（`settings` 分类，随备份走）
- New Settings → Scheduled tasks page: CRUD for user-configured BG automation jobs (cron / interval schedule + fetch or webcheck action + notify toggles). BG ticks every minute via a single `scheduler-tick` alarm (chrome.alarms 30-second floor), pulls the task list, runs each due task via `lib/scheduler/runner.ts#runTask` (30-second per-task timeout, CORS / network errors mapped to `RunResult.ok=false`), writes back `lastRunAt` + `lastResult`. Five IPC routes added — `scheduler_list` / `scheduler_create` / `scheduler_update` / `scheduler_delete` / `scheduler_run_now` (BG-side `entrypoints/background/scheduler/client-handlers.ts`, UI-side `hooks/useScheduledTasks.ts`). Cross-context result push goes via the new `scheduler_result` ServerMessage plus `lib/scheduler/sidepanel-channel.ts#publishResult`; the UI hook `useBackgroundAgent.handleMessage` bridges the wire to the local channel, and `useSchedulerNotifications` fires Sonner toasts (source tag distinguishes `'tick'` vs `'manual'`). Validation lives entirely in BG `lib/scheduler/validate.ts` (URL scheme / cron expression / interval minutes ≥ 1 / webcheck.expected requirement when condition is `'contains_text'`), failures flow back as `error` ServerMessage and surface in the UI as a toast — no double-validation. New i18n namespace `settings.scheduler.*` (parity across en/zh_CN/zh_TW enforced by `pnpm check` lint pass, ~35 keys: title / table columns / scheduleType / actionType / field labels / placeholders / dialog title & buttons / runNow toasts / permissionDenied body). Storage `local:scheduledTasks` was registered back in Phase A (`settings` class — flows with backup)
- 新增 LLM 工具 `scheduler_list` / `scheduler_create` / `scheduler_delete` / `scheduler_run_now`（不暴露 `scheduler_update`——防 LLM 误改 user schedule；Settings UI 是唯一 partial-edit 路径）。每个工具走 `chrome.runtime.sendMessage` → BG `schedulerClientHandlers` → BG 端校验 + 落 `scheduledTasks` storage。`scheduler_create` 回 `scheduler_create_result` ServerMessage（带 BG 端 `crypto.randomUUID()` 的新 id 与 task 镜像，LLM 据此调用 run_now/delete 时不会因 name-match 拿到旧 task）。验证、IPC 共享 helper（`sendAndReceive` + type predicates）抽到 `lib/scheduler/scheduler-ipc.ts` 让 `lib/scheduler/tool-scheduler.ts` 与 `hooks/useScheduledTasks.ts` 共用——避免「lib → hooks」反向依赖反过来被 copy-paste 绕过。新增 unit test `entrypoints/background/scheduler/manager.test.ts`（6 tests for `tick()` + `dispatchTask`，mock storage + broadcastAll + sendTaskNotification）与 `entrypoints/background/scheduler/client-handlers.test.ts`（13 tests for 5 IPC handlers + validation paths），都通过 mock 不需要起 real Chrome
- New LLM tools `scheduler_list` / `scheduler_create` / `scheduler_delete` / `scheduler_run_now` (no `scheduler_update` — LLM cannot edit user schedules to prevent drift; Settings UI is the only path for partial edits). Each tool uses `chrome.runtime.sendMessage` → BG `schedulerClientHandlers` → BG validates + writes `scheduledTasks` storage. `scheduler_create` replies with `scheduler_create_result` ServerMessage (carrying the BG-generated `crypto.randomUUID()` new id plus the written-back task mirror, so the LLM's `scheduler_run_now` / `scheduler_delete` calls don't risk matching the wrong same-name task by name). Validation, IPC shared helpers (`sendAndReceive` + type predicates) extracted to `lib/scheduler/scheduler-ipc.ts` so `lib/scheduler/tool-scheduler.ts` and `hooks/useScheduledTasks.ts` both consume it — avoiding the lib-→-hooks boundary being copy-paste-bypassed. New unit tests `entrypoints/background/scheduler/manager.test.ts` (6 tests for `tick()` + `dispatchTask`, mocks storage + `broadcastAll` + `sendTaskNotification`) and `entrypoints/background/scheduler/client-handlers.test.ts` (13 tests for the 5 IPC handlers + validation paths), both via mocks — no real Chrome required
- 新增 Multi-Channel Outbound Notification Gateway（Phase C）：让定时任务结果在浏览器关闭 / 用户离线时仍能送到外部通道。BG scheduler 任务跑完 → 除了桌面通知，再通过 `dispatchExternalNotifications` 走 3 种通道并发派发（`Promise.allSettled` + 各 adapter 5s `AbortSignal.timeout`，一通道失败不阻塞其它）。3 种通道：「ntfy」（POST `https://<host>/<topic>`，公开 server 无需 auth，self-hosted 可 `?user=&pass=` 加 Basic auth）；「telegram」（POST Bot API `sendMessage`，Markdown parse mode，400 失败降级 plain text 重发）；「webhook」（POST 结构化 JSON 到任意 URL，Discord / Slack / Gotify 兼容）。零依赖——只用 `fetch` + `URL` + `AbortSignal.timeout`，无 Node 专属 API。Storage：`local:notifyChannels`（settings class）+ `local:notifyChannelSecrets`（credentials class），backup registry 用 `splitSecret` 把 token / URL 隔离到 `credentials.json`，不污染 `config.json`。新 IPC `scheduler_test_channel` + reply `scheduler_channel_test_result` 让 Settings UI 的「Send test」按钮直接拿到 success / latencyMs / error 反馈。BG `entrypoints/background/scheduler/notify-external.ts` 读 channel + secret，按 `enabled + notifyOnSuccess + notifyOnFailure` 过滤；`scheduler-test-channel` 单 channel 走 `dispatchSingleChannelTest`，回带 latencyMs 的 wire 让 UI 实时显示。新增 UI `Settings → Notification channels`（table + add/edit dialog + inline enabled toggle + per-row test button 带 spinner 与 latency badge）。Settings UI 新增 nav entry `notifications`（`BellRing` 图标）。新增 i18n `settings.notifications.*` 命名空间（约 30 个 key：title / description / columns / channel kinds / form labels + placeholders / dialog 标题 / buttons / delete confirm / test result success / failure / permission hints）三语 parity 强制对齐。Storage safety：channel secret 用 `splitSecret` pattern 跟 `mcpServers` 一致——restoreSecret + fillMissing 都是 required，restore 时 merge by id 不覆盖本地已有 secret。fire-and-forget：BG tick 与 `dispatchTask` 都不 `await` 外部通知，BG manager 与外部网络解耦。N2 unit test `entrypoints/background/scheduler/notify-external.test.ts`（11 tests with mocked storage + fetch）覆盖 happy / priority / enabled / notifySuccess / ntfy-public-default / telegram-no-token / webhook-no-url / error isolation / storage throws。Total Phase C：3 新 files（`validate.ts` / `notify-external.ts` / `NotificationsSection.tsx`）+ 2 新 tests（`manager.test.ts` 增 11 cases、`client-handlers.test.ts` 增 1 case），无新 dep
- New Multi-Channel Outbound Notification Gateway (Phase C): scheduler task results now fire to external channels even when the browser is closed / user is offline. After BG runs a task, in addition to the existing desktop notification, `dispatchExternalNotifications` fans out to 3 channels in parallel via `Promise.allSettled` + per-adapter 5s `AbortSignal.timeout` (one channel's failure doesn't block the others). Three channels: **ntfy** (POST `https://<host>/<topic>`; public server needs no auth, self-hosted supports `?user=&pass=` for Basic auth); **telegram** (POST Bot API `sendMessage` with Markdown parse mode, falls back to plain text on 400); **webhook** (POST structured JSON to any URL, Discord / Slack / Gotify compatible). Zero new dependencies — only `fetch` + `URL` + `AbortSignal.timeout`, no Node-only APIs. Storage: `local:notifyChannels` (settings class) + `local:notifyChannelSecrets` (credentials class); backup registry uses the `splitSecret` pattern to keep tokens / URLs out of `config.json`. New IPC `scheduler_test_channel` + reply `scheduler_channel_test_result` so the Settings UI's "Send test" button gets success / latencyMs / error feedback in real time. BG `entrypoints/background/scheduler/notify-external.ts` reads channels + secrets and filters by `enabled + notifyOnSuccess + notifyOnFailure`; `scheduler-test-channel` for a single channel uses `dispatchSingleChannelTest` and returns latencyMs on the wire so the UI can show it live. New Settings UI "Notification channels" section (table + add / edit dialog + inline enabled toggle + per-row test button with spinner + latency badge). New `notifications` nav entry with `BellRing` icon. New i18n namespace `settings.notifications.*` (~30 keys: title / description / columns / channel kinds / form labels + placeholders / dialog titles / buttons / delete confirm / test result success + failure / permission hints), 3-locale parity enforced. Storage safety: channel secrets use the `splitSecret` pattern mirroring `mcpServers` — both `restoreSecret` and `fillMissing` are required, and restore merges by id without overwriting existing local secrets. Fire-and-forget: BG tick + `dispatchTask` never `await` external notifications, decoupling the BG manager from external network latency. N2 unit test `entrypoints/background/scheduler/notify-external.test.ts` (11 tests with mocked storage + fetch) covers happy / priority / enabled / notifySuccess / ntfy-public-default / telegram-no-token / webhook-no-url / error isolation / storage-throws. Total Phase C: 3 new files (`validate.ts` / `notify-external.ts` / `NotificationsSection.tsx`) + 2 new tests (manager.test.ts adds 11 cases, client-handlers.test.ts adds 1 case), no new deps
- 新增 Telegram Realtime Two-Way Gateway（Phase D）：把 Telegram bot 从单向通知升级为双向即时互动网关——用户从手机 Telegram 发消息，Cebian 在 ~50ms 内收到并 dispatch 给 agent，agent 回复即时推回 Telegram chat。架构採用 botgram-inspired「client-leased webhook + WebSocket bridge」：单文件 Node.js relay（`gateway/server.js`，零依赖，部署於 Koyeb）作為 public HTTPS webhook relay + WebSocket duplex 橋接；extension 端 `lib/telegram-gateway/worker-client.ts` 用瀏覽器原生 `WebSocket` + 指数退避 1s→30s 重连 + outbound 消息队列。安全模型：Gateway 端 `X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token` 常数时间比对 + `ALLOWED_CHAT_IDS` 白名单；extension 端 `validateChatId` 防御性二次检查（fail-open 默认，空白名单允许全部）。BG `bootstrapTelegramGateway`（`lib/telegram-gateway/bootstrap.ts`）创建 worker-client + `acquireKeepAlive` 保活 + wire 到 `telegramGatewayChannel` pub/sub。Inbound 由 BG 侧 `telegram-gateway/manager.ts` 路由：每个 chat_id（DM 或 group）固定映射到专用 session（从 chat_id 派生的确定性 UUID，标题「Telegram · <user>」），后台独立跑 agent、永不触碰用户当前打开的会话（History 列表可见、随时点开查看）；同一 session 的 burst 消息按到达顺序串行排队不丢；`interactiveMode` OFF 时完全忽略 inbound；agent 跑完后由 BG 自动把最后一条 assistant 文本（截断 4096）经 WS 送回该 chat。新增 Settings → Telegram Gateway 页面（Worker URL / Bot Token / Webhook Secret / WS Auth Token / Allowed Chat IDs CSV / Interactive Mode toggle / Save）。Header 新增 Telegram 连接状态 badge（绿=connected / 琥珀=connecting+reconnecting / 灰=disconnected，仅在非 disconnected 时显示）。Storage：`local:telegramGatewayConfig`（settings class）+ `local:telegramGatewaySecrets`（credentials class with splitSecret）。新 IPC `telegram_gateway_config_get/set` + `telegram_gateway_config_get_result`。新 i18n `settings.telegramGateway.*`（~11 keys）+ `settings.nav.telegramGateway`。Total Phase D：4 新 files（`server.js` / `worker-client.ts` / `channel.ts` / `bootstrap.ts` / `validate.ts` / `TelegramGatewaySection.tsx` / `useTelegramGatewaySettings.ts` / `useTelegramGatewayStatus.ts` / `client-handlers.ts`）+ 4 新 test files（worker-client.test.ts 9 / channel.test.ts 14 / validate.test.ts 11 / bootstrap.test.ts 7），无新 dep
- Telegram Gateway 對話 UX 升級：bot 收到訊息後立即在用戶訊息上貼 `👀` reaction（is_big 原生動畫）並發送 typing 指示（每 4s 續發）——agent 執行期間不發任何佔位訊息、不做流式中間渲染，聊天窗完全靜止；工具執行期間另有一行臨時狀態 `🔧 <工具名>...`：首個工具啟動時靜默發出，後續切換工具以 ~2.75s 節流就地更新，正式回覆落位前自動刪除——多步 ReAct 過程每步有名有姓、聊天窗不留殘渣；agent 完成後 reaction 換成 `👌`（失敗換 `❌`），回覆一次性落位：按段落 / 句子邊界切塊（首塊 = 完整第一段，語義不截斷；其餘段落分組 ≤2000 字元），首塊以 `reply_to` 回鏈用戶訊息（Markdown 失敗退 plain，reply 目標缺失再退無 reply），其餘塊靜默補發並關閉 link preview。agent 無法啟動（無模型 / session 建立失敗 / prompt 拋錯）時 reaction 換成 `❌` 並復位全部 UX 定時器；Telegram session 恆以 Fast 模式執行（不啟用 worker team / `delegate_task`，全局開關不滲透），頁頭也隱藏 Worker Team chip 與思考檔選擇器。Telegram session 加入 sliding window：每 15 個 turn 自動觸發 compaction 把舊語境摘要化，LLM context 保持短小、回應快速。Gateway wire protocol 新增 `sendChatAction` / `editMessage` / `setMessageReaction` / `deleteMessage` 四種 action（reply 統一為 `gateway_result`，sendMessage 支援 `parse_mode` / `reply_to_message_id` / `disable_notification` / `disable_link_preview`）；'message is not modified' 由 server-side 吞掉不視為錯誤
- Telegram Gateway chat UX upgrade: upon receiving a Telegram message the bot immediately sets a `👀` reaction on the user's message (native big animation) and sends a typing indicator (refreshed every 4s) — during the agent run no placeholder message is sent and streamed text is never rendered mid-run, so the chat window stays perfectly still; tool execution gets its own transient status line `🔧 <tool label>...` — sent silently when the first tool starts, updated in place with ~2.75s throttling as tools switch, auto-deleted right before the final reply lands — so every step of a multi-tool ReAct loop is named while the chat stays clean; when the agent finishes the reaction becomes `👌` (`❌` on failure) and the reply lands in one pass: it is cut into blocks at paragraph / sentence boundaries (block 1 = the complete first paragraph, never truncated mid-thought; remaining paragraphs grouped ≤2000 chars), block 1 is sent as a reply to the user's message (Markdown falling back to plain text, then to no-reply if the reply target is gone), and remaining blocks are sent silently with link previews disabled. When the agent cannot start (no model / session creation failure / prompt error), the reaction becomes `❌` and all UX timers are released; Telegram sessions always run in Fast mode (worker team / `delegate_task` never enabled — the global toggle does not leak through) and the Worker Team chip plus the thinking-level selector are hidden there. The Telegram session gains a sliding window: every 15 turns triggers a compaction that summarizes old context, keeping the LLM context short and responses fast. The gateway wire protocol adds `sendChatAction`, `editMessage`, `setMessageReaction` and `deleteMessage` actions (replies unified as `gateway_result`; sendMessage supports `parse_mode` / `reply_to_message_id` / `disable_notification` / `disable_link_preview`); 'message is not modified' is swallowed server-side and not treated as an error
- New Telegram Realtime Two-Way Gateway (Phase D): upgrades the Telegram bot from one-way notification to a bidirectional realtime interactive gateway — user sends a message from phone via Telegram, Cebian receives it within ~50ms and dispatches to the agent, agent reply is pushed back to the Telegram chat in realtime. Architecture uses the botgram-inspired "client-leased webhook + WebSocket bridge" pattern: a single-file Node.js relay (`gateway/server.js`, zero deps, deployed on Koyeb) serves as public HTTPS webhook relay + WebSocket duplex bridge; the extension side `lib/telegram-gateway/worker-client.ts` uses browser-native `WebSocket` + exponential backoff 1s→30s reconnect + outbound message queue. Security model: Gateway-side `X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token` constant-time compare + `ALLOWED_CHAT_IDS` whitelist; extension-side `validateChatId` defensive second check (fail-open default, empty whitelist allows all). BG `bootstrapTelegramGateway` (`lib/telegram-gateway/bootstrap.ts`) creates the worker-client + `acquireKeepAlive` keepalive + wires into `telegramGatewayChannel` pub/sub. Inbound is routed by the BG-side `telegram-gateway/manager.ts`: each chat_id (DM or group) maps to a fixed dedicated session (a deterministic UUID derived from the chat id, titled `Telegram · <user>`), runs the agent in the background without ever touching the currently open conversation (visible in History, openable anytime); burst messages on the same session are queued sequentially in arrival order so none are lost; with `interactiveMode` OFF, inbound is ignored entirely; after the agent finishes, the background automatically sends the last assistant text (truncated at 4096) back to that chat over WS. New Settings → Telegram Gateway page (Worker URL / Bot Token / Webhook Secret / WS Auth Token / Allowed Chat IDs CSV / Interactive Mode toggle / Save). Header gains a Telegram connection status badge (green=connected / amber=connecting+reconnecting / gray=disconnected, only shown when not disconnected). Storage: `local:telegramGatewayConfig` (settings class) + `local:telegramGatewaySecrets` (credentials class with splitSecret). New IPC `telegram_gateway_config_get/set` + `telegram_gateway_config_get_result`. New i18n `settings.telegramGateway.*` (~11 keys) + `settings.nav.telegramGateway`. Total Phase D: 4+ new files (`server.js` / `worker-client.ts` / `channel.ts` / `bootstrap.ts` / `validate.ts` / `TelegramGatewaySection.tsx` / `useTelegramGatewaySettings.ts` / `useTelegramGatewayStatus.ts` / `client-handlers.ts`) + 4 new test files (worker-client.test.ts 9 / channel.test.ts 14 / validate.test.ts 11 / bootstrap.test.ts 7), no new deps
- 新增 Canvas Pick Element（拾取元素）：Canvas pane header 新增 `MousePointerClick` 按钮（无文件打开时禁用），开启后在预览里 hover 即显示蓝色高亮框 + tooltip（`tag#id.class`；overlay `pointer-events:none` 不碰作者样式），点击以 capture 阶段拦截默认行为与页面脚本，把元素序列化（tag/id/class + nth-of-type 祖先链 + 截断 500 字符的 outerHTML 片段）经 sandbox 代理页转发回 sidepanel；host 侧 `normalizePick` 清洗不可信 payload（tag 正则校验 + snippet 500 / id 120 / className 200 / 祖先链 6 段截断）后经 `canvasPickChannel` pub/sub 送达 ChatInput，挂成合成 file 附件——chip 名即 `tag#id.html`，正文含 canvas 源文件路径 + CSS selector + outerHTML 片段，走 `<attached-file>` envelope 既有管线，agent 直接凭文本在 VFS 文件里定位修改。selector 算法只在 host 侧 `buildCssSelector` 一处实现（id 锚点 / 最多 2 个 class / nth-of-type 消歧 / 5 段上限）；同一文件同一元素重复拾取只弹「已添加」提示不重复挂（去重键与附件正文同源 `pickDedupeKey`）。拾取模式跨热更新保持（sandbox 页记住开关、inner iframe load 时重放），pick 成功 / Escape 取消后自动关闭并回传 toggle-off，杜绝「按钮显示已关而热更新悄悄重新激活」的状态漂移；Escape 在 sidepanel 与预览 iframe 内两侧都可取消。Firefox 构建上拾取按钮不渲染（Canvas 降级为静态渲染）。新 i18n keys `canvas.pane.inspectOn` / `canvas.pane.inspectOff`（en/zh_CN/zh_TW 三语 parity）
- New Canvas Pick Element: the Canvas pane header gains a `MousePointerClick` toggle (disabled while no file is open). When on, hovering the preview draws a blue highlight box + tooltip (`tag#id.class`; the overlay is `pointer-events:none` and never touches author styles); a capture-phase click intercepts the default action and page scripts, serializes the element (tag/id/class + nth-of-type ancestor chain + outerHTML snippet truncated to 500 chars) and relays it through the sandbox proxy page back to the sidepanel. The host-side `normalizePick` sanitizes the untrusted payload (tag-name regex; snippet 500 / id 120 / className 200 / ancestor chain 6 caps) and publishes via the `canvasPickChannel` pub/sub to ChatInput, which attaches it as a synthesized file attachment — the chip is named `tag#id.html` and the body carries the canvas source path + CSS selector + outerHTML snippet, riding the existing `<attached-file>` envelope so the agent can locate and edit the markup in the VFS file by text. The selector algorithm lives in exactly one place, host-side `buildCssSelector` (id anchor / ≤2 classes / nth-of-type disambiguation / 5-segment cap). Re-picking the same element in the same file toasts "already added" instead of duplicating the chip (dedupe key shares one source with the attachment body via `pickDedupeKey`). Pick mode survives hot reload (the sandbox page remembers the toggle and replays it on inner-iframe load); after a pick or Escape the host sends a toggle-off back down, eliminating the "button shows off while hot-reload silently re-arms" state drift; Escape works both in the sidepanel and inside the preview iframe. On Firefox the pick button is not rendered (the Canvas degrades to static rendering). New i18n keys `canvas.pane.inspectOn` / `canvas.pane.inspectOff` (parity across en/zh_CN/zh_TW)
- VFS 标签页文件工具栏新增「Open in Canvas」按钮：仅 `.html`/`.htm` 文件出现（`PanelLeft` 图标，紧跟预览/源码切换），点击后文件直接在 sidepanel 的 Canvas pane 打开、Canvas 面板自动置为展开，并弹「已在 Canvas 中打开」toast。session 从 `/workspaces/<id>/…` 路径解析；会话工作区之外的 HTML 文件按钮置灰并在 tooltip 说明原因（canvas 状态是 per-session 的，无主文件打开也无人可见）。VFS 页通过懒连接的 CLIENT_PORT 端口发 `canvas_open`，复用既有 BG handler，零新增后台代码
- The VFS tab's file toolbar gains an "Open in Canvas" button: shown only for `.html`/`.htm` files (`PanelLeft` icon, right after the preview/source toggle). Clicking opens the file directly in the sidepanel's Canvas pane, flips the Canvas panel to open, and toasts "Opened in Canvas". The session is resolved from the `/workspaces/<id>/…` path; HTML files outside any session workspace render the button disabled with an explanatory tooltip (canvas state is per-session — an unowned file would have no viewer). The VFS page sends `canvas_open` over a lazily-connected CLIENT_PORT, reusing the existing BG handler with zero new background code
- 新增 Telegram Bot `/tabs` 命令：在白名单 chat 发送 `/tabs`，bot 返回 inline keyboard 列出最多 10 个打开的 Chrome 标签页（title 截断 48 chars + 溢出计数）；点击某个 tab → extension 激活该 tab（后台 tab 先 switch + 250ms 渲染等待，截图后 300ms 自动还原原 active tab，active 查询按目标 tab 的 `windowId` scope）→ `chrome.tabs.captureVisibleTab` JPEG q60 → base64 经 WS 回 gateway → 原生 FormData/Blob multipart `sendPhoto` 发回 chat → editMessage 清除 keyboard 防止重复点击。`answerCallbackQuery` 由 gateway 在 webhook 收到 callback_query 时**唯一一次**调用（'⏳ Đang chụp tab…' toast）—— Telegram 拒绝对同一 callback_query_id 的第二次 answer，extension 端零 answer 调用，全部反馈走 editMessageText。错误路径全部收敛为 editMessage 友好文案：Chrome 关闭（gateway 检测 0 client）、extension 5s 无响应（gateway watchdog per `chat_id:message_id`，`sendPhoto`/`editMessage` 到达即取消）、window minimized/锁定（capture throw 映射文案）、tab 已关闭。Wire 扩展：inbound `telegram_callback` kind + outbound `sendPhoto` action + `sendMessage`/`editMessage` 增 `reply_markup`（inline keyboard）。`/tabs` bypass interactiveMode（显式命令非 LLM 路由，可一行翻回）。新 unit tests `tabs-command.test.ts` 12 cases（buildTabsKeyboard / parseTabCallbackData / 拦截 + 回归）。Total：3 files 修改（`gateway/server.js`、`lib/telegram-gateway/{types,worker-client}.ts`、`manager.ts`）+ 1 test file 新，无新 dep、manifest 不变
- New Telegram Bot `/tabs` command: send `/tabs` in a whitelisted chat and the bot replies with an inline keyboard listing up to 10 open Chrome tabs (title clipped at 48 chars + overflow count). Tapping a tab → the extension activates it (background tabs switch first + 250ms render wait; the previous active tab is auto-restored 300ms after capture, with the active-tab query scoped to the target tab's `windowId`) → `chrome.tabs.captureVisibleTab` JPEG q60 → base64 over WS back to the gateway → native FormData/Blob multipart `sendPhoto` → editMessage clears the keyboard to prevent stale taps. `answerCallbackQuery` is called **exactly once** by the gateway when the webhook receives the callback_query ('⏳ Đang chụp tab…' toast) — Telegram rejects a second answer for the same callback_query_id, so the extension makes zero answer calls and all feedback flows through editMessageText. Every error path converges to a friendly editMessage: Chrome closed (gateway sees 0 clients), extension silent for 5s (gateway watchdog per `chat_id:message_id`, cancelled as soon as `sendPhoto`/`editMessage` arrives), window minimized/locked (capture throw mapped to a friendly message), tab already closed. Wire protocol extensions: inbound `telegram_callback` kind + outbound `sendPhoto` action + `reply_markup` on `sendMessage`/`editMessage` (inline keyboard). `/tabs` bypasses interactiveMode (explicit command, not LLM routing — one line to flip). New unit tests `tabs-command.test.ts` 12 cases (buildTabsKeyboard / parseTabCallbackData / interception + regression). Total: 3 files modified (`gateway/server.js`, `lib/telegram-gateway/{types,worker-client}.ts`, `manager.ts`) + 1 new test file, no new deps, manifest unchanged

- 备份支持自选 VFS 路径（partial 模式新分类 `vfsCustom`）：创建备份弹窗在「记忆」下新增「VFS 文件与文件夹（自选）」，勾选后打开树形路径选择器（懒加载 readdir / stat 逐层展开，目录优先排序；读取失败与空目录区分显示），可自由勾选任意 VFS 目录（含整个子树）或单个文件；未选任何路径时确认按钮禁用。选择以绝对路径记入 manifest（`vfs.custom.roots` + fileCount），collect 按前缀采集——单个文件 root 由 stat 分流直接读取（此前 walkFiles 遇文件根会 ENOTDIR、`clearDirContents` 遇文件根会中断整个 replace 恢复，本次一并修复）；恢复预览展示 vfsCustom 分类、文件数与所选路径清单，replace 模式的强确认额外点名「以下自选 VFS 路径会先被清空」（自选路径可能罩住未勾选分类的子树，必须让用户看到具体范围再确认）。manifest 字段全部 optional、format version 不变，旧版备份照常恢复；两端路径经 `sanitizeVfsCustomRoots` 统一净化（normalizePath 解析 `~` / `..` / 冗余斜杠、丢弃 VFS 根 `/`、按规范化形态去重），构造的备份包无法注入任意清空前缀。新 i18n keys：`settings.backup.create.{catVfsCustom,catVfsCustomHint,vfsPick,vfsSelected,vfsCustomEmpty}`、`settings.backup.restore.{catVfsCustom,confirmReplaceVfsCustom}`、`settings.backup.picker.{title,hint,selectedCount,empty,loadFailed}`（en/zh_CN/zh_TW 三语对齐）

- Custom VFS path backup (new `vfsCustom` category, partial mode). The create-backup dialog gains "VFS files & folders (custom)" below Memory: tick it to open a tree path picker (lazy readdir / stat expansion, folders first; load failures are distinguished from empty folders) and freely check any VFS folders (whole subtrees) or single files — confirm stays disabled until at least one path is picked. The selection is recorded as absolute paths in the manifest (`vfs.custom.roots` + fileCount); collect gathers by root prefix, with a single-file root stat-branched and read directly (previously `walkFiles` threw ENOTDIR on a file root and `clearDirContents` would abort the entire replace restore on one — both fixed here). The restore preview lists the vfsCustom category, its file count and the picked paths, and the replace-mode strong confirmation now appends "The following custom VFS paths will be wiped first: …" — custom roots can cover subtrees of categories the user did not select, so the exact scope must be visible before confirming. All new manifest fields are optional and the format version is unchanged, so old backups restore as before; roots on both sides go through `sanitizeVfsCustomRoots` (normalizePath resolves `~` / `..` / stray slashes, drops the VFS root `/`, dedupes by normalized form), so a crafted backup cannot inject arbitrary clear prefixes. New i18n keys: `settings.backup.create.{catVfsCustom,catVfsCustomHint,vfsPick,vfsSelected,vfsCustomEmpty}`, `settings.backup.restore.{catVfsCustom,confirmReplaceVfsCustom}`, `settings.backup.picker.{title,hint,selectedCount,empty,loadFailed}` (parity across en/zh_CN/zh_TW)

- 联网搜索支持上下文内联图片：`web_search` 的 extract 契约新增可选 `images` 字段（`{ url, alt }`），5 个内置引擎脚本开始收集结果缩略图；进入上下文前经 `lib/search/images.ts` 统一过滤——只收绝对 http(s) 直链（位图扩展名或引擎缩略图域，`.svg` 刻意排除），拒绝跟踪像素 / 广告端点与 URL 尺寸提示 < 100px 的低分辨率图，DuckDuckGo 图片代理解包出真实 URL，按 url 去重、单条 result 上限 3 张、单次调用上限 12 张（预算耗尽按行截断）。工具输出在每条结果下追加 `images:` 行（URL + alt），模型据此原样输出 Markdown 图片。聊天渲染层把 web（非 VFS）内联图改为紧凑缩略图（约 1/3 面板宽 + `object-cover` + 边框），`loading=lazy` + `decoding=async`（离屏不拉取）、`referrerPolicy=no-referrer`（图源拿不到来源页）、加载失败降级为「图片不可用」chip（不渲染破图）；点击仍进 lightbox 看原图，多图段自动排成 flex 横排。系统提示新增「Inline Images」小节：只在回答本质上需要视觉信息时插图（旅行 / 美食 / 商品 / 人物 / 艺术 / "长什么样"），代码 / 逻辑 / 定义 / 抽象概念一律不插；**只允许使用工具输出里逐字出现的图片 URL**（与 Critical Rule 3 对目标 URL 的要求同源），禁止凭记忆构造或猜测。VFS 内联图（workspace / skill 资源）渲染不变

- Contextual inline images for web search: the `web_search` extract contract gains an optional `images` field (`{ url, alt }`) and all 5 built-in engine scripts now collect result thumbnails; everything passes a single filter in `lib/search/images.ts` before entering the context — absolute http(s) direct links only (bitmap extension or engine thumbnail host; `.svg` deliberately excluded), tracking pixels / ad endpoints and low-resolution size hints (< 100px) rejected, DuckDuckGo image proxies unwrapped to the real URL, deduped by url with a 3-per-result / 12-per-call cap (partial truncation when the budget runs out mid-line). The tool output appends an `images:` line per result (URL + alt) that the model inlines verbatim as Markdown. The chat renderer now shows web (non-VFS) inline images as compact thumbnails (~1/3 panel width + `object-cover` + border) with `loading=lazy` + `decoding=async` (offscreen images never fetched), `referrerPolicy=no-referrer` (the image host sees no referring page), and an "image unavailable" fallback chip instead of a broken image; clicking still opens the lightbox at full size and image-only paragraphs flow as a flex row. The system prompt gains an "Inline Images" section: include images only when the answer is inherently visual (travel / food / products / people / art / "what does X look like"), never for code / logic / definitions / abstract topics, and **only image URLs that appear verbatim in tool outputs** (mirroring Critical Rule 3 for destination URLs) — never recalled or constructed from memory. VFS inline images (workspace / skill assets) render unchanged

- Telegram 网关回复支持原生图片发送：AI 回复里的 markdown 内联图片（web http(s) 直链）不再以纯文本链接落地——finalize 解析 `![alt](url)` 并剥离出干净文本后按图数路由：单图走 `sendPhoto`（caption = 首个文本块，≤1024 字符硬限，reply 回链用户消息，Markdown 解析）；多图走 `sendMediaGroup` 原生相册（caption 只挂首图，单组上限 10 张）。发送被拒（Telegram 400 / URL 不可达）或回执超时 8s（旧 relay 静默丢弃新 action kind 的兜底）→ 降级为纯文本块 + 尾随 `🖼 <url>` 链接块，内容与链接双保底、零丢消息；URL 解析支持一层平衡括号（Wikipedia 式），同一 URL 去重、VFS 内联图（`#/...`）不参与路由。wire 扩展：`sendPhoto` 新增 `image_url`（与 `image_base64` 二选一）+ caption `parse_mode` + `reply_to_message_id`，新增 `sendMediaGroup` action（`reply_to_message_id` → `reply_parameters` Bot API 7+；`parse_mode` 由 relay 译到带 caption 的 InputMedia 上）。⚠️ 需重新部署 relay（`gateway/server.js`）后单图 / 相册路径才生效；未部署时自动走文本降级，行为等同旧版。VFS 图片（workspace / skill 资源）暂不参与原生发送（后续可经 base64 路径补足）

- Native photo dispatch for Telegram gateway replies: markdown inline images (web http(s) links) in AI replies no longer land as plain-text links — finalizeTurn parses `![alt](url)`, strips the copy clean, and routes by image count: single image via `sendPhoto` (caption = first text block capped at the 1024-char Telegram hard limit, reply-anchored to the user's message, Markdown parsed); multiple images via a native `sendMediaGroup` album (caption on the first item only, 10 photos per group cap). On rejection (Telegram 400 / unreachable URL) or an 8s receipt timeout (the graceful path for old relays that silently drop unknown action kinds), delivery degrades to clean text blocks plus a trailing `🖼 <url>` links block — content and links both survive, zero message loss. URL parsing supports one level of balanced parentheses (Wikipedia-style), same-URL duplicates are deduped, and VFS inline images (`#/...`) stay on the text path. Wire extensions: `sendPhoto` gains `image_url` (mutually exclusive with `image_base64`), caption `parse_mode` and `reply_to_message_id`; new `sendMediaGroup` action (`reply_to_message_id` → `reply_parameters`, Bot API 7+; `parse_mode` translated by the relay onto the caption-bearing InputMedia). ⚠️ Re-deploying the relay (`gateway/server.js`) is required for the photo / album paths; until then replies automatically degrade to text, matching the old behavior. VFS images (workspace / skill assets) are not natively dispatched yet (a future base64-path candidate)

- ChatInput toolbar 新增 Skills 切换芯片（`local:chatSkillAuto`）：Auto = run_skill 自动放行权限询问且不写永久授权；Manual = 现状（弹 permissionRequest 卡片等用户选 Allow Once / Always）。镜像 PersonaChip / WorkerTeamChip 的位置与颜色规范，图标 ShieldQuestion / ShieldCheck。BG 侧缓存 + storage watch，热路径零 disk I/O。Toggle OFF → 立即恢复询问，零残留授权。新 i18n keys `chat.composer.skillAutoToggle.{auto,manual,toggleHint}`（en/zh_CN/zh_TW）

- Skills auto-approve toggle in the ChatInput toolbar (`local:chatSkillAuto`): Auto = run_skill auto-approves the permission request without persisting a grant; Manual = current behavior (permissionRequest card with Allow Once / Always buttons). Placed and styled to mirror PersonaChip / WorkerTeamChip; ShieldQuestion / ShieldCheck icons. BG side caches the value + subscribes via storage.watch so the hot path stays off disk I/O. Toggle OFF resumes asking immediately with no leftover permanent grants. New i18n keys `chat.composer.skillAutoToggle.{auto,manual,toggleHint}` (en/zh_CN/zh_TW)

- ChatInput textarea 新增 `@` 触发 worker-role mention：输入 `@<prefix>` 弹紧凑 popover，列 4 个 worker role（content_writer / frontend_coder / reviewer / researcher）按 prefix 过滤，↑↓/Enter/Esc 键盘导航（不抢 textarea 光标、不触发 form submit）。选中后在原 token 位置插入 ` @<role> `，对应 mention chip 加入消息。Send 时 chip → 完整 ROUTE DIRECTIVE 块 prepend 到 message（与 slash / quote 同一管线，bubble parser 逐块剥离）：header `[DIRECTIVE — ATTACHED ROUTE: "<role>" (id=<id>)]` + instruction body 明确命令主代理**必须** `delegate_task({ role })` 派发该请求（传 `---` 之后的用户原话、剥掉 @token）、**禁止**自答（覆盖 preamble 的 carve-out）、`delegate_task` 不可用时明说 Worker Team 未启用——多名 role 各自成块。`@<role>` token 保留在 user text 中（选项 B：UI 可见 + directive 并存）；worker-role chip 不进 mention 解析批次（resolver 对它恒 null），不会误弹「Couldn't read mention」警告。WorkerTeam chip OFF 时 popover 关闭——入口 gate。`detectAtToken` 严格 boundary 检查：仅 `@` 紧跟行首 / 空白后触发，防止 `user@host` 这类 email 误激活。新 component `WorkerRoleMentionPopover.tsx`；新增 i18n keys `chat.composer.workerRoleMention.{popoverTitle,disabledHint,chip.{content_writer,frontend_coder,reviewer,researcher},noMatches,nav,select,closeHint,close}`（en/zh_CN/zh_TW）

- ChatInput textarea `@` triggers a worker-role mention: typing `@<prefix>` opens a compact popover listing the 4 worker roles (content_writer / frontend_coder / reviewer / researcher), filtered by prefix. ↑↓ / Enter / Esc keyboard navigation (does not steal textarea focus, does not submit the form). On select, the `@<token>` is replaced by ` @<role> ` in place, and a corresponding mention chip is added. At send time, the chip is prepended as a COMPLETE ROUTE directive block (same pipeline as slash / quote — the bubble parser strips blocks one by one): header `[DIRECTIVE — ATTACHED ROUTE: "<role>" (id=<id>)]` plus an instruction body that explicitly commands the main agent to dispatch the request via `delegate_task({ role })` (passing the user's words below the `---` separator, @token removed), forbids answering directly (overriding the preamble carve-outs), and requires reporting Worker Team as disabled when `delegate_task` is unavailable — multiple roles render as separate self-contained blocks. The `@<role>` token is kept in the user text (option B: visible + directive coexisting); worker-role chips are excluded from the mention-resolution batch (the resolver always returns null for them), so no spurious "Couldn't read mention" toast. When the WorkerTeam chip is OFF, the popover stays closed (entry gate). `detectAtToken` enforces strict boundary: only triggers when `@` immediately follows start-of-input or whitespace, preventing false positives like `user@host`. New component `WorkerRoleMentionPopover.tsx`. New i18n keys `chat.composer.workerRoleMention.{popoverTitle,disabledHint,chip.{content_writer,frontend_coder,reviewer,researcher},noMatches,nav,select,closeHint,close}` (en/zh_CN/zh_TW)

- Worker Team 的 researcher 角色接入 `web_search`：toolWhitelist 在 fs_read_file / fs_list / fs_search / rag_inspect 之外新增 web_search（工厂按用户当前搜索引擎配置构造，与主会话同一构造路径，runner dispatch 时并入 tool pool），role system prompt 同步教其用 `web_search({ query })` 抓新闻 / 价格 / 时效性事实并引用来源 URL——`@researcher` 现在能回答「今天金价」这类必须联网的问题。其余 3 个 role 工具集不变；researcher 仍 read-only（无 fs_write）。runner 测试的 UNIVERSE fixture 与 researcher 过滤 pin 同步更新
