- Worker Team's `researcher` role gains `web_search`: the toolWhitelist adds web_search alongside fs_read_file / fs_list / fs_search / rag_inspect (factory-built from the user's current search-engine config, same construction path as the main session, merged into the tool pool at runner dispatch), and the role system prompt now teaches `web_search({ query })` for news / prices / time-sensitive facts with source-URL citation — `@researcher` can now answer questions that require live web data such as today's gold price. The other 3 roles' toolsets are unchanged; researcher stays read-only (no fs_write). The runner test's UNIVERSE fixture and the researcher filter pin are updated accordingly

- 区域截图升级为 Copilot 式标注编辑器：拖拽裁剪完成后图片不再直接进 chat，而是先在页面上弹出浮动编辑窗口（锚定裁剪区旁、自动避让视口边缘）。工具栏提供画笔 / 文字 / 橡皮擦 / 4 色 swatch（红/黄/蓝/白）/ Undo（按钮 + Ctrl/Cmd+Z）——标注画在独立 overlay canvas 上（stroke-list 模型，橡皮擦 destination-out 只擦标注层不伤底图），✓ 把两层合成的无损 PNG 作为附件塞进 chat；Copy 一键把成品写入系统剪贴板（去任意地方粘贴，不进 chat）；✕ / Esc 取消。编辑器窗口内屏蔽原生右键菜单（原生「复制图片」拿到的是透明标注层，粘贴空白图）。底图未解码完之前 ✓ / Copy 置灰防呆。新文件 `lib/browser/region-editor.ts`（in-page 注入、自包含函数）；新增 i18n keys `chat.composer.cropEditor.{insertToChat,draw,text,erase,undo}`（en/zh_CN/zh_TW，✕ / Copy 复用 `common.cancel` / `common.copy`）

- Region screenshot upgraded to a Copilot-style annotation editor: after a drag-crop completes, the image no longer attaches to chat directly — a floating editor window pops up over the page first (anchored beside the crop, auto-flipped away from viewport edges). The toolbar offers Draw / Text / Eraser / 4 color swatches (red/yellow/blue/white) / Undo (button + Ctrl/Cmd+Z) — annotations render on a separate overlay canvas (stroke-list model; the eraser is a destination-out stroke that only clears the annotation layer, the base image stays untouched). ✓ composites both layers into a lossless PNG and attaches it to chat; Copy writes the composite straight to the system clipboard (paste anywhere, nothing enters chat); ✕ / Esc cancels. The native context menu is blocked inside the editor window (the native "Copy image" grabs the transparent annotation canvas and pastes blank). ✓ and Copy are gated behind image decode to prevent blank exports. New file `lib/browser/region-editor.ts` (in-page injected, fully self-contained function); new i18n keys `chat.composer.cropEditor.{insertToChat,draw,text,erase,undo}` (en/zh_CN/zh_TW; ✕ / Copy reuse the existing `common.cancel` / `common.copy`)

- Telegram 渠道的 `ask_user` 现在渲染为 inline keyboard 而不是永久挂起：此前 Telegram 会话里 agent 调用 `ask_user` 时表单无法送达（Telegram 是纯文本渠道），InteractiveBridge 无超时也无 resolve 路径，agent 永久卡在「🔧 Ask User...」状态行、后续消息全部堵在串行队列。现在 ask_user pending 时自动发送 `❓ <问题>` + inline keyboard（每选项一行；多选带 ✅ 前缀重绘 + `✅ Xong` 收口；`✍️ Nhập tay` 转自由文本，已点勾与文本合并不丢失；无选项问题走纯文本、下一条消息即答案），支持一次调用多道题逐题串行提问；答案经 `sessionManager.resolveTool` 回流，与 sidepanel 表单同一 resolve 链。10 分钟整场超时自动补 skipped 并清除键盘；键盘挂起时发送普通文本 = 放弃表单、消息照常聊天（对齐 sidepanel 语义）；`/tabs` 命令不受影响（照常走截图流程，`au:` 回调不弹「Đang chụp tab」toast、不吃 5s watchdog）。gateway 对 `au:` 回调保持无状态，`cap_*` 流程行为原状；部署顺序要求 gateway 先于 extension 上线

- `ask_user` on the Telegram channel now renders as an inline keyboard instead of hanging forever: previously, when the agent called `ask_user` in a Telegram session, the form could never be delivered (Telegram is a plain-text channel) and the InteractiveBridge had no timeout or resolve path, leaving the agent stuck on a "🔧 Ask User..." status line with all subsequent messages queued behind it. Now an ask_user pending state sends `❓ <question>` plus an inline keyboard (one option per row; multi-select re-renders with ✅ prefixes and commits via `✅ Xong`; `✍️ Nhập tay` switches to free text with prior ticks merged into the answer; option-less questions go as plain text where the next message is the answer), and a single call with multiple questions asks them sequentially. Answers flow back through `sessionManager.resolveTool` — the same resolve chain as the sidepanel form. A 10-minute per-ask timeout auto-fills skipped answers and clears the keyboard; sending a plain message while the keyboard is up dismisses the form and the message proceeds as normal chat (mirroring sidepanel semantics); the `/tabs` command is unaffected (capture flow intact — `au:` callbacks no longer show the "Đang chụp tab" toast nor arm the 5s watchdog). The gateway stays stateless for `au:` callbacks and the `cap_*` flow is byte-for-byte unchanged; the gateway must be deployed before the new extension build

- Telegram 网关新增可观测性（fail-loud，终结静默失败）：新增 `/status`（`/ping` 等效）命令——gateway 直接答复、不转发 agent，extension offline 时依然可用（正是最需要它的时刻），报告 gateway OK/DEGRADED、extension 在线数、uptime、最近 Telegram 入站 / 最近 extension action（秒级 ago）、当前 agent 状态；extension 全部离线时收到普通消息会立刻收到「⚠️ 没有任何 Chrome extension 在线——消息未被处理」警告而非被静默吞掉；agent 沉默超过 45s（re-arm 语义：距最后一条用户可见 action；fire-gate：仅 extension 申报的 `thinking`/`tool` 状态才报警——旧 extension / idle / waiting_user 一律抑制，任何部署组合零误报）主动推送「⏳ Agent 尚未响应」；`/health` v2 返回 `uptimeSec` / `lastInboundAt` / `lastBroadcastAt` / `lastExtensionActionAt` / `clientsDetail[]`（含 `sinceLastPongMs`——>60s 即 half-open socket）。配套：extension 在状态**转变**时经新增 one-way frame `agent_state`（`idle` / `thinking` / `tool`（带工具名）/ `waiting_user`——与 ask_user 键盘联动；turn abort 也显式回 idle）上报，gateway 存入 `agentStates` 供 `/status` 读取；不添加任何依赖

- Telegram gateway observability (fail-loud — no more silent failures): new `/status` (alias `/ping`) command answered by the gateway itself without forwarding to the agent — it works even when the extension is offline (exactly when it is needed most) and reports gateway OK/DEGRADED, extension count, uptime, seconds-ago for the last Telegram inbound / last extension action, and the current agent state; when no extension is online an ordinary incoming message now immediately gets a "⚠️ no Chrome extension online — your message was not processed" warning instead of being silently swallowed; if the agent goes silent for 45s the gateway proactively pushes a "⏳ Agent hasn't responded" warning (re-arm semantics: 45s since the last user-visible action; fire-gate: the warning fires only when the extension has reported `thinking`/`tool` — old extensions, `idle` and `waiting_user` are always suppressed, so zero false positives under any deploy combination); `/health` v2 returns `uptimeSec` / `lastInboundAt` / `lastBroadcastAt` / `lastExtensionActionAt` / `clientsDetail[]` (with `sinceLastPongMs` — >60s means a half-open socket). Companion change: the extension reports state **transitions** via a new one-way `agent_state` frame (`idle` / `thinking` / `tool` (with tool name) / `waiting_user` — tied into the ask_user keyboard; aborted turns also explicitly report idle), stored by the gateway into `agentStates` for `/status`; no new dependencies

- Telegram 新增 `/model` 命令：从手机直接给该 chat 的会话换模型，不用回到 Chrome 打开侧边栏。命令列出当前可用的全部模型（凭据已配 / OAuth 已验证 / 自定义 provider 恒可见——与侧边栏下拉同源 `listUsableModelGroups`），按 provider 分组渲染 inline keyboard，当前模型带 `✅` 前缀；点击即落库 + 对齐活着的 agent，并回复「✅ Đã chọn model: … (áp dụng từ tin nhắn tiếp theo)」。`callback_data` 用 `sm:<8位hex token>:<下标>`（恒 ≤14 byte，远在 Telegram 64-byte 上限内），token 每次 `/model` 重新生成——旧键盘的迟到点击一律静默吞掉；点击后先失效键盘再落库（one-shot，防双击重复换）。换模型写的是**会话行**（`touchUpdatedAt: false`，不把会话顶到历史列表最前），并广播 `session_changed`——侧边栏开着同一会话时模型 chip 会**即时**跟着变，无需刷新。因为 Telegram 的 prompt 不带 turn，会话行是唯一模型来源，所以落库后必须显式对齐活 agent（`refreshSessionConfig`，排进该 session 的串行队列等 turn 跑完——该函数在 agent 非 idle 时静默 skip，不排队会导致模型换了但 agent 仍用旧的直到 SW 重启）。另外修掉一个连带 bug：模型解析成功后 `modelFallback` 兜底标记没被清掉，导致「会话行模型失效 → Telegram 收到 ❌ → 用户用 `/model` 换成可用模型」之后**每条消息仍然失败**直到 SW 重启——现在 resolve 成功即解除标记。gateway 侧把 `sm:` 与 `au:` 一并纳入 `isExtensionKeyboard` 白名单：不再弹「⏳ Đang chụp tab…」toast，也不再 arm 5 秒 watchdog（该 watchdog 会把键盘改成「Hết giờ chụp」并清掉——而换模型要等 turn 结束，超过 5 秒是常态）。**部署顺序**：gateway 先于 extension 上线
- Telegram bot giờ có menu lệnh khi gõ `/`: gateway đăng ký `/model`, `/tabs`, `/status`, `/ping` qua Bot API `setMyCommands` lúc boot (best-effort, retry 1 lần sau 10s — fail thì chỉ log, không chặn khởi động vì autocomplete thiếu vẫn gõ tay được). Trước đây bot không đăng ký gì nên gõ `/` chỉ thấy danh sách rỗng. Lưu ý `setMyCommands` **thay thế toàn bộ** danh sách (kể cả lệnh từng set qua BotFather) — thêm lệnh mới thì sửa mảng `BOT_COMMANDS` trong `gateway/server.js`, đừng set tay qua BotFather vì lần boot sau sẽ ghi đè

- New Telegram `/model` command: switch the model of that chat's session straight from your phone, without going back to Chrome and opening the side panel. The command lists every currently usable model (configured API key / verified OAuth / custom providers always visible — same source as the sidebar dropdown, `listUsableModelGroups`), renders an inline keyboard grouped by provider with a `✅` prefix on the current model, and applies the pick on tap: the row is persisted, the live agent is realigned, and it replies "✅ Đã chọn model: … (áp dụng từ tin nhắn tiếp theo)". `callback_data` uses `sm:<8-hex token>:<index>` (always ≤14 bytes, far under Telegram's 64-byte cap) with a freshly generated token per `/model` invocation, so late taps on a stale keyboard are silently swallowed; the keyboard is invalidated before the write (one-shot, so a double-tap cannot switch twice). The switch writes the **session row** (`touchUpdatedAt: false`, so it does not bump the session to the top of History) and broadcasts `session_changed` — a side panel already showing that session updates its model chip **immediately**, no refresh needed. Because Telegram prompts carry no turn, the session row is the sole model source, so the live agent must be realigned explicitly after the write (`refreshSessionConfig`, enqueued on that session's serial queue to wait for the running turn — the function silently skips while the agent is not idle, and without the queue the model would appear switched while the agent kept the old one until the SW restarted). A connected bug is fixed too: `modelFallback` was never cleared after a successful model resolve, so "row model goes unusable → Telegram gets ❌ → user runs `/model` and picks a working model" left **every following message failing** until the SW restarted — the flag is now cleared on a successful resolve. On the gateway side, `sm:` joins `au:` in the `isExtensionKeyboard` whitelist: no more "⏳ Đang chụp tab…" toast, and no 5-second watchdog (which would rewrite the keyboard to "Hết giờ chụp" and clear it — while a model switch waits for the running turn, so exceeding 5 seconds is the normal case). **Deploy order**: gateway before the extension build (same convention as the existing `au:` path)
- The Telegram bot now shows a command menu when you type `/`: the gateway registers `/model`, `/tabs`, `/status` and `/ping` via the Bot API `setMyCommands` at boot (best-effort with one retry after 10s — a failure only logs, it never blocks startup, since a missing autocomplete still lets commands be typed by hand). Previously nothing was registered, so typing `/` showed an empty list. Note that `setMyCommands` **replaces the whole list** (including anything set through BotFather) — to add a command, edit the `BOT_COMMANDS` array in `gateway/server.js` rather than setting it by hand in BotFather, whose entry would be overwritten on the next boot

- 知识库（RAG）连接测试新增向量索引告警：测试连接时若无法创建向量检索索引（HNSW），现在会在「设置 → 知识库」就地显示一条琥珀色提示并附上原始错误，而不是只在 service worker 控制台打一条几乎看不见的 `console.warn`。关键词检索不受影响，但向量检索会退化为全表扫描——此前用户对此毫无感知，只会在集合变大后觉得检索莫名变慢
- Knowledge (RAG) connection test now surfaces a vector-index warning: if the dense retrieval index (HNSW) cannot be created, Settings → Knowledge shows an inline amber notice with the underlying error, instead of a `console.warn` in the service worker console that is effectively invisible. Keyword search is unaffected, but vector search degrades to a sequential scan — previously nothing told the user, who would only notice retrieval getting mysteriously slow as a collection grew

- 知识库（RAG）的嵌入维度改由模型决定：测试连接时先向嵌入端点发一个短字符串，按**模型实际返回的向量宽度**建列，而不是用设置里手填的数字（Cebian 默认 1536，但不少本地模型是 1024）。当设置值与模型不符时，就地提示并给一个「改用 N」按钮一键更正——测试连接本身仍是只读操作，不会代用户改设置。此前默认值不匹配时，建列会直接失败（列一旦建成 `vector(n)`，宽度不符的数据就再也写不进去）。同时，嵌入端点不可达时也会在测试结果里直接说明，而不是等到索引时才以维度不符的形式暴露
- Knowledge (RAG) embedding dimension is now decided by the model: Test connection sends a short string to the embedding endpoint first and sizes the column from the width the model **actually returns**, instead of the hand-typed number in Settings (Cebian defaults to 1536, while many local models are 1024). When the saved value disagrees with the model, an inline notice offers a one-click "Use N" fix — Test connection itself stays read-only and never rewrites your settings. Previously a mismatched default made column creation fail outright, since once the column is `vector(n)` data of the wrong width can never be written. An unreachable embedding endpoint is now also reported right in the test result, rather than surfacing much later as a dimension mismatch during indexing

- 知识库（RAG）改为按文书结构切分：识别 `Phần / Chương / Mục / Tiểu mục / Điều / Khoản` 层级与 markdown 标题，尽量让一个 `Điều` 完整落在一个 chunk 里；`Điều` 过长时按 `Khoản` 再拆，仍过长才退回滑动窗口。每个 chunk 携带其所属标题路径（如 `Chương II > Điều 4`），该路径会一并进入嵌入输入与 BM25 词流，因此检索「điều 4 khoản 2」这类按条文定位的查询不再因为标题与正文被切开而失准。无结构的普通文本行为不变；代码围栏、目录、句中引用（`theo Điều 5 của Luật`）都不会被误判为标题
- Knowledge (RAG) now chunks by document structure: it recognises the `Phần / Chương / Mục / Tiểu mục / Điều / Khoản` hierarchy and markdown headings, keeping a whole `Điều` in one chunk where it fits; an oversized `Điều` splits on `Khoản` boundaries first and only falls back to the sliding window as a last resort. Every chunk carries the heading path it sits under (e.g. `Chương II > Điều 4`), and that path feeds both the embedding input and the BM25 token stream — so article-scoped queries like "điều 4 khoản 2" no longer miss because the heading and its body were split apart. Unstructured prose behaves exactly as before; code fences, tables of contents, and mid-sentence citations (`theo Điều 5 của Luật`) are never mistaken for headings

- Canvas 面板新增「下载 HTML」按钮（标题栏，打开文件时可用）：把当前预览的文件按字节原样存为 `.html`，文件名取自路径的 basename；内容直接来自内存快照，不走 IPC
- The Canvas pane gains a "Download HTML" button in its header (enabled while a file is open): saves the currently previewed file verbatim as `.html`, named after the path's basename; the bytes come straight from the in-memory snapshot, no IPC involved

### 变更 / Changed

- 知识库（RAG）重新索引默认改为「只增不删」：往已有 collection 里加文件时，之前索引过的文件不再受影响（此前没有任何机制保证这点，重新索引只挑选部分文件时的行为并不明确）。要清理不在本次选择里的旧文件，需在重新索引对话框里显式勾选「让 collection 与本次选择一致」——勾选后、写入任何数据之前，会先列出**确切的文件清单**让你确认；拒绝则整轮取消，一个字节都不写。collection 行上会提示「有 N 个源不在最近一次索引中」作为何时该清理的信号（刻意不叫「已删除」：我们不追踪磁盘状态，分不出文件被删了还是这次没选）。另外，文件内容变短时会自动裁掉尾部多余的 chunk，这只影响该文件本身
- Knowledge (RAG) re-indexing is now add-only by default: adding a file to an existing collection no longer touches the files already indexed there (previously nothing guaranteed this, so re-indexing with a partial file selection had unclear behaviour). To clean up files that are not part of the current selection, tick "Sync collection with this selection" in the re-index dialog — the exact file list is shown for confirmation before anything is written, and declining cancels the whole run without writing a byte. Collection rows now show "N sources not in the last index run" as a signal for when a cleanup is due (deliberately not called "deleted": we do not track disk state, so a file that was removed and one that simply was not picked look identical). A file that gets shorter also has its now-surplus trailing chunks trimmed, scoped to that file alone

- 知识库（RAG）重新索引时跳过未变动的分块：内容、所属标题路径、上下文前缀与嵌入模型都没变的分块直接沿用已有向量，不再重复调用嵌入接口；Contextual Retrieval 开启时，文档未变动的分块也跳过生成前缀的 LLM 调用。第一次重新索引仍会全部重算（分块方式刚变过，没有可复用的），收益从第二次开始。另外，若 collection 里存在**另一种嵌入模型**的向量、且它们会保留下来，索引会中止并说明两条出路（把该 collection 的文件全部重选一遍，或开启同步模式替换）——两种模型的向量混在同一空间里 cosine 会失去意义，且不会报任何错，所以宁可停下
- Knowledge (RAG) skips unchanged chunks when re-indexing: a chunk whose content, heading path, context prefix and embedding model are all unchanged reuses its stored vector instead of calling the embedding endpoint again, and with Contextual Retrieval on, chunks from an unchanged document also skip the prefix-generation LLM call. The first re-index still recomputes everything (chunking just changed, so nothing is reusable); the saving starts from the second run. Separately, if the collection holds vectors from a **different embedding model** that would survive the run, indexing stops and states the two ways out (re-select every file in that collection, or turn on sync to replace them) — vectors from two models in one space make cosine meaningless, and nothing would report it, so stopping is the safer default

- 知识库（RAG）的数据库与嵌入调用现在有超时与重试：数据库查询 15 秒超时、瞬时故障（连接中断、服务端重启、资源不足）自动重试 3 次并带指数退避；schema 迁移这类重活单独放宽到 10 分钟，因为 `ALTER COLUMN TYPE` 与建索引在大集合上本就需要更久，用统一超时会误杀它们。嵌入调用同样有 15 秒超时与重试，遇到限流（429）会优先按服务端返回的 `Retry-After` 等待，而不是盲目退避；请求本身有误（400/401/404）不重试——重试只会再失败一次。此外，聊天发送时的检索嵌入也补上了超时，端点卡住不会再让输入框一直转圈
- Knowledge (RAG) database and embedding calls now have timeouts and retries: database queries time out after 15 seconds and transient failures (dropped connections, server restarts, resource exhaustion) are retried 3 times with exponential backoff; heavy schema work gets its own 10-minute ceiling, because `ALTER COLUMN TYPE` and index creation genuinely take longer on large collections and a single shared limit would kill them. Embedding calls likewise time out after 15 seconds and retry — a rate limit (429) waits for the server's `Retry-After` when it provides one instead of backing off blindly, while a bad request (400/401/404) is not retried, since retrying only fails again. Send-time retrieval embeddings also gained a timeout, so a hung endpoint no longer leaves the composer spinning

- Telegram 工具状态行（`🔧 <label>...`）现在每秒轮换 emoji 前缀，缓释「工具在跑但屏幕完全静止」的等待焦虑：cycle 顺序 `🔧 → ⚙️ → 🛠 → 🔩 → 🔧 ...`（其中 `⚙️` / `🛠` 显式拼 U+FE0F variation selector，避免在 text-default codepoint 上回退到黑白线稿），1Hz 节奏由**递归 `setTimeout`** 调度——下一帧只在上一帧的 `sendOutbound().finally()` 落定后才排队，杜绝慢网下并行 `editMessageText` 撞 429 / 互相覆盖。Label 切换**不重置** frame 索引：emoji 在切换瞬间同字形短暂停在两个 label 上（`🛠 Reading file... → 🛠 Browsing web...`），视觉上像「同一把工具换目标」，比每跳工具都从 `🔧` 重新起跑自然。Label 变更的 `pendingStatusText` 优先于动画 tick（动画跳过、节流窗口先发、下一帧从当前 frame 续接）；finalize / abort / gateway teardown 三条收尾路径都通过 `stopStatusAnimation` 显式停掉 timer 并 `clearTimeout` 尚未触发的下一帧，状态行删除时 `statusDead` 标记再保一层（双重保险）；动画启动时机为「状态行首发送出且落地」之后，避免对还在 in-flight 的 `message_id` 发起 edit
- Telegram tool status line (`🔧 <label>...`) now rotates the emoji prefix every second to soften the "tool is running but the screen is frozen" wait: cycle order `🔧 → ⚙️ → 🛠 → 🔩 → 🔧 ...` (where `⚙️` / `🛠` are explicitly followed by U+FE0F variation selector to avoid the monochrome outline fallback on text-default codepoints), paced at 1Hz by a **recursive `setTimeout`** — the next frame is only scheduled after the previous frame's `sendOutbound().finally()` settles, so a slow network can never stack parallel `editMessageText` calls that hit 429 or overwrite each other. Label changes **do not reset** the frame index: the emoji briefly stays on the same glyph across two labels (`🛠 Reading file... → 🛠 Browsing web...`), which reads as "the same tool retargeted" and feels more natural than restarting at `🔧` for every new tool. The label-change `pendingStatusText` takes priority over animation ticks (animation skips, the throttle window goes first, the next frame resumes from the current frame); all three teardown paths — finalize, abort, gateway teardown — call `stopStatusAnimation` to clear the timer and `clearTimeout` any pending next-frame, with the `statusDead` flag on status-line delete as a second line of defense; the animation starts only after the status line's first send lands, so edits are never issued against an in-flight `message_id`
- AI 回复的流式上屏改为打字机缓冲队列（Typewriter Buffer Queue）：新内容先进队列，再以约每秒 30 次的匀速逐字上屏，模型一次吐出大段内容时自动加速追赶（约 0.4 秒追平积压），生成结束立即冲刷剩余队列——不吞字、不跳变；历史消息等非流式渲染路径零改动
- chat 里指向 VFS `.html` 文件的链接（`#/...` 形态）现在直接在 Canvas pane 里打开：点击自动展开 Canvas 面板并加载该文件，不再跳到独立的 VFS 视图。链接拦截只接管 HTML 文件——`.md` 等其它类型与 VFS 标签页内部的 markdown 链接行为不变
- Streaming AI replies now land through a typewriter buffer queue: new content enters a queue and is revealed at a steady ~30 per-second pace, large bursts auto-accelerate to catch up within ~0.4s, and the remaining buffer flushes the moment generation ends — no swallowed text, no jumps; non-streaming rendering (chat history) is untouched
- Links in chat pointing at VFS `.html` files (`#/...` form) now open directly in the Canvas pane: clicking expands the Canvas panel and loads the file instead of jumping to the separate VFS view. Interception only takes over HTML files — `.md` and other types, and markdown links rendered inside the VFS tab itself, keep their previous behavior
- `frontend_coder` worker system prompt 的规则 (5) 收紧为「one `fs_create_file` then emit handoff and STOP — **NEVER** re-read or `fs_list` to verify; the file is final」，把这条原本只是软建议的 handoff-after-write 流程硬编码为 loop guard，让 LLM 在大多数情况下能直接收到提示就停下；Non-artifact clause 同步把规则 (5) 从「relax 1/3/5」改成「keep rules 2 + 4 + **5**; relax 1/3」，即使最终产物不是 artifact 也禁止 re-read 自己刚写的文件（同一份浪费 round-trip）。整体 prompt 由 ~479 字符增至 ~572 字符，`worker-roles.test.ts` 的 char cap 由 500 上调到 600，留 28 字符 buffer 给后续微调；这是与 Subtask 8.8 同源的 prompt-side 防线，作用于 LLM 自身行为（不一定每次生效），L2 detector 是兜底
- The `frontend_coder` worker system prompt rule (5) tightened to "one `fs_create_file` then emit handoff and STOP — **NEVER** re-read or `fs_list` to verify; the file is final", turning what was a soft handoff-after-write suggestion into an explicit loop guard so the LLM can stop on its own in the majority of cases. The Non-artifact clause no longer relaxes rule (5): it now reads "keep rules 2 + 4 + **5**; relax 1/3", so even non-artifact tasks are forbidden from re-reading a freshly-written file (same wasted round-trip). Total prompt grew from ~479 to ~572 chars; `worker-roles.test.ts` char cap bumped 500 → 600 to leave a 28-char buffer for future tweaks. This is the prompt-side counterpart of Subtask 8.8 and acts on the LLM's own behavior (does not always hold); the L2 detector is the deterministic backstop
- Settings 4 个区块（RagSection / AdvancedSection / PageInteractionSection / CollectionsSection）做视觉统一：每张外层卡片新增 size-9 圆角 pastel 图标徽章（RAG：Database 蓝 / Layers 紫 / SlidersHorizontal 翠 / Bot 靛 / Folder 橙；Advanced：Sparkles 蓝 / MousePointerClick 翠 / Users 靛；PageInteraction：CircleDot 蓝 / MousePointer2 翠 / Sliders 紫 / Zap 橙），深色模式对应 `*-950/40` + `*-400` 变体，徽章 aria-hidden（标题文字旁已有 Label/Text 承担可读语义）。Retrieval Mode 单选改造为 full-bleed 选择卡：Hybrid 选中态走 emerald 边框 + 50/30 背景 + 1px ring + 嵌入式 outline Badge「Recommended」；Vector 选中态走标准 primary 边框；整个 label 区域都是 click target，Radix RadioGroupItem 仍保留键盘语义。Card 1 连接状态指示器改为彩色圆点 + 短标签（emerald ● Sẵn sàng / amber ● Thiếu pgvector / destructive ● Kết nối thất bại），参照 `components/settings/mcp/MCPServerRow.tsx` size-2 圆点样式，idle 状态返回 null 不留死重。各卡片 hint 文本全部缩到 ≤ 1 句，去掉重复或冗长副标题（三语 locale 通过 pnpm check 的 i18n lint 强制对齐）。Settings → Skills / Prompts 共享的 FileTree 顶层条目（工作区根目录直接子节点 = skill / prompt 文件夹）改用 colored monogram 徽章（8 色 pastel 调色板 + FNV-1a 哈希 + ≤ 2 字符 Unicode monogram，覆盖 ASCII 与 CJK），嵌套条目保留默认 FolderOpen / Folder / FileIcon，让用户在长列表中一眼区分每个 skill / prompt
- Settings sections (RagSection / AdvancedSection / PageInteractionSection / CollectionsSection) received a unified visual polish: every outer card now leads with a size-9 rounded pastel icon badge (RAG: Database blue / Layers purple / SlidersHorizontal emerald / Bot indigo / Folder orange; Advanced: Sparkles blue / MousePointerClick emerald / Users indigo; PageInteraction: CircleDot blue / MousePointer2 emerald / Sliders purple / Zap orange), with `*-950/40` + `*-400` dark-mode counterparts. The badge carries `aria-hidden` because the title text beside it is the real label. The Retrieval Mode radio became full-bleed selection cards: the active Hybrid option uses an emerald border + `bg-emerald-50/30` + 1px ring + inline outline `Recommended` Badge; Vector uses the standard primary border. The whole `<label>` is now the click target while the Radix `RadioGroupItem` preserves keyboard semantics. The Card 1 connection status indicator became a coloured dot + short label (emerald ● Ready / amber ● pgvector missing / destructive ● Connection failed), mirroring the `size-2 rounded-full` pattern from `components/settings/mcp/MCPServerRow.tsx`; the idle state renders null so the row carries no dead weight. All card hint texts were trimmed to ≤ 1 sentence (the three-locale i18n parity is enforced by `pnpm check`'s lint pass). The shared `FileTree` used by Settings → Skills and Settings → Prompts now renders a coloured monogram badge on every top-level entry (immediate child of the workspace root) — 8-colour pastel palette seeded by FNV-1a, ≤ 2-char Unicode monogram (covers both ASCII and CJK so Chinese-named folders show the actual characters, not `?`). Nested entries keep the default `FolderOpen` / `Folder` / `FileIcon` rendering, so the user can scan a long skill / prompt list by colour + monogram in a glance
- 顶部 Header 中打开侧边栏的按钮图标由 `History`（时钟）改为 `PanelLeft`，tooltip 从「History」改为「Sidebar」/「侧边栏」/「側邊欄」，与实际包含 Collections / MCP / Memory / History 的抽屉面板语义一致
- 侧边栏 History 默认只展开「Pinned」与「Today」两个分组，「Previous 7 days / 30 days / 更早」保持折叠，需要时点 header 才展开；默认列表缩短，避免一打开就一长串
- `<context>` block 新增两个数字字段 `bodyTextSize` (字符数) 和 `wordCount` (空格切词数)，给主代理后续做 `read_page` vs `delegate_dom` 路由的启发式输入。数字在 injected script 里算好，只发两个整数跨 IPC，不传完整 body text（Stage 2 会接上 system prompt routing 规则，本条只交付数据层）
- `delegate_dom` 现在校验 `expected_schema`：子代理返回的 JSON 会用 TypeBox `Value.Check` 验证 schema；schema 不是合法 JSON 时 runner 在调用 LLM 前就返回 `Invalid expected_schema: not valid JSON`（不浪费 token）；LLM 输出不符合 schema 时自动重试 1 次（同时把 `complexity` 从 `simple` 升级到 `complex`，并在 prompt 末尾追加 `<retry-feedback>` 块让 LLM 看到具体错处），仍失败则返回 `Schema validation failed: <path>: <message>`。共享 helper 抽到 `lib/agent/schema-validate.ts`，`delegate_task` 也复用同一份 `parseExpectedSchema` + `checkSchema`（`worker-runner.ts` 仅 import、零行为差异），错误格式与 `lib/mcp/client.ts` 的 `${instancePath}: ${message}` 对齐
- `delegate_dom` 新增 `complexity: 'fast'` 单发（single-shot）模式：当 `<context>` block 显示 `wordCount > 2000` 时，主代理可改用此模式跳过 ReAct loop，整条流程只跑一次 LLM call：复用 `read_page` 的 `article` 提取（`getDocumentHtml` + offscreen `convertArticleToMarkdown`），把清理后的 Markdown body 一次性喂给 LLM，再走 Stage 1 接的 schema 校验（不重试）。Readability 识别失败时回退到 raw-text extraction；fast path 完全不创建 agent、不走 `acquireKeepAlive`，`sub_agent:dom:done` 日志会记录 `effectiveComplexity: 'fast'` + `attemptCount: 1`。System prompt 的「Tools」roster 新增 `delegate_dom` 条目，「Choosing the Right Tool」与「Reading Pages」小节各加一条路由提示，让主代理识别长页面走快路。Schema 与 `RunDomSubAgentOptions.complexity` 的字面量集合从 `'simple' | 'complex'` 扩到 `'simple' | 'complex' | 'fast'`
- 侧边栏四个 region 现在共用同一套水平边距（Worker team / MCP / Collections 均带 `mx-3 mt-3`，Collections 补齐后与前者一致；History 也加上 `mx-3 mt-3`，不再贴右边缘），左右边缘对齐；排序调整为 Worker team → MCP Servers → Collections → History
- 修复「输入消息后用户气泡不锁定在视口顶部、AI 回复滚动把人问题顶走」的 bug：`useStickToBottom.scrollToUserPrompt` 一直通过 `[data-user-message="last"]` 选 DOM 节点，但 `UserMessageBubble` 从未给最后一个 user 消息挂这个属性，导致 snap 静默 no-op；现在 chat 页传 `isLast={idx === lastUserMsgIndex}` 给 `UserMessageBubble`，仅最新一条 user 消息节点带这个标记，hook 重新生效——Enter 后气泡立刻位于视口顶部 16px 处，AI 流式输出在其下方生成，用户按自己节奏手动滚动
- MCP 服务器列表改为折叠式紧凑行（合并此前的瘦身后 card 与本次重做）：折叠态单行 `[状态点] [名称] [展开箭头]`，点击行展开后底部显示传输协议 pill / URL / 认证方式 / 当前状态，再下面是 Disable / Edit / Delete 操作按钮；Settings 与 sidebar 抽屉共享同一行组件。整行作为拖拽手柄（无需独立 grip），PointerSensor 5px 阈值让普通点击仍走展开按钮，按住并位移才触发拖拽；键盘拖拽由 dnd-kit KeyboardSensor 提供（Tab → Space 拾起 → 方向键 → Space 落下）。状态点取代折叠行的 toggle 开关，开关与编辑 / 删除一起搬到展开面板。Sidebar drawer 的 MCP section 现在也采用与 Worker team 一致的折叠外壳（`rounded-lg border` + `mx-3 mt-3` + chevron 按钮 header + `N / N 已配置` 徽标），折叠时只露 header 一行；Settings 页标题旁也新增同款 `N / N 已配置` 徽标，描述文案同步缩短（去掉「Model Context Protocol」全称，三语对齐）
- 聊天区消息字重从 normal (400) 提到 medium (500)：user 气泡 (UserMessageBubble) 和 AI 回复正文 (AgentMessage) 的 `text-[0.9rem]` 都加上 `font-medium`，Geist 500 比 Geist 400 厚重一档，长消息读起来不再发"mỏng"，更接近 Copilot / Claude.ai 的字感
- 斜杠命令（如 `/english`）的展开内容不再在气泡里铺满整段文本：气泡上方现在渲染一个紧凑 chip（Zap 图标 + `/{name}` 标签 + 琥珀色描边），气泡本身只显示用户在命令后实际输入的文字；mention chip（`@prompt` / `@skill`）在 one-shot 提及时走同一套路径。**Pin 指令（`pinned="true"`）跳过气泡上的 chip**——pin 已经在底部的 composer chip 条可见，每条气泡再重复一次只是污染聊天历史；模型仍按原样接收完整的展开指令块
- 会话列表里的标题不再只是首条消息的前 50 个字符（slash 命令展开后那一行经常是 `[DIRECTIVE — ...]` 字面块，做标题很难看）：第一个 assistant 消息落地后 background 会异步调一次 LLM（用当前会话正在用的模型 + 凭证），按 GPT/Claude 风格生成 ≤40 字符的 topic-name 标题；标题生成失败 / 取消 / 用户在中途手动改过名，原 heuristic 标题保留不动（不会被 LLM 覆盖）；pre-feature / 备份恢复的会话不重命名
- 聊天输入框（ChatInput 的 `<textarea>`）加上 `spellCheck={false}`：之前用户用浏览器默认字典里没有的语言（如越南语）打字，每个字都被画上红色波浪线（拼写错误标记），看起来像出错；chat composer 是 prompt 容器，浏览器原生的拼写检查在这里没有意义，关掉之后非英文输入也不再被误标记
- Knowledge (RAG) 设置纳入备份体系：`ragSettings`（连接串 / embedder / rerank 配置）已注册到 `BACKUP_REGISTRY`，敏感字段（`neonConnectionString` / `embedderApiKey` / `rerankApiKey`）走 `splitSecret` 抽到 credentials.json（非加密导出时也只放在加密层），safe 副本里把对应字段清空再写回 config.json；`ragCollections` 同样以 settings 分类纳入备份，merge 恢复时按 collection 名补缺（本地全保留、备份里本地没有的补入），safe 部分不含密钥所以两种模式都不会泄露
- AI 流式输出末尾的等待指示器从橙色脉冲方块改成 Copilot 风格双圆点轨道旋转 spinner：两个直径 9px（`r=4.5`，比最初 3px 草案大 3 倍）的填充圆点（`fill=currentColor`，无 stroke）对称放在 24×24 SVG 中心左右各 6.5px 处（`cx=5.5` / `cx=18.5`，`cy=12`），整组 `<g>` 施加 `animate-spin`（1.2s/圈）做 **orbital rotation**——两个圆点保持自身朝向不变、围绕 SVG 中心做轨道运动，而不是各自绕轴自转。轨道直径 13px > disc 直径 9px 所以无论转到哪个角度两个圆点都互不遮挡。DOM wrapper 从 `w-4 h-4` 升到 `w-6 h-6`（24×24）以容纳大圆点，`align-middle` 与正文 x-height 对齐不变；wrapper 加 `opacity-70` 让圆点比纯 `text-primary` 柔和一档，避免在长流式输出中过于抢眼。pulse 节奏对不齐 token 落地速度的问题也顺带解决
- 系统提示新增 1 句轻量 batching hint：建议模型把多个彼此独立的 tool call（并行读多个文件、并行检查多个 URL、互不依赖的 `fs_*` 操作）放进同一个 assistant 回合发出，由运行时 `Promise.all` 并发执行以降低延迟；同时明确「依赖上一轮结果的 call 必须串行」，避免模型把数据依赖当成可并行。这条 prompt 不改 runtime（`pi-agent-core@0.84.2` 已默认 `toolExecution: "parallel"`、单次 follow-up request 批量回传结果——见 audit），只为让更「保守」的模型（如 antigravity / gemini-3.8-flash 经 9router-go 网关仅 1 tool call / 回合）从并行能力中获益

- The Header's sidebar-toggle button icon changed from `History` (clock glyph) to `PanelLeft`, and its tooltip changed from "History" to "Sidebar" / "侧边栏" / "側邊欄", matching the drawer panel that actually contains Collections / MCP / Memory / History
- Sidebar History now expands only the "Pinned" and "Today" groups by default; "Previous 7 days / 30 days / Older" stay collapsed and only reveal on header click — the list opens to a tighter, more glanceable view
- The `<context>` block now exposes two new numeric fields — `bodyTextSize` (character count) and `wordCount` (whitespace-split word count) — as input signals for the main agent's future `read_page` vs `delegate_dom` routing heuristic. Both numbers are computed in the injected script on the page side, so only two integers cross the IPC boundary — the full body text is never serialized. (Stage 2 will wire the corresponding system-prompt routing rule; this entry only ships the data layer.)
- `delegate_dom` now validates `expected_schema`: the sub-agent's JSON is checked against a TypeBox `Value.Check` schema. Malformed schema (not valid JSON) returns `Invalid expected_schema: not valid JSON` from the runner *before* any LLM call is made (no tokens wasted). Schema mismatch auto-retries once with `complexity` escalated `simple → complex` and a `<retry-feedback>` block appended to the prompt telling the LLM exactly which field failed; if the retry also fails, the call returns `Schema validation failed: <path>: <message>`. The shared helpers now live in `lib/agent/schema-validate.ts`; `delegate_task` reuses the same `parseExpectedSchema` + `checkSchema` (the `worker-runner.ts` update is an import-only move with zero behavior change), and the error format (`${instancePath}: ${message}`) matches `lib/mcp/client.ts` so log/UI output is consistent regardless of which validator produced it
- `delegate_dom` gains a new `complexity: 'fast'` single-shot mode: when the `<context>` block reports `wordCount > 2000`, the main agent can skip the ReAct loop and run exactly one LLM call — the fast path reuses `read_page`'s `article` extraction (`getDocumentHtml` + offscreen `convertArticleToMarkdown`), feeds the cleaned Markdown body straight to the LLM, then runs the Stage 1 schema validator (no retry). Readability extraction failure falls back to raw-text extraction; the fast path never creates a sub-agent, never calls `acquireKeepAlive`, and the `sub_agent:dom:done` log records `effectiveComplexity: 'fast'` + `attemptCount: 1`. The system prompt's `## Tools` roster gains a `delegate_dom` entry, and both `### Choosing the Right Tool` and `### Reading Pages` get a routing bullet so the main agent learns to recognize long pages and take the fast path. The schema literal set widens from `'simple' | 'complex'` to `'simple' | 'complex' | 'fast'`
- `delegate_dom` `complexity: 'fast'` gains two automatic short-circuits on top of the existing single-shot LLM path:
  - **JSON-LD bypass** — when the page exposes `<script type="application/ld+json">` and the harvested `WebPage.mainEntity` (or fallback non-`WebPage` / non-`WebSite` / non-`BreadcrumbList` node) passes `parseExpectedSchema` + `checkSchema` against `expected_schema`, the runner returns the parsed JSON directly with **zero LLM tokens**. Bad blocks are silently skipped per the W3C JSON-LD 1.1 spec; cross-block `@id` references are resolved; `@graph` arrays are unwrapped. If JSON-LD is absent or the mainEntity doesn't match the schema, the runner falls through to the existing article-body LLM path — same `{ status, data, reason }` shape either way. Debug log emits `sub_agent:dom:fast:jsonld_bypass` (with `@type` + raw/node count) on hit and `sub_agent:dom:fast:jsonld_schema_mismatch` on miss.
  - **Vision fallback** — when `convertArticleToMarkdown` plus the raw-text fallback both produce an empty body (canvas-rendered charts, WebGL maps, image-only infographics) **and** the configured sub-agent model is vision-capable (`model.input` includes `'image'`), the runner swaps the active tab if needed, captures the visible viewport via `chrome.tabs.captureVisibleTab({ format: 'jpeg', quality: 75 })`, restores the previously active tab, and feeds the JPEG as an `ImageContent` block (`{ type: 'image', data, mimeType: 'image/jpeg' }`) alongside the text prompt to the LLM — the model answers from the screenshot rather than the empty body. If the sub-agent model is text-only, the empty-body error still surfaces so the main agent can fall back to `read_page` or `screenshot`. Both short-circuits are gated to `complexity: 'fast'` only — `simple` and `complex` (the ReAct paths) are unchanged.
  The `delegate_dom` tool description (`complexity` parameter + object-level description) and the main agent's system prompt (`### Choosing the Right Tool` + `### Reading Pages` sections) document both short-circuits so the main agent knows when to pass `expected_schema` and when to trust auto-capture.
- The four sidebar regions now share a single horizontal-margin vocabulary: Worker team / MCP / Collections all carry `mx-3 mt-3`, and History adds the same `mx-3 mt-3` so its content no longer bleeds to the right edge — all four sections share the same left and right edges. Region order is now Worker team → MCP Servers → Collections → History
- Fixed the "user bubble not pinned at viewport top on send" regression — `useStickToBottom.scrollToUserPrompt` queries `[data-user-message="last"]` to snap the latest prompt to the top, but `UserMessageBubble` never attached that attribute, so the snap silently no-op'd and the AI stream scrolled the question off-screen. The chat page now passes `isLast={idx === lastUserMsgIndex}`, only the newest user message carries the marker, and the snap works as designed: on Enter the bubble jumps to 16px from the viewport top, AI text streams below, and the user scrolls at their own pace
- MCP server list now renders as collapsible compact rows (consolidating the prior leaner-card step and this redesign into a single entry describing the final behavior): collapsed = single line `[status dot] [name] [chevron]`; clicking the row expands it to reveal the transport pill / URL / auth / live status, with Disable / Edit / Delete buttons at the bottom. Settings and the sidebar drawer share the same row component. The whole row is now the drag handle (no separate grip); the PointerSensor 5px activation threshold keeps normal clicks on the expand button while press-and-move triggers the drag — keyboard reorder still works via dnd-kit's KeyboardSensor (Tab → Space → arrows → Space). The status dot replaces the on/off toggle on the collapsed row; the toggle now lives in the expanded panel along with Edit / Delete. The sidebar drawer's MCP section also adopts the Worker-team collapsible shell (`rounded-lg border` + `mx-3 mt-3` + chevron-button header with `aria-expanded` + `aria-label` + `N / N configured` counter badge) and shows only the header row when collapsed. Settings adds the same `N / N configured` badge next to its title and trims the description (drops the full "Model Context Protocol" form, all three locales aligned)
- Chat message weight bumped from normal (400) to medium (500): both the user bubble (`UserMessageBubble`) and the agent body (`AgentMessage`) add `font-medium` to their `text-[0.9rem]` container. Geist 500 reads visibly heavier than 400, so longer replies no longer feel thin — closer to the body weight in Copilot / Claude.ai
- Slash commands (e.g. `/english`) no longer dump the full expanded body into the message bubble; the directive now renders as a compact chip above the bubble (Zap icon + `/{name}` label + amber outline), while the bubble itself shows only the words the user actually typed after the command. Mention chips (`@prompt` / `@skill`) follow the same path on one-shot mentions. **Pinned directives (`pinned="true"`) skip the bubble chip** — the pin is already visible in the composer strip at the bottom of the panel and would just clutter chat history if repeated on every bubble. The model still receives the full expanded directive block as before
- Sidebar session titles no longer come from `text.slice(0, 50)` on the first user message — that line is often a literal `[DIRECTIVE — ATTACHED COMMAND: "..."]` block after slash-command expansion, which makes for an unreadable title. After the first assistant message lands, the background fires a one-shot LLM call (using the session's active model + credentials, no new setting) to produce a topic-name title (≤40 chars, ChatGPT/Claude style) and broadcasts `session_changed` so the row updates live. On LLM failure / abort / mid-flight manual rename the original heuristic title is kept (the LLM result is never written). Pre-feature / restored-from-backup sessions aren't renamed — only brand-new sessions created in this SW lifetime
- Chat composer textarea (`<textarea>` in ChatInput) now sets `spellCheck={false}`: previously, typing in a language not covered by the browser's default dictionary (e.g. Vietnamese) painted a red wavy underline under every word as a misspelling marker, which read as an error. The chat composer is a prompt container — browser-native spell-check has no role here — so disabling it stops the misleading underlines on non-English input
- Knowledge (RAG) settings are now part of the backup: `ragSettings` (connection string / embedder / rerank config) is registered in `BACKUP_REGISTRY`, sensitive fields (`neonConnectionString` / `embedderApiKey` / `rerankApiKey`) flow through `splitSecret` into `credentials.json` (only the encrypted tier carries them in unencrypted exports), the safe copy clears those fields before writing to `config.json`. `ragCollections` is similarly covered under the settings class; merge restore applies `fillMissingById` (keep every local collection, add anything from the backup that is missing locally) and no keys are involved, so neither mode ever leaks secrets
- The trailing "still streaming" indicator at the end of an AI reply changed from an orange pulsing square to a Copilot-style two-disc orbital spinner: two 9px filled discs (`r=4.5`, 3× the initial 3px draft; `fill=currentColor`, no stroke) symmetrically placed 6.5px to either side of the 24×24 SVG center (`cx=5.5` / `cx=18.5`, `cy=12`), wrapped in a single `<g>` that applies `animate-spin` (1.2s per cycle) for **orbital rotation** — the discs keep their orientation and travel around the SVG center together, rather than each self-rotating around its own axis. The 13px orbit diameter exceeds the 9px disc diameter, so the two never occlude each other at any angle. DOM wrapper grew from `w-4 h-4` (16×16) to `w-6 h-6` (24×24) to fit the larger discs; `align-middle` baseline alignment to body x-height is unchanged; wrapper carries `opacity-70` to soften the discs against long streams. The previous `animate-pulse` opacity rhythm also no longer competes visually with the token-by-token landing rate
- Header 左侧两个按钮调换顺序：先 `PanelLeft`（打开侧边栏），再 `SquarePen`（新建会话）；原顺序为「新建会话 → 打开侧边栏」，与常用工具栏「先导航、再新建」的阅读顺序相反
- Reordered the two leading buttons in the header: `PanelLeft` (open sidebar) now comes first, followed by `SquarePen` (new chat). The previous order (new chat → open sidebar) ran against the natural "navigate first, create second" reading order of most toolbars
- 思考块（`ThinkingBlock`）不再随流式状态自动展开：之前 `isOpen = isLive || manualOpen`，agent 还在 thinking 时强制展开、流结束后再自动收起；改为 `isOpen = manualOpen` 后块默认折叠，header 只显示 `Thinking...` / `Thinking Process` 标签与未旋转的 chevron，用户点击 header 才展开；流结束时 `useEffect` 仍会把 `manualOpen` 拉回 `false`，避免用户中途手动展开后留下打开状态
- The thinking block (`ThinkingBlock`) no longer auto-expands with streaming state: it used to compute `isOpen = isLive || manualOpen`, forcing the body open while the model was thinking and auto-collapsing it on completion. It now computes `isOpen = manualOpen`, so the body stays collapsed by default and only the header (label + non-rotated chevron) is visible — clicking the header opens it. The post-stream `useEffect` still resets `manualOpen` to `false` so a mid-stream manual expand does not survive into the finished message
- 聊天字号滑块范围从 14–15 px（步长 0.1）调整为 15–18 px（步长 0.5）：slider 现在停靠 7 档（15 / 15.5 / 16 / 16.5 / 17 / 17.5 / 18），默认值 15 px。已有用户的旧 `local:chatFontSize` 数值会通过 `clampChatFontSize` 截到新范围下限 15；旧的离散 `xs` / `sm` / `md` / `lg` / `xl` 字符串键通过 `LEGACY_FONT_SIZE_PX` 表按相对大小映射到新范围（15 / 15.5 / 16 / 16.5 / 17），保留用户原本「小字号 / 大字号」偏好，不会全部压到下限
- 固定 Prompt / Skill 现在跨会话全局生效：固定后新开对话仍保持固定，取消后所有对话同步取消；文件夹 / 文件 / RAG 固定仍仅限当前会话。状态写入 `local:composerPinnedContexts`（备份以 `settings` 分类纳入），通过 `useStorageItem` 的 `watch` 实时跨侧边栏同步
- 引用（Quote）现在改成跟 mention chip 同款 inline directive 形状：bubble 上方渲染 quote chip（Quote icon + 第一段引用预览截断 48 字符，zinc 中性色调区别于 slash 琥珀 / prompt 紫 / skill 蓝），bubble body 跟 mention / slash 一样只显示用户敲的字（通过 `stripDirectives` 自动剥掉 quote directive 块）；LLM 仍然通过 directive body 收到完整引用文本。引用统一走 directive 解析路径后，原本的「slash + quote 重复」、「mention + quote 重复」两个 bug 在结构上就消失了：quote 块被 `rewriteLastUserMessage` 的 `beforeUser` 段保留为单次出现，`stripDirectives` 再从 bubble body 里干净地剥掉。`rewriteLastUserMessage.DIRECTIVE_OPEN_RE` 也加上 `QUOTE` 进 alternation，避免 quote-only 路径下 bubble 把整段 quote 文本 wipe 掉。`name` 字段塞的是引用预览（不是空串），但 directive 的 `"..."` delimiter 不允许嵌入 `"`，ChatInput 在塞 name 前把 `"` 替换成全角引号 `＂`，wire format 仍可解析、bubble chip 标签读起来也仍像引用。多 chip 合并时 name 字段追加 `· N excerpts` count 后缀（preview 长度按 48 - 后缀长度预算）；count 在 Message.tsx 里拆出来渲染成独立的非 truncate span，确保 preview 被截断时 count 仍 visible。Quote / 附件按钮（pick element、pick region、screenshot、file upload、record）点击后通过 commit 后 `useEffect` 把键盘焦点送回输入框（attachments 数量增长 / quoteChips 数量增长时 trigger，commit 后 DOM 已稳定、不会与其他 focus trap 赛跑；同步路径上的 focus 经常被后续 commit 偷走）；stream 开始时 attachments 被清零（count 减少）不会误触发，mention chip 走 setMentions 不受影响，popover「连选多个」流程保持原有行为
- Chat font-size slider range moved from 14–15 px (step 0.1) to 15–18 px (step 0.5): the slider now snaps to seven discrete stops (15 / 15.5 / 16 / 16.5 / 17 / 17.5 / 18), default 15 px. Existing users with a numeric `local:chatFontSize` in the old range are clamped to the new minimum (15) by `clampChatFontSize`; legacy string keys (`xs` / `sm` / `md` / `lg` / `xl`) are remapped through `LEGACY_FONT_SIZE_PX` to 15 / 15.5 / 16 / 16.5 / 17 in the new range so the user's relative "smaller / larger" intent survives the migration rather than everyone landing on the minimum
- Pinned Prompt / Skill chips are now global across all chats: pinning one in any session keeps it pinned in every other / future session; unpinning drops it everywhere at once. Folder / file / RAG pins remain session-scoped. The pin membership is persisted in `local:composerPinnedContexts` (registered in `BACKUP_REGISTRY` under the `settings` class), and the change syncs in real time across already-open sidepanels via `useStorageItem`'s `watch` subscription
- Quoted text now follows the same inline-directive shape as mention chips: the bubble renders a quote chip above (Quote icon + 48-char preview of the first quote's body, neutral zinc tone distinct from slash-command amber / prompt purple / skill blue), and the bubble body shows only the user's typed words — `stripDirectives` peels the quote directive block off the same way it handles PROMPT / SKILL / COMMAND. The LLM still receives the full quote verbatim via the directive body. Unifying quote with the directive pipeline erases two prior bugs structurally — the "slash + quote duplicates" and "mention + quote duplicates" regressions both stop existing because there is no longer a raw-prefix splice path to diverge from the directive path. `rewriteLastUserMessage.DIRECTIVE_OPEN_RE` now also matches `QUOTE` so quote-only sends preserve the directive on the bubble (without that, `DIRECTIVE_OPEN_RE.test(text)` would miss it and the whole quote block would get clobbered to `displayText`). The directive `name` slot holds the quote preview (not an empty string); since the header regex uses `"..."` with a `[^"]*` capture, ChatInput substitutes raw `"` for the fullwidth variant `＂` so the wire format always parses while the chip label still reads as a quote. When multiple quote chips are stacked, the name field appends a `· N excerpts` count suffix and the preview budget shrinks accordingly. The count is split out in `Message.tsx` into a separate non-truncating span so it stays visible even when the preview is truncated to the chip's `truncate max-w-24` slot. After clicking the Quote / attach buttons (pick element, pick region, screenshot, file upload, record), keyboard focus is restored to the textarea via a post-commit `useEffect` that triggers when the `attachments` array length increases or the `quoteChips` array length increases. The post-commit timing avoids the race against the focus traps and React commits that used to steal a synchronous `textareaRef.focus()` call. The clearing of `attachments` on stream start (count decrease) does not re-trigger, mention chips use `setMentions` so the popover "select multiple" flow is untouched
- `fs_read_file` 单次返回的文本上限从 100 KB 降到 16 KB：原本允许一个工具结果占据约 24k token，反复回声后会把 session 推到 1M context 上限（典型场景：在长 session 中重复读同一份大文件，如 debug log 里 `hugo_and_the_seal_draft.html` 98 KB 被读 6 次，最终 `inputTokens` 877,879 撞到 Gemini 3.7 Flash 的 1,048,576 限制并 400）；新阈值更低，遇到 > 16 KB 的文件会直接附带「Truncated (>16.0 KB). Use start_line/end_line to read specific sections.」提示，让 agent 自然走分块读取。`fsReadFileTool` 的 `description` 同步从 `>100 KB` 改为 `>16 KB` 以让 agent 知道新阈值
- `fs_read_file` per-call text cap lowered from 100 KB to 16 KB. A single tool result could previously occupy ~24k tokens, which repeated tool-result echoes within a long session could push past the 1M-token context limit (typical case: re-reading the same large file across turns — the debug log showed `hugo_and_the_seal_draft.html` at 98 KB read 6 times in one session, pushing `inputTokens` to 877,879 and hitting Gemini 3.7 Flash's 1,048,576 ceiling with a 400). The new threshold forces a truncation notice ("Truncated (>16.0 KB). Use start_line/end_line to read specific sections.") on any file over 16 KB, so the agent naturally falls into chunked reads. `fsReadFileTool`'s `description` was also updated from `>100 KB` to `>16 KB` so the agent sees the new threshold
- 新增 400 上下文溢出自动恢复（3-strikes）：当 provider 返回 `exceeds the maximum number of tokens` / `INVALID_ARGUMENT` / `context length` / `1048576` 等错误时，background 在 `agent_end` 处理器里识别 400 并触发自动恢复：每次从尾部回溯删除 25% 消息直到总大小落到 `contextWindow × 75%` 以内，调用 `agent.continue()` 重跑本轮（in-place mutation，不创建 branch）。最多自动重试 2 次，2 次都失败就广播 IPC `context_overflow` 消息给 UI，sidepanel 渲染一张红边卡片（含模型原始错误摘要 + 重试 / 停止按钮）：**重试**走 50% 截断比例再跑一次，**停止**只重置 `recoveryAttempts` 计数器并广播一个 idle 状态的 `session_state`（保留 live `AgentSession`，不杀进程——user 紧接着发新 prompt 不会走冷加载）。同时每轮 `prompt()` 开始前先做 80% 预检（`maybeCompact` 里新增分支），若估算 token 已超过 `contextWindow × 80%` 就把 `reserveTokens` 收紧强制触发一次普通压缩路径，赶在 400 之前主动把上下文缩回去。预检对小 contextWindow 的自定义模型同样生效：`maybeCompact` 现在把 `reserveTokens` / `keepRecentTokens` 按 `contextWindow × 0.5` / `× 0.4` 上限夹紧后再喂给 `shouldCompact` 与 `findCompactionCutPoint`，否则 pi-agent-core 默认（16384 / 20000）在 8k 上下文里直接吞掉整个 transcript（`cut <= 0` 永远 no-op）且让 80% 预检变成 dead code。计数器在 `prompt()` 写入新 user 消息时重置（turn 边界），也在 `agent_end` 检测到 `stopReason !== 'error'` 时重置（success-path，防止刚被救回来那轮成功结束后下一次 400 直接弹卡）。Stop 路径和重试都正确处理 SW keep-alive：自动重试 `agent.continue()` 前 `acquireKeepAlive()`，catch 里 `releaseKeepAlive()`，避免 SW 在 SW 终止后被遣返的事件触发时已经死了
- Added auto-recovery for 400 context overflow (3-strikes): when the provider returns errors matching `exceeds the maximum number of tokens` / `INVALID_ARGUMENT` / `context length` / `1048576` (and similar context-length signatures), the background detects the overflow in the `agent_end` handler and triggers automatic recovery — each strike walks the message tail backwards, dropping the oldest 25% until the total fits under `contextWindow × 75%` chars, then calls `agent.continue()` to re-run the turn in place (no branch is created, no `entryIds` are touched). Up to 2 auto-retries happen silently; the third failure broadcasts a new IPC `context_overflow` message and the sidepanel renders a destructive-bordered card showing the model's original error summary plus Retry / Stop buttons — **Retry** applies a deeper 50% drop and continues, **Stop** only resets the `recoveryAttempts` counter and broadcasts an idle `session_state` (it deliberately keeps the live `AgentSession` alive, so the user's next prompt doesn't cold-reload a fresh agent). Each new turn also runs an 80% pre-check at the top of `prompt()`: when estimated tokens exceed `contextWindow × 80%`, `maybeCompact` tightens `reserveTokens` to force the regular compaction path and shrink the transcript *before* a 400 is even possible. The pre-check now also works for custom providers with small `contextWindow`: `maybeCompact` clamps `reserveTokens` / `keepRecentTokens` against `contextWindow × 0.5` / `× 0.4` before feeding them to `shouldCompact` and `findCompactionCutPoint` — without the clamp, the pi-agent-core defaults (16384 / 20000) swallow the entire transcript on an 8k-context model (`cut <= 0` is always no-op) AND render the 80% pre-check dead code. The counter is reset on `prompt()` (turn boundary) and on any `agent_end` whose `stopReason !== 'error'` (success-path — a successful turn after a recovered one must not leave the next 400 in strike-3 territory). Both Stop and Retry correctly manage the SW keep-alive: `acquireKeepAlive()` is called before the auto-retry's `agent.continue()`, and `releaseKeepAlive()` runs in the catch so the SW isn't terminated mid-recovery
- `transformContext` 追加硬上限滑动窗口兜底，作为 3-strikes 自动恢复路径的互补层：每轮 LLM 请求前按 `contextWindow − reserveTokens − systemPrompt footprint` 算出消息预算（`reserveTokens` 走 `clampedCompactionReserve`，与 `maybeCompact` 共用一份 per-contextWindow 夹紧值），超出预算时从最早丢弃、沿用 `findCompactionCutPoint` 的 turn-start 对齐以确保 tool_call / toolResult 配对安全（复用 issue #9 修过的同一条切点算法），摘要始终钉在 head 不丢；`state.messages` 仍保留完整历史（无损），此处只是 LLM 边界的视图变换、不写回 state。同一份夹紧公式抽到 `lib/agent/compaction.ts` 的 `clampedCompactionReserve` / `clampedCompactionKeepRecent`，`maybeCompact` 与 `transformContext` 共享同一份夹紧逻辑，避免下次再有人在两处复制粘贴同样的 `Math.min(..., contextWindow × 0.5)`
- `<CompactionDivider>` 升级为玻璃胶囊分隔条：amber 半透明边框 + 柔光阴影（`shadow-[0_0_24px_-12px_rgba(245,158,11,0.4)]`）+ 琥珀色描边的 chevron 展开按钮，整条胶囊都可点击切换（移动端友好）。展开后下方铺一段 dashed 边框 + 等宽字体的摘要披露框（与 `ThinkingBlock` 同款「内部记录」样式），渲染 `compactionSummary.summary` 原文——暂不解析 Markdown，避免引入额外依赖；披露展开状态各自实例独立持有，多段压缩时互不影响。`tokensBefore` 字段终于接回 UI：以小号 tabular-nums 徽章展现在分隔条右侧，格式化为 `−12k`。call site `entrypoints/sidepanel/pages/chat/index.tsx` 现在把 `summary={{ summary, tokensBefore }}` 一并传给 divider

- Added a hard sliding-window cap to `transformContext` as a complement to the 3-strikes auto-recovery path: each LLM call computes a message budget (`contextWindow − reserveTokens − systemPrompt footprint`, with `reserveTokens` pulled from `clampedCompactionReserve` so it shares the same per-contextWindow clamp as `maybeCompact`), and when the post-summary array exceeds that budget the oldest messages are dropped while always snapping the cut to a user turn (reuses `findCompactionCutPoint`'s turn-start alignment, so the tool_call / toolResult pair safety from issue #9 carries over unchanged). Any `compactionSummary` at the head is always pinned and never dropped; `state.messages` still retains the full transcript (lossless) — this is purely an LLM-side view filter, not a state mutation. The per-contextWindow clamp math is extracted into `clampedCompactionReserve` / `clampedCompactionKeepRecent` in `lib/agent/compaction.ts`, shared between `maybeCompact` and `transformContext` so the same `Math.min(..., contextWindow × 0.5)` formula lives in one place
- `<CompactionDivider>` upgraded to a glass-capsule separator: amber translucent border + soft glow shadow (`shadow-[0_0_24px_-12px_rgba(245,158,11,0.4)]`) + amber chevron toggle, the whole capsule is clickable (mobile-friendly). When expanded, a dashed-border + monospace disclosure block renders the raw `compactionSummary.summary` text below — same "internal record" styling as `ThinkingBlock`; markdown rendering is deferred to avoid pulling in a new dependency, the disclosure state is held locally per instance so multiple compressed sections don't interfere with each other. The previously dormant `tokensBefore` field is finally surfaced as a small tabular-nums badge on the right edge of the divider (formatted as `−12k`). The call site at `entrypoints/sidepanel/pages/chat/index.tsx` now passes `summary={{ summary, tokensBefore }}` through to the divider

- `delegate_task` worker 现在硬超时（120 s）并显式提示用户换 model：runner 内 `AbortController` 复合父 signal + 120 s `setTimeout`，到达上限立刻 `agent.abort()` 并主动 `throw new Error('Worker timed out')` 落入 catch（避免 flow 继续走到 `extractJsonOrRaw` → 「JSON parse fail」→ 无意义 retry）；catch 块按真实情况读 `outputFileExists`（`readOutputIfAny` 区分「worker 被 abort 但已写一半文件」与「完全没产出」），handoff JSON 标 `timedOut: true` + `attemptDurationMs` + 真实 `outputFileExists`。单次自愈重试逻辑（`shouldRetry` 谓词 + `assembleHandoff` 的 `retryable` 标记）现在区分两类超时：(a) `timedOut + outputFileExists=true` → `retryable=true`，`shouldRetry` 允许 1 次重试接完（mechanical self-heal，被硬 abort 中途但已落盘的文件值得再跑一次）；(b) `timedOut + outputFileExists=false` → `retryable=false`，fail-fast（同因失败，再撞 120 s 也救不回来）。`shouldRetry` 的 attempts 上限保持 2（单次自愈硬顶）。`DelegationCard` 新增 `timedOut` 状态：badge 用 amber/warning 配色 + Clock 图标（区别于 failed 的 destructive 红 X，让用户走「换 model」而不是「看 handoff_notes」两条不同 action path）；banner 显式提示 "Worker [model name] timed out / API error. Switch to a different model on the Sidebar Team Roster and retry."，带 modelKey 时把 model 名字 inline 进文案；底部新增 "Failed after N s" 时长脚注（failed / timedOut 都显示），帮用户区分「真慢」与「API 卡死」。`delegate-task.ts` 的 `summarizeHandoffJson` 也透传 `timedOut` + `attemptDurationMs` 给 LLM，可见 JSON 截断到 2 KB 后主代理仍能看到 timeout 信号


- `delegate_task` worker now hard-times out at 120 s with an explicit user prompt to switch model: the runner composes an `AbortController` from the parent signal + a 120 s `setTimeout`, fires `agent.abort()` the instant the cap hits, then actively `throw new Error('Worker timed out')` to land in the catch block (instead of falling through to `extractJsonOrRaw` → "JSON parse fail" → useless retry); the catch block reads the real `outputFileExists` via `readOutputIfAny` (distinguishing "worker aborted mid-write but the file exists" from "no output at all") and returns a handoff JSON carrying `timedOut: true` + `attemptDurationMs` + the real `outputFileExists`. The single-pass self-heal retry logic (`shouldRetry` predicate + `assembleHandoff`'s `retryable` flag) now distinguishes two timeout classes: (a) `timedOut + outputFileExists=true` → `retryable=true`, and `shouldRetry` allows 1 retry to finish the work (mechanical self-heal — a hard-aborted worker that already landed a file is worth one more pass); (b) `timedOut + outputFileExists=false` → `retryable=false`, fail-fast (same-cause failure — burning another 120 s budget won't help). `shouldRetry`'s attempts hard-cap stays at 2 (single self-heal ceiling). `DelegationCard` gains a new `timedOut` status: amber/warning badge with a Clock icon (distinct from `failed`'s destructive red X so the user takes the "switch model" path rather than the "read handoff_notes" path); an amber banner reads "Worker [model name] timed out / API error. Switch to a different model on the Sidebar Team Roster and retry.", with the model name interpolated inline when `modelKey` is present. A "Failed after N s" duration footer now appears on both `failed` and `timedOut` cards so the user can tell "genuinely slow" from "API-stuck". `summarizeHandoffJson` in `delegate-task.ts` also passes `timedOut` + `attemptDurationMs` through to the LLM-visible JSON, so even after the 2 KB summary cap truncates `handoff_notes` the main agent still sees the timeout signal

- `DelegationCard` 新增 3 项实时视觉反馈，让 `status='running'` 时用户能「看见」worker 在跑、知道跑了多久、知道它在做什么：(1) **Live elapsed timer** —— header badge 显示 "Running… (Xs / 120s)" 倒计时，计时起点 `attemptStartedAt` 由 caller 通过 `useRef<Map<string, number>>` 注入并按 `tc.id` 缓存（避免 React re-mount 把 timer 归零，也避免在 render body 直接调 `Date.now()` 触发每次重渲染都重算），`timeoutMs` prop 从 `lib/agent/worker-roles` 的 `WORKER_TIMEOUT_MS` 同步（UI 显示上限与 runner abort 阈值 single source of truth）；(2) **Pulse / shimmer 动画** —— 整卡 border 转 `warning/50` + 左侧 `border-l-2 border-l-warning` + `animate-pulse`（2s opacity 1 → 0.5 → 1 循环），角色图标同时改 `animate-spin`，状态转出 running 后全部退场（保留「card 还在动 = 还在跑」的视觉信号）；(3) **Role action badge** —— running 时 header 额外挂一个 outline pill，4 个 role 各自的中英文 i18n 标签（`chat.delegation.action.content_writer="writing"` / `frontend_coder="coding"` / `reviewer="reviewing"` / `researcher="researching"`，对应 zh_CN「撰写中 / 编码中 / 审查中 / 研究中」、zh_TW「撰寫中 / 編碼中 / 審查中 / 研究中」），让用户一眼看出 worker 在做什么而不只是「在跑」。`WORKER_TIMEOUT_MS` 从 `worker-runner.ts` 的 private const 移到 `lib/agent/worker-roles.ts` 顶层 export（background runner 与 sidepanel UI 都从同一处 import，runner 不必再被 UI 跨 entrypoint 拖入 bundle 路径）
- `DelegationCard` now ships 3 live visual feedback features so users can *see* that a worker is running, for how long, and what it's doing while `status='running'`: (1) **live elapsed timer** — the header badge shows "Running… (Xs / 120s)" countdown. The `attemptStartedAt` timestamp is injected by the caller via a `useRef<Map<string, number>>` keyed by `tc.id` (avoids React re-mount resetting the timer to 0 and avoids calling `Date.now()` in the render body, which would re-fire on every render and re-compute the elapsed value). The `timeoutMs` prop is sourced from `WORKER_TIMEOUT_MS` in `lib/agent/worker-roles` so the UI's displayed cap and the runner's abort threshold stay a single source of truth; (2) **pulse / shimmer animation** — the whole card border switches to `warning/50` with a left `border-l-2 border-l-warning` accent and `animate-pulse` (2 s opacity 1 → 0.5 → 1 cycle), the role icon also gets `animate-spin`, and every effect retires the moment the attempt resolves (so a still-animating card unambiguously means "still running"); (3) **role action badge** — while running, an extra outline pill appears in the header per role (i18n: `chat.delegation.action.content_writer="writing"` / `frontend_coder="coding"` / `reviewer="reviewing"` / `researcher="researching"`, with zh_CN "撰写中 / 编码中 / 审查中 / 研究中" and zh_TW "撰寫中 / 編碼中 / 審查中 / 研究中"), so the user sees *what* the worker is doing, not just that it's running. `WORKER_TIMEOUT_MS` moved from `worker-runner.ts`'s private const to a top-level export in `lib/agent/worker-roles.ts` (background runner and sidepanel UI both import from the same place, and the sidepanel no longer needs to cross an entrypoint boundary — pulling the runner's factory + tool modules — into its bundle path)

- 文本文件 attachment 上限从 100 KB 提升至 1 MB：原本 composer 拖入 `.txt` / `.md` / `.ts` 等文本文件 > 100 KB 会被直接 toast 拒收，提到 1 MB 后绝大多数代码文件、长 log、单篇 markdown 都能整篇附上（≈ 285k token，按 3.5 char/token 算），不至于一开始就要求 user 走 `fs_read_file` 分块。Mention chip（prompt / skill / vfs-file）与 `delegate_task` worker skill body 仍保持 100 KB，因为它们在 pin 时每次 send 都跟着走，预算比一次性 attachment 敏感；`fs_read_file` 工具结果保持 16 KB 不变（之前的改动已经把这个路径压低，详见上文 `fs_read_file` 那条）
- Text file attachment cap raised from 100 KB to 1 MB: previously the composer hard-rejected any text file (`.txt` / `.md` / `.ts` / etc.) over 100 KB, which left most code files, long logs, and mid-sized markdown documents unreadable as attachments and forced the user into the chunked `fs_read_file` path on day one. At 1 MB (≈ 285k tokens at 3.5 chars/token) the common cases now ship in full. Mention chips (prompt / skill / vfs-file) and the `delegate_task` worker skill body stay at 100 KB — they ride along on every send when pinned, so the budget stays tight. The `fs_read_file` tool result cap (16 KB) is unchanged — see the earlier `fs_read_file` entry in this section for the rationale behind that separate, much tighter bound

- Worker Team 并行委派（`delegate_task` 新增 `tasks: [...]` 批量形态，最多 4 个独立 task 同跑）：schema 新增可选 `tasks: DelegateTaskItem[]`（与单 task 形态互斥——同时传 `tasks` + `task`/`role` 会被 tool 层报错退回，避免 LLM 双路径歧义），single-task caller 完全向后兼容。新增 `runBatchWorker` runner：外层 pre-flight（abort signal / role validate / model resolve）走 `Promise.all` 并行，单 item 解析失败不波及其他；per-item prompt composition + `runWorkerAttempt` 走 `Promise.allSettled`，item timer fire 只 abort 该 item，1 parent abort signal 透传所有 item。`WorkerHandoff` 新增可选 `batch?: readonly WorkerHandoff[]` + `batchSummary: { total, succeeded, failed, partial, cancelled }` 预聚合字段，外层 `status` 决策表（all success → success；≥1 success + ≥1 fail → partial；all fail → failed）与 `aggregateBatchStatus` UI helper 严格 1:1 镜像避免漂移。3 道安全护栏按 item 独立计：3-tier timer (TTFT 120s / Idle 180s / per-role ceiling)、`MAX_OUTPUT_CONTENT_CHARS = 50_000` 不乘 N、tool-layer 2 KB cap per-item 不乘 N（最坏 batch tool result ≈ 16 KB）。Batch 内只跑**无依赖**的独立 task（content_writer + frontend_coder + reviewer 读同一文件这种流水线场景需顺序调用两次，PREAMBLE + tool description 双侧声明约束，避免 worker B 在 worker A 写完前 fs_read 落空触发误重试）
- Worker Team parallel dispatch (`delegate_task` gains a `tasks: [...]` batch shape, up to 4 independent tasks in parallel). The schema adds an optional `tasks: DelegateTaskItem[]` field that **mutually excludes** the single-task `task`/`role` shape — passing both raises a tool-layer error so the LLM can't take two divergent paths; single-task callers are fully back-compat. New `runBatchWorker` runner: outer pre-flight (abort signal / role validation / model resolve) fans out via `Promise.all`, a single item resolve failure doesn't poison siblings; per-item prompt composition + `runWorkerAttempt` go through `Promise.allSettled`, an item-level timer fires `abort()` only on that item, one parent `abortSignal` is plumbed to every item. `WorkerHandoff` gains additive optional `batch?: readonly WorkerHandoff[]` and `batchSummary: { total, succeeded, failed, partial, cancelled }` pre-aggregated fields. Outer `status` decision table (all success → success; ≥1 success + ≥1 fail → partial; all fail → failed) is mirrored 1:1 in the UI's `aggregateBatchStatus` helper so the two never drift. The three safety rails stay per-item: 3-tier timer (TTFT 120 s / Idle 180 s / per-role ceiling), `MAX_OUTPUT_CONTENT_CHARS = 50_000` does NOT multiply by N, tool-layer 2 KB cap stays per-item (worst-case batch tool result ≈ 16 KB). Batch is restricted to **independent** tasks with no file dependencies (a content_writer → frontend_coder → reviewer pipeline reading the same file must run as two sequential single-task calls, not one batch — this is called out in both the PREAMBLE and the tool description to prevent worker B's `fs_read_file` racing worker A's write and triggering spurious retries)
- `DelegationCard` 新增 batch container 形态：当 main agent 用 `tasks: [...]` 形态委派时，UI 渲染 outer `DelegationCard`（header 显示 coarse 计数 `"Batch · N tasks — X succeeded, Y failed"` + batch status badge），body 渲染 N 个内层 `DelegationCardItem`（与单 task 形态视觉一致，per-item status badge / elapsed timer / output 链接互相独立——一个 item 卡死不影响其他 item 渲染 / 点击 / 折叠）。Running 状态下 outer + 内层同时显示 pulse 动画 + amber border（coarse「整个 batch 还在跑」信号）。Chat parser `parseBatchHandoffText` 走 line-by-line 解析 runner 的 `renderBatchToolResult` 输出（`[batch]` / `[batch-summary]` / `[item N]` / 缩进 `summary:` / `error:` / `--- output_content (...) ---`），把每个 item 的 task 描述从原始 `tc.arguments.tasks[i].task` 注入（parser 拿不到这层语义）
- `DelegationCard` now renders a batch container shape when the main agent delegates via `tasks: [...]`: outer `DelegationCard` shows a coarse count header ("Batch · N tasks — X succeeded, Y failed") plus a batch-level status badge, body renders N inner `DelegationCardItem`s (visually identical to single-task cards — each item's status badge / elapsed timer / output link stays independent, so one item stalling never blocks the others' render / click / fold). While running, both the outer and inner cards share the pulse animation + amber border (coarse "the whole batch is still alive" signal). The chat parser `parseBatchHandoffText` parses `renderBatchToolResult`'s output line-by-line (`[batch]` / `[batch-summary]` / `[item N]` / indented `summary:` / `error:` / `--- output_content (...) ---`); the per-item `task` description is injected from the original `tc.arguments.tasks[i].task` (the parser cannot recover that semantic layer from the rendered text alone)

- Reviewer role 内嵌 15 条静态 audit checklist（不再走外部 `cl-review-checklist` skill）：把 15 个 pattern（`localStorage` / `sessionStorage` / `document.cookie` / `indexedDB` / `<script src>` https-only / `<link href>` https-only / `:root` color tokens / `prefers-color-scheme` / `grid | flex` / viewport meta / `<title>` / `<img alt>` / `prefers-reduced-motion` / `on*= 内联事件处理器` / `overflow-x: auto`）直接 inline 到 reviewer 的 `systemPrompt`，按 9 个 fail + 6 个 warn 标注（item 14 inline event handlers 从 fail 降为 warn —— 不再误判传统 `onclick` / `oninput` HTML），每条给一行简短 pattern。Budget：reviewer `systemPrompt` cap 从 500 char 单独放宽到 2 KB（其他 3 个 role 仍守 500 char），测试在 `lib/agent/worker-roles.test.ts` 用 regex 同时 pin 住「15 个 marker + fail/warn 比例 + ≤ 2 KB cap」三重契约。Inline 比走 skill 省一次 hydration round-trip + skill-body read，LLM 第一次 system prompt 就看到 15 个 pattern name，audit 准确率高于动态加载
- Reviewer role now inlines a 15-item static audit checklist (no longer loaded via the external `cl-review-checklist` skill). All 15 patterns (`localStorage` / `sessionStorage` / `document.cookie` / `indexedDB` / `<script src>` https-only / `<link href>` https-only / `:root` color tokens / `prefers-color-scheme` / `grid | flex` / viewport meta / `<title>` / `<img alt>` / `prefers-reduced-motion` / `on*= inline event handlers` / `overflow-x: auto`) are written directly into the reviewer's `systemPrompt`, labeled with 9 fail + 6 warn defaults (item 14 inline event handlers downgraded from fail to warn so traditional `onclick` / `oninput` HTML doesn't get penalized), one short pattern line per item. Budget: reviewer `systemPrompt` cap relaxed from 500 chars to 2 KB for this role alone (the other 3 roles keep their 500-char cap). Tests in `lib/agent/worker-roles.test.ts` pin the triple contract via regex — 15 numbered markers + fail/warn ratio + ≤ 2 KB cap. Inlining beats skill loading because it removes the VFS hydration round-trip + skill-body read, so the LLM sees all 15 pattern names on its first system prompt turn and audit accuracy climbs versus dynamic load

- Reviewer handoff 走结构化 `REVIEWER_HANDOFF_SCHEMA` 契约：新增 `lib/agent/schema-validate.ts` 暴露 `REVIEWER_HANDOFF_SCHEMA`（TypeBox JSON Schema literal，要求 `status` / `output_file` / `summary` / `handoff_notes` / `checklist` 5 字段必填）+ `REVIEWER_CHECKLIST_ITEM_IDS` 15 个 stable kebab-case id 常量；`delegate_task` 在 caller 没显式传 `expected_schema` 但 role 是 `reviewer` 时自动注入这套 schema（caller-wins：显式传空 / 自定义 schema 都以 caller 为准），schema fail 走既有单次自愈重试（重试 prompt 末尾带 `<retry-feedback>` 列出具体字段错处）。Handoff JSON 现在带结构化 `checklist: [{ item, status: 'pass'|'fail'|'warn', evidence }]`（schema 强制 `minItems: 1`，reviewer 必须至少 emit 一条不能 silent-pass；`evidence ≤ 200 chars`、`item` 走开放 `^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$` kebab pattern 方便未来加 item），runner 在 `assembleHandoff` 用窄 type guard 透传脏数据 silently drop 而不抛错（schema 漏过的脏数据不会让 UI crash）。`delegate-task.ts` 的 `summarizeHandoffJson` + `renderBatchToolResult` 都把 `checklist_summary: { pass, warn, fail }` 聚合并透传到 LLM 可见的 JSON（主代理一眼看见 reviewer audit 计数，不用从 prose summary 里 grep 'fail' / 'warn' 字面）
- Reviewer handoff now goes through a structured `REVIEWER_HANDOFF_SCHEMA` contract. New `lib/agent/schema-validate.ts` exposes `REVIEWER_HANDOFF_SCHEMA` (a TypeBox JSON Schema literal requiring `status` / `output_file` / `summary` / `handoff_notes` / `checklist` as the 5 required fields) and `REVIEWER_CHECKLIST_ITEM_IDS`, a 15-id stable kebab-case constant. `delegate_task` auto-injects this schema for `role: 'reviewer'` calls when the caller doesn't pass `expected_schema` (caller wins: passing explicit empty / custom schema overrides the auto-inject). Schema fail still rides the existing single-pass self-heal retry (the retry prompt appends a `<retry-feedback>` block listing the exact field error). The handoff JSON now carries a structured `checklist: [{ item, status: 'pass'|'fail'|'warn', evidence }]` — schema forces `minItems: 1` so the reviewer must emit at least one row (no silent pass); `evidence ≤ 200 chars`, `item` uses the open `^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$` kebab pattern so future items need no schema change. The runner's `assembleHandoff` passes the field through with a narrow type guard — dirty data silently drops rather than throws (a schema leak never crashes the UI). `summarizeHandoffJson` and `renderBatchToolResult` in `delegate-task.ts` aggregate `checklist_summary: { pass, warn, fail }` and surface it in the LLM-visible JSON (the main agent sees the audit counts at a glance instead of grepping 'fail' / 'warn' from prose summaries)

- Reviewer audit 在 `DelegationCard` 渲染 3 列迷你表：当 reviewer handoff 含 `checklist` 数组时（schema-validated），inner card 在 Summary 之下 / HandoffNotes 之上挂一张 mini-table——header 行显示 "Audit" + 3 个 bucket 计数（✓ pass / ⚠ warn / ✗ fail），per-row：kebab id（monospace）/ status icon（lucide `Check` / `AlertTriangle` / `X`）/ evidence 截 80 chars（完整文本挂 `title` 属性给 hover / screen reader）。`props.checklist?: readonly ChecklistItem[]` 走窄 type（`string` 而非 enum-typed id）保留 schema 开放 pattern 自由度——`summarizeChecklist` 是驱动 header 计数的 pure helper，undefined / 空数组时 caller 不渲染表格（非 reviewer role / 老 reviewer 没 emit 都不误报）。i18n：3 语 locale 新增 `chat.delegation.reviewer.{auditHeader, statusPass, statusWarn, statusFail}`（`Audit` / `审查清单` / `審查清單`），screen reader 念「pass / warn / fail」而不是 lucide icon 名。Chat parser 加 `parseChecklistFromObj` 把 handoff JSON 的 `checklist` 字段窄化成 UI 类型
- Reviewer audit now renders as a 3-column mini-table in `DelegationCard`. When a reviewer's handoff carries a `checklist` array (schema-validated), the inner card mounts a mini-table between Summary and Handoff Notes — the header row shows "Audit" plus three bucket counts (✓ pass / ⚠ warn / ✗ fail), and each row is `kebab-id` (monospace) / status icon (lucide `Check` / `AlertTriangle` / `X`) / evidence truncated to 80 chars (full text on the `title` attribute for hover / screen-reader). The `props.checklist?: readonly ChecklistItem[]` shape stays narrow (`string` rather than enum-typed id) so the schema's open-pattern freedom is preserved — `summarizeChecklist` is the pure helper that drives the header counts, undefined / empty array means the caller doesn't render the table at all (so non-reviewer roles and older reviewers that didn't emit don't get a misleading empty card). i18n: all 3 locales gain `chat.delegation.reviewer.{auditHeader, statusPass, statusWarn, statusFail}` (`Audit` / `审查清单` / `審查清單`); screen readers announce "pass / warn / fail" instead of the lucide icon names. The chat parser adds `parseChecklistFromObj` to narrow the handoff JSON's `checklist` field into the UI type
- Added a one-line batching hint to the agent system prompt: when several tool calls are independent of each other (parallel reads of multiple files, parallel URL checks, unrelated `fs_*` operations), issue them in the same assistant turn rather than one at a time — the runtime runs independent calls in parallel via `Promise.all`. The hint also makes the data-dependency rule explicit: only batch truly independent calls. Runtime is unchanged (`pi-agent-core@0.84.2` already defaults to `toolExecution: "parallel"` and bundles all tool results into a single follow-up request — see audit); the prompt just nudges more conservative models (e.g. antigravity / gemini-3.8-flash via the 9router-go gateway, which only ever emits one tool call per assistant turn) to take advantage of the existing parallel path

### 修复 / Fixed

- 修复长会话在上下文写满后彻底卡死、已完成的工作全部白费的问题：AI 连续调用工具收集内容时，现在会在轮次中途自动压缩上下文，而不是只在用户发下一条消息前才压；压缩摘要生成失败时也不再原样重发整段超长上下文（那必然再次报错），改为丢弃最早的历史并在对话里标出；确实压不动时 AI 会主动暂停并说明原因，而不是反复报错。另外修正了中文内容的上下文用量估算——此前按英文经验值估算，中文被低估 2.7–4 倍，导致压缩触发过晚 ([#72](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/72))
- 修复 Skill 的 `bgFetch:<match-pattern>` 域名权限可被 HTTP 重定向绕过的问题：受限请求现在会在网络层拒绝重定向，避免请求、凭据或响应流向声明范围外的地址；裸 `bgFetch` 仍保留原有的自动跟随行为 ([#78](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/78))
- 修复技能授权卡片等待确认期间，技能权限声明或脚本被修改后仍会执行新内容的问题：授权现在绑定当时的权限与脚本快照，内容变化会拒绝执行并要求重新授权；「始终允许」也只保存卡片中实际展示的权限 ([#80](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/80))

- Fixed long chats wedging permanently once the context filled up, losing all the work already done. While the AI is making back-to-back tool calls, the context is now compacted mid-turn instead of only before the user's next message; when summarization fails, the over-long context is no longer resent verbatim (which could only fail again) — the earliest history is dropped and marked in the transcript instead; and when nothing can be compacted any further the AI pauses itself and explains why instead of failing over and over. Context-usage estimates for Chinese text were also corrected — they previously used an English-tuned heuristic that underestimated Chinese by 2.7–4×, so compaction triggered far too late ([#72](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/72))
- Fixed an HTTP redirect bypass in Skill `bgFetch:<match-pattern>` URL permissions. Scoped requests now reject redirects at the network layer so requests, credentials and responses cannot escape the declared URL scope; unrestricted `bgFetch` keeps its existing automatic-follow behavior ([#78](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/78))
- Fixed a Skill executing modified permissions or script content when it changed while its permission card was awaiting confirmation. Authorization is now bound to the permissions and script snapshot shown at approval time; changed content is rejected and must be authorized again, and “Always allow” persists only the permissions actually displayed ([#80](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/80))

- 修復在 Telegram session 的頁頭切換模型 / 思考檔「選了幾秒後又跳回」：選擇只寫全局默認 + 本地草稿，從不落會話行，而 Telegram 側 agent 以會話行為模型來源——下一次 session_state 廣播就把它蓋回舊值。現在頁頭選擇立即落會話行（Telegram 下一條訊息即用新模型，活 agent 由 `refreshSessionConfig` 就地對齊、不等冷啟動），並且會話級選擇與全局默認徹底獨立（全局只在新建對話時作為種子；既有會話的選擇不再回寫全局）；順帶在 Telegram session 隱藏了對其無意義的 Worker Team chip 與思考檔選擇器（模型選擇器保留，作為 session 級選擇入口）
- Fixed the model / thinking-level picker in a Telegram session snapping back seconds after a selection: the pick only wrote the global default + a local draft, never the session row, while the Telegram-side agent takes its model from the session row — the next session_state broadcast reverted it. The header selection now persists to the session immediately (the next Telegram message uses it; the warm agent is re-aligned in place via `refreshSessionConfig` instead of waiting for a cold start), and session-level selection is fully independent from the global default (the global only seeds brand-new chats; existing-session picks no longer write back to it); the Worker Team chip and the thinking-level selector, which mean nothing to a Telegram session, are now hidden there (the model picker stays as the session-level selection entry)
- 修复 mid-stream stream_ops 偶发与 session_state 重复应用导致回复开头文本被叠加（"ABCC"）的问题：v1.7.0 改成 80ms 窗口合并 delta 后，commit `0822474` 用 210ms 时长豁免窗挡住 subscribe 期间可能重复的尾帧，但窗长 `FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS * 2 + 50` 是固定常量，TTFB 慢 / 模型思考停顿 / MV3 SW timer 漂移时尾帧会落在 210ms 之外，豁免失效、trailing 帧盖在 `session_state` 快照上 → UI 出现 "Dưới đây là câu trả lời chiDưới đây là câu trả lời chi tiết..." 之类的首字翻倍。改用每端口流式 cursor：producer 在每条 `tail_append` 上 stamp `messageId` / `startOffset` / `endOffset`（endOffset = 应用 delta 后的字段长度，startOffset = endOffset - delta.length），BG 在 `viewers.ts` 维护 `WeakMap<Port, Map<CursorKey, number>>`（CursorKey = `${messageId}/${blockIndex}/${field}`），按 `(messageId, blockIndex, field, endOffset)` 与 port cursor 比较：`endOffset ≤ cursor` 整段丢（已被快照覆盖）、`startOffset < cursor < endOffset` 部分重叠则 `delta.slice(cursor - startOffset)` 裁前缀后再下发、`startOffset ≥ cursor` 原样下发。`session_state` / `tail_replace` / `message_end` 三种快照路径都走新 helper `sendSessionStateToAllViewers` / `sendSessionStateToPort`（替换了原本 12 处分散的 `broadcastToViewers({type:'session_state'…})` 与 `post(port, {type:'session_state'…})`），保证「快照 ⟹ cursor」永不被新代码路径绕过。`messageId` 进 cursor key；当前线上 session-manager 桥接只转发 pi-agent-core 的 `message_update` 事件（`start` AssistantMessageEvent 被 pi 转成 AgentEvent `message_start`，并不在这条 path 上），加上每次 `message_end` / `agent_end` 都会 `dropStreamBroadcast` 把 `messageId` 计数器抹掉重新创建，因此 production `messageId` 始终保持初始值 0；跨轮隔离实际上是靠 `text_start` / `thinking_start` / `toolcall_start` 这三个同样走 `message_update` 路径的结构事件触发的 `tail_replace` reseed——每条新块把 fresh partial 的字段长度（多数为 0）写回 cursor，覆盖上一轮同 key。`messageId` 字段保留在 cursor key 中是面向未来桥接形态演化的 zero-cost 防御位。`tail_replace` 不带 `messageId` 时退化为 `startOffset=0` 全量 reseed（兼容历史快照）。不再依赖固定时长窗：trailing 帧无论迟到多久，只要内容已被快照覆盖就被 cursor 静默丢弃。共删除 `suppressStreamOpsFor` / `suppressedUntil`（commit `0822474` 的 210ms 豁免表）与 12 处分散快照路径，新增 `entrypoints/background/chat/viewers.test.ts`（cursor 过滤 / partial overlap / inter-turn reset / tail_replace reseed / sendSessionStateToPort seed / 非 stream_ops 帧绕过 6 条用例）、`stream-broadcast.test.ts` 新增 `'start' bump messageId` 与 `text_delta 端到端 offsets` 2 条 producer 契约测试、`client-handlers.test.ts` 改写为 cursor 端到端回归（断言 trailing 帧在任意延迟下只要 endOffset ≤ cursor 必丢）、`lib/agent/stream-replica.test.ts` 给所有 `tail_append` 字面量补齐 `messageId` / `startOffset` / `endOffset` 三字段（其性质测试新增 offset 跟踪 helper）
- Fixed occasional "ABCC" text duplication at the start of streaming responses where a mid-stream `stream_ops` frame landed on top of the `session_state` snapshot. v1.7.0's switch to 80ms-window delta coalescing exposed a race that commit `0822474` papered over with a 210ms suppression window on the new viewer port (`FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS * 2 + 50`); slow TTFB, model thinking pauses, or MV3 service-worker timer drift could push the trailing frame past that fixed window, breaking the suppression and causing the leading chunk to be applied twice — yielding the "Dưới đây là câu trả lời chiDưới đây là câu trả lời chi tiết..." pattern.
