Replaced the time-based window with a content-based cursor: producer stamps every `tail_append` with `messageId` / `startOffset` / `endOffset` (endOffset = field length after applying the delta; startOffset = endOffset − delta.length); `viewers.ts` maintains `WeakMap<Port, Map<CursorKey, number>>` where `CursorKey = ${messageId}/${blockIndex}/${field}`. For each port, BG compares `tail_append` against the cursor: `endOffset ≤ cursor` drops the whole op (snapshot already covers it), `startOffset < cursor < endOffset` trims `delta.slice(cursor − startOffset)` and posts the new suffix only, `startOffset ≥ cursor` passes through unchanged. The three snapshot paths (`session_state`, `tail_replace`, `message_end`) all funnel through new helpers `sendSessionStateToAllViewers` / `sendSessionStateToPort` in `viewers.ts`, replacing the 12 scattered `broadcastToViewers({type:'session_state'…})` and `post(port, {type:'session_state'…})` sites — the "snapshot ⟹ cursor" invariant is now locally enforced and future snapshot paths can't bypass it. `messageId` is part of the cursor key; in the current bridge only pi-agent-core's `message_update` events are forwarded to `queueStreamEvent` (the `start` AssistantMessageEvent is converted by pi into a top-level `message_start` AgentEvent that does not enter that path), and every `message_end` / `agent_end` calls `dropStreamBroadcast` which deletes and re-creates the `messageId` counter — so in production `messageId` stays at its initial value of 0 for the entire state lifetime. Cross-turn isolation is actually provided by `text_start` / `thinking_start` / `toolcall_start` (which DO travel through `message_update`) triggering a `tail_replace` reseed on each new block start — the fresh partial's field length (typically 0) overwrites the prior turn's cursor entry under the same key. The `messageId` field is retained in the cursor key as a zero-cost defensive slot for possible future bridge evolution. `tail_replace` without `messageId` falls back to a full reseed with `startOffset=0` per block (safe default for historical snapshots). The 210ms `suppressStreamOpsFor` / `suppressedUntil` table (commit `0822474`) is removed; timing is irrelevant now — any trailing frame whose content is already in the port's snapshot is dropped regardless of how late it arrives. New `entrypoints/background/chat/viewers.test.ts` covers cursor filter / partial overlap / inter-turn reset / `tail_replace` reseed / `sendSessionStateToPort` seed / non-`stream_ops` bypass (6 cases); `stream-broadcast.test.ts` gains 2 producer-contract tests (`'start' bumps messageId`, `text_delta end-to-end offsets`); `client-handlers.test.ts` is rewritten as a cursor end-to-end regression (asserts that a trailing frame at any delay is dropped when `endOffset ≤ cursor`); `lib/agent/stream-replica.test.ts` adds `messageId` / `startOffset` / `endOffset` to every `tail_append` literal (property test gets offset-tracking helpers)
- 自定义 OpenAI-compatible provider 的 Base URL 指向 `http://localhost:<port>` 时发请求被浏览器拦截：`wxt.config.ts` 中 sandbox 与 extension_pages 的 `connect-src` 都只放行 `'self' https: wss: data: blob:`，没有 `http:` 也没有 loopback；任何发到本地代理（如 9router、LM Studio、Ollama）都会被报「Refused to connect because it violates the document's Content Security Policy」。在两条 `connect-src` 末尾追加 `http://localhost:* http://127.0.0.1:*`，仅放行 loopback，不扩大到任意 `http://*`；`http://api.vilao.ai/v1` 等公网 https 端点行为不变
- Fixed custom OpenAI-compatible providers whose Base URL points at `http://localhost:<port>` (e.g. 9router, LM Studio, Ollama) failing with "Refused to connect because it violates the document's Content Security Policy": the `connect-src` directives in both `sandbox` and `extension_pages` policies in `wxt.config.ts` only allowed `'self' https: wss: data: blob:` — no `http:` and no loopback. Appended `http://localhost:* http://127.0.0.1:*` to both `connect-src` directives; the widening is loopback-only (not `http://*`), so public https endpoints like `https://api.vilao.ai/v1` are unaffected
- 修复使用 Exa（或其他 tool）导致聊天气泡上的 Edit 按钮在后续对话中永久消失的 bug：pi-agent-core 的 `Durable payload contains undefined` 断言会递归拦截含有 `undefined` 字段的树操作（如 tool_result 块内某些可选状态）；此时 `syncTail` 会静默失败并卡住 `committedCount` 水位线，后续发送的 user 消息无法落树（无 `entryId`），UI 因此隐藏了编辑入口。现在在消息存盘前通过新增的一道 `omitUndefinedFields` 清理所有内嵌块，确保 `syncTail` 顺畅入库
- 聊天区消息字号原本写死 `text-[0.9rem]`，与 settings 里的字号 slider 失联（`useChatFontSize` 写入 `--chat-font-size`，但消息容器不消费它）；UserMessageBubble 和 AgentMessage 改成 `text-[length:var(--chat-font-size)]`，字号现在随用户偏好变化（与 MarkdownRenderer / ChatInput 已有的用法对齐）
- MarkdownRenderer 中 `<p>` 和 `<li>` 强制 `font-normal` 把外层 AgentMessage 的 `font-medium` 覆盖掉，AI 回复读起来仍像 400；移除该 override，让正文继承父级 medium weight
- Crop region 模式下把选择框拖出视口后再松手，只截到视口内可见那一块（滚下去的部分全丢）：picker 在 mousedown / move / up 里给扩展侧的是文档坐标的 rect，但 `chrome.tabs.captureVisibleTab` 只能拍视口，超出视口的部分被 offscreen canvas 在 `img.width / height` 处 clamp 掉，最终得到的就是「带 visible 字样的一块图」；改成 scroll-and-stitch：扩展侧按视口高度把 rect 切成 N 条，按顺序在 tab 里 `window.scrollTo` 到每条的文档 y、`captureVisibleTab` 拍当前视口、`crop-image` 裁出对应矩形，再通过新的 `composite-vertical` IPC 在 offscreen 里纵向粘成一张图，最后把 tab 滚回原位；这样既不依赖 `captureBeyondViewport`（Chrome 在 clip 越过 `documentElement.scrollHeight` 时会用当前视口重复填充缺失区域，导致截图里首屏被叠 2-3 次），也不需要 CDP attach/detach，调试器 banner 不会出现
- Crop region 模式下把拖出的矩形 clamp 到 `document.documentElement.scrollWidth/scrollHeight` 内再送给截图管线：scroll-and-stitch 的每条 strip 的 CSS 起点和高度都不能超过文档边界，否则 `window.scrollTo` 会顶到 maxScrollY、`captureVisibleTab` 拿到的是边界外的视口内容
- Crop region 模式下把鼠标贴近视口边缘时的自动滚动太慢（之前是固定 ±10px / RAF，约 600 px/s，900 高的视口得拖 1.5 秒才能滚一屏）：EDGE 缓冲带从 30 加宽到 50，并把单帧位移从常数改成随深度线性 ramp（贴边时 MAX 60 px/帧 ≈ 3600 px/s，刚进缓冲带时 MIN 14 px/帧 ≈ 840 px/s），既能甩手快速滚到目标段落，也能控制一下慢慢选
- Crop region picker 的 `edgeSpeed` 在视口死区（鼠标不在任何边缘缓冲带内）仍然返回非零值——`ramp = MIN + (MAX-MIN)*factor` 永远 ≥ MIN，所以 `if (ramp === 0) return 0` 永远不成立，结果死区里以约 840 px/s 持续漂向视口右下；改成先看 `factor === 0`（真死区）就立即返回 0，否则才走 ramp + sign 计算
- Crop region picker 的 `edgeSpeed` 在所有边缘都返回正数，导致左/上边缘自动滚根本滚不动（顶/左缓冲带里的鼠标被「钉死」在该位置）：`window.scrollBy(dx, dy)` 的正方向是下/右，左/上边缘必须返回负值才行。现在分两个分支分别设 `sign = -1`（顶/左）和 `+1`（底/右），最后 `return sign * Math.min(MAX, ramp)`
- Crop region picker 撞上 Chrome 的 `MAX_CAPTURE_VISIBLE_TAB_CALLS_PER_SECOND` quota（同一 tab 限速 2 次/秒）：scroll-and-stitch 每条 strip 调一次 `chrome.tabs.captureVisibleTab`，3+ 条的高 rect 不节流就会爆 quota。在循环开头加 550ms 最小间隔节流（`lastCaptureAt > 0` 时若 `Date.now() - lastCaptureAt < 550` 则 await 到满再继续），第一条不延迟；5 条 strip 现在大约 5-6 秒完成
- Crop region picker 的 `chrome.tabs.captureVisibleTab(tabId, ...)` 之前传错了参数：MV3 signature 是 `(windowId?, options?)` 不是 `(tabId?, options?)`，传 tabId 进去 Chrome 当成 windowId 去找一个不存在的 window → silent failure → 弹「Failed to capture region」。改成不传第一个参数（picker 在用户当前 active tab 里，默认就是 current window 的 active tab）
- Crop region picker 出错时 toast 现在直接显示根因：把 picker 上抛的 `message`（限 200 字）放进 toast 的 `description`，用户能看到具体原因——「No strips produced for region capture」「Crop failed: ...」之类——而不是笼统的「Failed to capture region」；完整信息仍落到 console 方便 debug
- AI 回复气泡在 LLM 流式输出结束时仍会小抖一下（cursor ~16px）：之前 `isStreaming: true → false` 那一帧，光标 `<span>`（16px 高）从 DOM 卸载，同时 `<MessageMetaRow>` 挂载到下方，两个独立的 reflow 同时发生在同一帧，叠加后看上去是 body chat 闪一下。曾尝试把光标与 meta-row 都改成「always-render + 可见性由 `isStreaming` 决定」彻底消 dựt，但流结束后尾部光标槽（16px）+ `space-y-3` 间距（0.75rem）+ meta-row `mt-2`（0.5rem）会在内容与元信息之间留下约 36px 空行，视觉上更糟。最终方案：meta-row 槽始终渲染 + `isStreaming` 时整块 `invisible`，消除 ~20-24px 的元信息挂载抖动；cursor 退回 `{isStreaming && <span .../>}` 条件挂载，流结束时会留下 ~16px 的小抖动，但比原始 ~36-40px 复合 dựt 小 ~60%，且不再引入空行
- AI 回复光标在流结束后继续闪橙点（CSS cascade 冲突）：cursor `<span>` 同时挂 `animate-pulse`（keyframes 把 opacity 拉到 1/0.5）和 `opacity-0`（要把它藏掉），CSS 动画声明在 cascade 里高于普通 author declarations，keyframes 的 opacity:1 一直盖住类的 opacity:0，光标在流结束后仍然可见且继续脉冲。最终方案：整个 cursor `<span>` 按 `{isStreaming && <span .../>}` 条件挂载——流结束就把 span 从 DOM 卸载，根本不会有 `animate-pulse` 与 `opacity-0` 共存的状态，cascade 冲突无从发生；也不需要 `transition-opacity` 做平滑淡出，cursor 直接消失

- 修复部分 provider/model（如 MiniMax M3、DeepSeek distill 等）把 reasoning 以原始 `<think>...</think>` inline 文本塞进 text content block 而非走 provider-channel 的 `{type:'thinking'}` 结构，导致 chat bubble 直接看到 `<think>...` 字面 + 推理 body 被铺在正文里：现在 `getAssistantText` 跑一遍 `stripThinkTags` 把 inline tag 与 body 从文本里剥掉，新增 `getLeakedThinking` 把被剥掉的 reasoning 抽出来，chat 页按序先渲染 provider-channel thinking、再渲染 leaked reasoning（每个一段独立的 `<ThinkingBlock>`），最后才渲染清洗后的正文；`getAssistantText` 复用了 title-generation 已经在用的同一个 `stripThinkTags`（fast-path idempotent，clean text 第二次调用直接 short-circuit），session-manager 路径与 DOM sub-agent 路径无回归——后者反而把「只 emit thinking 不 emit answer」从误判成功改成正确重试
- Fixed a chat-bubble rendering leak where providers/models that emit reasoning as raw inline `<think>...</think>` text (instead of a structured `{type:'thinking'}` content block — affects MiniMax M3, DeepSeek-distill, and similar) dumped the literal tag plus reasoning body into the visible bubble. `getAssistantText` now runs the joined text through `stripThinkTags` (already used by title generation, idempotent via fast-path on clean text) so the visible bubble is always clean, and a new `getLeakedThinking` returns the stripped-out reasoning bodies in source order. The chat page renders provider-channel thinking blocks first, then one `<ThinkingBlock>` per leaked reasoning entry, then the cleaned text — so leaked reasoning is collapsible just like structured thinking. No regression at the other two `getAssistantText` call sites: title generation already strips internally (now strips twice on already-clean text but the parser short-circuits); the DOM sub-agent runner now correctly treats "only thinking emitted, no answer" as a retry-worthy failure rather than a successful empty result
- Chat message size was hardcoded to `text-[0.9rem]`, ignoring the size slider in settings (`useChatFontSize` writes `--chat-font-size` but the message containers never consumed it). `UserMessageBubble` and `AgentMessage` now use `text-[length:var(--chat-font-size)]`, so message text scales with the user's preference — matching how MarkdownRenderer and ChatInput already worked
- Worker Team 主代理委派链路补齐 fallback 链路：之前 `delegate_task` 把 `mainModel` 硬编码为 `null`，导致 Settings → Advanced → Worker Models 留空（per-role 继承主会话）的 worker 在 `resolveWorkerModel` 三层兜底（`modelOverride` / `workerModels[role]` / `mainModel`）下仍然跑不到主模型，runner 立即 throw「No model could be resolved for role」。现在 `createDelegateTaskTool` 接收一个 `getMainModel?: () => ModelIdentity | null` 闭包，`session-manager.createAgent` 把活 agent 的 `modelIdentity` 包成该闭包注入；worker 端拿到主会话模型后即可直接跑，per-role 配置完全空也不会再卡住 worker。Normal send 路径同步改为读 1 份 `workerTeamOn` snapshot 喂给 tools + system prompt + user reminder：之前三处独立 `getValue()` 可能在同一 dispatch 内读到不同 flag、引发「prompt 说能用 worker / tool list 还没 push delegate_task」（或反之）的 LLM 幻觉调用，现在三者锁同一份 storage snapshot，`refreshAllSessionTools` 同样改造
- Fixed Worker Team delegation fallback chain: previously `delegate_task` hard-coded `mainModel: null`, so any worker role without a per-role `workerModels[role]` entry fell through `resolveWorkerModel`'s three-layer fallback (`modelOverride` / `workerModels[role]` / `mainModel`) and threw "No model could be resolved for role" immediately, breaking delegation for users who configured "Same as conversation model" everywhere. `createDelegateTaskTool` now accepts a `getMainModel?: () => ModelIdentity | null` closure; `session-manager.createAgent` wraps the live `AgentSession.modelIdentity` into that closure and injects it, so the worker runner falls through to the main session model without any per-role configuration. Normal-send path is also tightened to read a single `workerTeamEnabled` snapshot per dispatch and feed it into `buildSessionToolArray`, `composeSystemPrompt`, and the user-message reminder block simultaneously — previously the three read sites could disagree mid-dispatch, causing the LLM to either hallucinate calls to a tool that hadn't been pushed into its list or miss the worker when the prompt was right. `refreshAllSessionTools` follows the same single-snapshot contract
- 修复 retry/edit 后 stale reminder 仍误导模型的问题：原本 `rewindAndResume` 只重读 `workerTeamEnabled` 刷新 system prompt 与 tool list，user message 里的 `<reminder-instructions>` 块仍保留切换前的副本，导致用户从 Fast 切到 Team（或反之）后立即按 Retry，prompt 已在说「DEFAULT to delegate_task」但 user message reminder 仍是空的，模型继续按旧指令行事。引入 `lib/agent/prompt-envelope.ts` 的 `wrapReminderInstructions(body)` + `rewriteReminderInstructions(raw, body)` helper：retry/edit 在 `continue()` 之前直接改写最后一条 user message envelope 头部的 reminder 块，不重拼整条 user message（避免重读 stale attachments / context / memories），同时与 prompt-composer 拼装路径共用同一份 byte shape，让 prompt-cache prefix 在 OFF 状态仍然 byte-stable。`prompt-composer.ts` 的 reminder 副本也升级为模块级常量 `TEAM_REMINDER_COPY` 单一来源
- Fixed stale user-message reminder misleading the model on retry/edit: previously `rewindAndResume` only re-read `workerTeamEnabled` to refresh the system prompt and the tool list, leaving the `<reminder-instructions>` block inside the truncated user message stuck on the previous toggle's copy — so a user who flipped Fast ↔ Team during the idle window and then pressed Retry saw a prompt that asked for `delegate_task` while the user-message reminder still told the model to write directly. New helpers `wrapReminderInstructions(body)` + `rewriteReminderInstructions(raw, body)` in `lib/agent/prompt-envelope.ts`: retry/edit calls `rewriteReminderInstructions` on the last user message right before `continue()`, only swapping the reminder block header without re-composing the whole envelope (no re-read of stale attachments / context / memories), and the byte shape is shared with the prompt-composer assembly path so the prompt-cache prefix stays byte-stable in OFF state. The reminder copy in `prompt-composer.ts` is also hoisted to a module-level `TEAM_REMINDER_COPY` constant as a single source of truth
- 修复 Team 模式下主代理仍走 native `fs_create_file` 写 HTML 的根问题：之前仅有 prompt 软引导（`<available-workers>` 块 + reminder + `<reminder-instructions>` 提醒「call delegate_task」），主代理在某些 model（Minimax-M3 / Gemini flash 系等）下会忽略软引导、自行用 `fs_create_file` 写 HTML，结果 HTML 大文件直接进主代理 context（20+ 次 tool result 累加），且绕开了 `frontend_coder` worker 的 artifact skill 与 hard rules。引入 `lib/agent/worker-team-routing.ts` 的 hard gate：装在 main session 的 `beforeToolCall` 上、紧接 permission gate 之前执行；Team ON 时：
  - `fs_create_file` + `.html` / `.htm` 路径 → 直接 block，reason 引导「Call `delegate_task({ role: 'frontend_coder', output_path: ... })` instead. Do NOT retry this native fs_create_file in Team mode.」；
  - `fs_edit_file` + `.html` / `.htm` + `new_string` 全文档级 rewrite（`length > HTML_REWRITE_MIN_CHARS = 300` 且同时含 `<!doctype html>` 或 `<html` 与 `</html>` 或 `<body>`）→ 同样 block，reason 要求 `input_files: [path]` + `output_path: path` 让 worker 保留旧内容；
  - 局部小改动（typo / 改字 / 换 div / 调色）一律放行；非 HTML 路径（README.md / .txt 嵌 `<!doctype html>` codeblock）也放行。worker 子代理（`runWorkerAttempt` 内的 `createCebianAgent`）不传该 hook，`frontend_coder` 自己写 HTML 不被拦
- Fixed the root cause of the main agent still writing HTML via native `fs_create_file` in Team mode: previously only soft prompt-level guidance existed (the `<available-workers>` block + the user reminder + the `<reminder-instructions>` instruction "call delegate_task"); certain models (Minimax-M3 / Gemini flash variants) ignored the soft cue and went straight to `fs_create_file`, dumping the entire HTML into main-agent context via 20+ tool results and bypassing the `frontend_coder` worker's artifact skill + hard rules. Added `lib/agent/worker-team-routing.ts` as a hard gate installed on the main session's `beforeToolCall`, executed right before the permission gate: when Team is ON:
  - `fs_create_file` with a `.html` / `.htm` path → blocked outright, reason reads "Call `delegate_task({ role: 'frontend_coder', output_path: ... })` instead. Do NOT retry this native fs_create_file in Team mode.";
  - `fs_edit_file` on `.html` / `.htm` with a full-document rewrite (`new_string.length > HTML_REWRITE_MIN_CHARS = 300` AND contains both a document opener `<!doctype html>` or `<html` AND a closer `</html>` or `<body>`) → likewise blocked, reason requires `input_files: [path]` + `output_path: path` so the worker preserves the existing content;
  - small local edits (typos / word changes / div tweaks / color adjustments) still pass; non-HTML paths (README.md / .txt containing `<!doctype html>` codeblocks) also pass. Worker sub-agents (`createCebianAgent` inside `runWorkerAttempt`) do not receive this hook, so `frontend_coder`'s own HTML writes remain unblocked
- `MarkdownRenderer`'s `<p>` and `<li>` set `font-normal` unconditionally, overriding the agent body's `font-medium`. Removed the override so reply text inherits medium weight from the parent (otherwise the previous weight bump only affected the user bubble)
- Crop-region picker used `chrome.tabs.captureVisibleTab` after dragging outside the viewport, so anything past the fold got clamped at `img.width / height` in the offscreen canvas and the cropped image was just the visible portion. Now does scroll-and-stitch: the extension splits the document-coordinate rect into viewport-sized strips, calls `window.scrollTo` + `captureVisibleTab` for each strip in turn, crops the strip via the existing `crop-image` offscreen helper, and glues the strips together with a new `composite-vertical` IPC handler in the offscreen document — final tab scroll position is restored in `finally`. No CDP, no `captureBeyondViewport` quirks (which padded beyond-`scrollHeight` areas with repeated viewport content — a tall capture of a short page would show the page top stacked two or three times)
- Crop-region picker clamped the dragged rectangle to `document.documentElement.scrollWidth/scrollHeight` before sending it to the capture pipeline. Each strip in the scroll-and-stitch loop is anchored to its CSS `y` and the strip's `height` must fit within the document; clamping at the picker level keeps `window.scrollTo` from hitting `maxScrollY` and feeding the next `captureVisibleTab` viewport content from outside the document
- Crop-region auto-scroll at viewport edges was too slow. Widened the edge zone from 30 to 50 px and replaced the constant ±10 px/frame (~600 px/s) with a linear ramp over cursor depth: 14 px/frame at the zone boundary (~840 px/s) up to 60 px/frame right at the edge (~3600 px/s). Flicking past the edge rapidly scrolls large stretches; easing in from the inside still gives precise control
- Crop-region picker's `edgeSpeed` kept returning a non-zero value inside the viewport dead zone (cursor not in any edge buffer). The previous `ramp = MIN + (MAX-MIN)*factor` is always ≥ MIN, so `if (ramp === 0) return 0` never fired — the dead zone kept drifting toward bottom-right at ~840 px/s. Now checks `factor === 0` first (the real dead-zone predicate) and returns 0 immediately when the cursor is well inside the viewport
- Crop-region picker's `edgeSpeed` returned a positive value for every edge, so dragging toward the top/left edge scrolled the page in the *wrong* direction (down/right) and the cursor kept getting pushed out of the buffer zone — auto-scroll on those edges was effectively dead. Now branches `sign = -1` (top/left) and `+1` (bottom/right) and returns `sign * Math.min(MAX, ramp)` so each edge scrolls toward the page content
- Crop-region picker was hitting Chrome's `MAX_CAPTURE_VISIBLE_TAB_CALLS_PER_SECOND` quota (same tab capped at 2 captures/sec): scroll-and-stitch calls `chrome.tabs.captureVisibleTab` once per strip, so a 3+ strip rect blew the quota. Added a 550ms minimum interval at the top of each loop iteration (`await 550 - (Date.now() - lastCaptureAt)` when `lastCaptureAt > 0`); the first strip fires immediately. A 5-strip region now takes ~5-6 seconds end-to-end
- Crop-region picker was calling `chrome.tabs.captureVisibleTab(tabId, …)` with the tab id as the first argument — Chrome's MV3 signature is `(windowId?, options?)`, so passing `tabId` made it look for a window that didn't exist and silently failed (toast: "Failed to capture region"). Drop the first argument; the picker runs in the user's active tab, so omitting it captures the right viewport
- Crop-region picker error toasts now surface the underlying cause in the description: the picker's `message` (clamped to 200 chars) lands in the toast's `description`, so the user sees *why* it failed ("No strips produced for region capture", "Crop failed: …", etc.) instead of a bare "Failed to capture region". The full text still lands in the console for debug
- Streaming assistant bubble still has a small jump on stream end (cursor ~16px): on the `isStreaming: true → false` frame, the cursor `<span>` (16px tall) unmounted at the same instant `<MessageMetaRow>` mounted below — two unrelated reflows firing in the same frame read as a body jump. An earlier attempt tried making both unconditional (cursor always in the trailing slot via `opacity-0`, meta-row wrapper always rendered via `invisible`) to eliminate the jump entirely, but post-stream the cursor slot (16px) + `space-y-3` margin (0.75rem) + `mt-2` on the meta-row (0.5rem) left ~36px of empty space between the content and the action row — visually worse than the original jump. Final fix: meta-row wrapper stays always-rendered with `invisible` during streaming (eliminating the ~20-24px action-row jump), but the cursor reverts to `{isStreaming && <span .../>}` — stream end still produces a ~16px reflow, but it's ~60% smaller than the original ~36-40px combined jump and no longer leaves any empty space
- Streaming cursor left a permanently visible pulsing orange dot after the stream ended: the cursor `<span>` carried both `animate-pulse` (keyframes set opacity to 1/0.5) and `opacity-0` (intended to hide it). CSS animation declarations outrank normal author declarations in the cascade, so the keyframe opacity kept overriding the class opacity and the cursor stayed visible (and pulsing) after the stream. The cursor `<span>` is now conditionally mounted via `{isStreaming && <span .../>}` — there is no longer any state where `animate-pulse` and `opacity-0` coexist, so the cascade conflict can't fire; the cursor simply unmounts on stream end, and no `transition-opacity` fade is needed
- 在 sidepanel 焦点下按 ESC 现在也能取消 Crop region（裁剪区）picker，与 click-pick 行为对齐：`ChatInput` 中负责 keydown 的 `useEffect` 之前只在 `isPicking` 为 true 时挂 document keydown 监听，`isPickingRegion` 未覆盖，导致用户在 sidepanel 里按 ESC 关不掉裁剪；early-return 改为 `!isPicking && !isPickingRegion` 时直接返回，两个状态都加进依赖数组，共用同一个 `cancelElementPicker()` 收尾（`cancelElementPicker` 内部 `currentCleanup?.()` 是幂等 no-op，快速连按 ESC 也不会重复触发）
- Pressing ESC while the sidepanel has focus now also cancels the crop-region picker, matching the existing click-pick behavior: the `useEffect` in `ChatInput` that listens for document keydown was previously gated only on `isPicking`, leaving `isPickingRegion` uncovered, so ESC inside the sidepanel had no effect on an active crop picker. The early-return now checks both `isPicking` and `isPickingRegion`, both states are added to the dependency array, and both modes share the same `cancelElementPicker()` teardown (`cancelElementPicker` is `currentCleanup?.()` — an idempotent no-op, so rapid ESC presses don't double-fire). The page-side capture-phase handler in `lib/browser/element-picker.ts` still handles ESC when the page has focus; this change only covers the sidepanel-focus case that previously fell through
- 修复 `pnpm dev` 控制台报 `Refused to connect to ws://localhost:3210/` 及 `[wxt] Failed to connect to dev server`：sandbox 与 extension_pages 的 `connect-src` 只放了 `wss:` 没放 `ws:`，Vite HMR 用的是明文 `ws://`，CSP scheme matcher 按协议逐项匹配，`http://localhost:*` 不覆盖 `ws://`。在两条 `connect-src` 的 loopback 段后追加 `ws://localhost:* ws://127.0.0.1:*`（scheme token `ws:` 同步加上，与 `wss:` 同形状），与现有 `http://localhost:* http://127.0.0.1:*` 同形状、仍仅放行 loopback
- Fixed `pnpm dev` console errors `Refused to connect to ws://localhost:3210/` and `[wxt] Failed to connect to dev server`: the `connect-src` directives in both `sandbox` and `extension_pages` policies in `wxt.config.ts` listed `wss:` but not `ws:`. Vite HMR uses plaintext `ws://`; CSP scheme matchers are protocol-specific, so `http://localhost:*` did not cover `ws://`. Appended `ws://localhost:* ws://127.0.0.1:*` after the existing loopback widening in both `connect-src` directives (the `ws:` scheme token was added next to `wss:`, matching shape) — same loopback-only scope as the existing `http://localhost:* http://127.0.0.1:*`
- 修复 dev 控制台刷屏 60 条 `Loading the font violates "font-src https: data:"`：KaTeX 的 woff2 在 `katex-swap` CSS 里通过 `url(/assets/KaTeX_*.woff2)` 引用，dev 模式下 Vite 把它从 `http://localhost:3210/assets/...` 提供（不在 `https:` 白名单），prod 模式下解析到 `chrome-extension://<id>/assets/...`（扩展自身 origin 也不在 `https:` 白名单，且 `font-src` 没 `'self'`）。`katex-swap` 用 `font-display: swap`，下载期间用 fallback 字体可见，错误未引起用户注意但已经在 prod 静默失败。在 `extension_pages font-src` 末尾追加 `'self' http://localhost:* http://127.0.0.1:*`，dev / prod 同时覆盖；sandbox 页面 `font-src` 已包含 `'self'`，且 sandbox 不加载 bundled tailwind CSS，无需改动
- Fixed 60× console errors `Loading the font violates "font-src https: data:"`: KaTeX's `katex-swap` CSS references woff2 files via `url(/assets/KaTeX_*.woff2)`; in dev Vite serves them from `http://localhost:3210/assets/...` (not in the `https:` allowlist), in prod they resolve to `chrome-extension://<id>/assets/...` (the extension's own origin, also not covered by `https:` and `font-src` had no `'self'`). `katex-swap` uses `font-display: swap` so the fallback font masked the error and users didn't notice, but prod was silently failing. Appended `'self' http://localhost:* http://127.0.0.1:*` to `extension_pages font-src`; both dev and prod now covered. The sandbox `font-src` already includes `'self'` and sandbox pages don't import the bundled tailwind CSS, so no edit there

- 修复 dev / prod 加载 VFS / sidepanel / settings 时报 `Module "buffer" has been externalized for browser compatibility`：根因是 `lib/content/frontmatter.ts` 用的 `front-matter@4.0.2` 透传拉 `js-yaml@3.14.2`，后者 `lib/type/binary.js` 在模块初始化时 `require('buffer').Buffer`，Vite 把 Node `buffer` 在浏览器端 externalize 后整页直接挂掉。换成直接 `yaml@^2.9.0`：默认走 `parseYaml(text, { strict: false })`，遇到 `DUPLICATE_KEY` 这类 yaml@2 严格模式的报错时降级到 `parseDocument(...).toJS()`（last-wins），其它解析错误 / 未知 tag 仍按 front-matter@4 旧行为静默返回空 data；frontmatter 起始符正则同时支持 `---` 与 `= yaml =`，结束符支持 `---` 与 `...`，BOM / CRLF / 空 frontmatter 都覆盖。public API `parseFrontmatter(content): { data, body }` 不变，9 个调用点零改动；同步删除两个零引用的私有 helper `serializeFrontmatter` / `serializeYaml`（[Naming & module API]）。新增 `lib/content/frontmatter.test.ts` 共 11 个 case（BOM / CRLF / 空 / `= yaml =` 起始 / `...` 结束 / 坏 YAML / 重复键 / YAML 1.2 布尔字面量等）。注意 yaml@2 遵循 YAML 1.2：bare `yes` / `no` / `on` / `off` 与 bare ISO-8601 日期（如 `date: 2024-05-15`）都解析为字符串而非布尔 / `Date` 实例，只有显式 `!!timestamp` tag 才会得到 `Date`——全仓 grep 已确认无影响
- Fixed the `Module "buffer" has been externalized for browser compatibility` error that broke VFS / sidepanel / settings pages in dev and prod: root cause was `lib/content/frontmatter.ts` using `front-matter@4.0.2`, which transitively pulls `js-yaml@3.14.2` whose `lib/type/binary.js` does `require('buffer').Buffer` at module-init time — once Vite externalized Node `buffer` for the browser, the whole entry failed to load. Switched to `yaml@^2.9.0` directly: defaults to `parseYaml(text, { strict: false })`, and on `DUPLICATE_KEY`-class errors from yaml@2's strict mode falls back to `parseDocument(...).toJS()` (last-wins); other parse errors / unknown tags still degrade to empty data, matching the old front-matter@4 behavior. The frontmatter opener regex now matches both `---` and `= yaml =`; the closer matches `---` and `...`; BOM / CRLF / empty frontmatter are all covered. The public API `parseFrontmatter(content): { data, body }` is unchanged — all 9 call sites needed no edits. Two dead private helpers (`serializeFrontmatter` / `serializeYaml`, zero repo references — [Naming & module API]) were removed at the same time. Added `lib/content/frontmatter.test.ts` with 11 cases covering BOM / CRLF / empty / `= yaml =` opener / `...` closer / malformed YAML / duplicate keys / YAML 1.2 boolean literals. Note: yaml@2 follows YAML 1.2, so bare `yes` / `no` / `on` / `off` AND bare ISO-8601 dates (e.g. `date: 2024-05-15`) both parse as strings instead of booleans / `Date` instances — only an explicit `!!timestamp` tag yields a `Date`. grep confirmed no in-repo file is affected
- 修复选区 Quote 浮动按钮在侧边栏滚动时跳动 / 滞后的 bug：`SelectionQuoteButton` 在 `window` 的 `scroll` 事件触发时调用 `recompute()`，把选区当前的 viewport rect 写入 `pos`，但 Shadcn Button 默认带 `transition-all`（`transition-property: all; transition-duration: 150ms`），每个 scroll tick 改 `top`/`left` 都被 ease 化，又被下一个 scroll tick 打断，看上去 button 在追着 scroll 而不是贴在选区上。改为：在 `useEffect` 里给 `window` 的 `scroll` 注册 capture-phase listener、`resize` 用默认 phase listener，每个 tick 同步调 `recompute()`，并在 button 的 className 上加 `!transition-colors` override —— `transition-colors` 只 transition color / background-color / border-color 等颜色相关属性，`top` / `left` 不在其中，所以 `top` / `left` snap 在与 scroll 同一帧，无 150 ms ease。Button 渲染也从原本的 React 树内改成 `createPortal(<Button/>, document.body)`，确保 `position: fixed` 不会被 Radix ScrollArea viewport 或其它祖先的 `transform` / `will-change` / `contain` 拉成它的 containing block（之前若有这种 ancestor，button 会被一起滚走）。`onSelectionChange` 同时加 identity gate（`startContainer` / `endContainer` DOM node ref + offset）防 reflow 触发的二次重算。Hard-scroll 把 selection 清掉时 button 仍按旧逻辑隐藏
- Fixed the floating Quote button used to lag or jump while scrolling the sidepanel: every scroll tick re-anchored via `recompute()` and wrote the selection's new viewport rect into `pos`, but `Button`'s default `transition-all` (`transition-property: all; transition-duration: 150ms`) animated each `top`/`left` change, and the next scroll tick kept interrupting the in-flight ease — so the button appeared to chase the scroll rather than track the selection. Now `useEffect` registers a capture-phase `window` `scroll` listener and a default-phase `window` `resize` listener (resize is functionally identical either way — `window` sits at the top of the capture chain and resize doesn't bubble), each tick calls `recompute()` synchronously, and the button's className additionally carries `!transition-colors` (the `!` `important` prefix guarantees it overrides Shadcn's default). `transition-colors` only transitions color-related properties — `top` / `left` are not in the list, so they snap on the same frame as the scroll with no 150 ms ease to interrupt across rapid ticks. The button itself is rendered through `createPortal(..., document.body)` instead of staying inside the React tree, so `position: fixed` is unambiguously viewport-relative and cannot be captured into a containing block by any Radix ScrollArea / Tailwind `transform` / `will-change` / `contain` ancestor that would otherwise make the button scroll along with content. `onSelectionChange` keeps an identity gate (DOM node refs for `startContainer` / `endContainer` + their offsets) so reflow-induced `selectionchange` events short-circuit and don't double-recompute during a scroll burst. Hard scrolls that wipe the selection still hide the button as before
- 修复 `/vfs` 中 HTML 预览的链接点击被 Chrome 静默拦截：`entrypoints/html-preview.sandbox/main.ts` 的 `INNER_SANDBOX` 和 `entrypoints/vfs/ui/FileView.tsx` 的 outer iframe 都明确不放 `allow-popups`（commit `c4fd09c` 故意排除），导致 `<a target="_blank">` 没有任何反应、`<a target="_top">` 同样静默失败、不带 `target` 的 `<a>` 又把内层 iframe navigate 走（且多数外站因 `X-Frame-Options` / `frame-ancestors` 拒绝被框架而报「This content is blocked. Contact the site owner to fix the issue.」）。改为：(1) 两个 iframe 的 `sandbox` 都补上 `allow-popups`（`allow-top-navigation` 仍然不放——避免预览把 sidepanel parent tab navigate 走）；(2) 在 `renderHtml` 把用户的整段 HTML 包进一个新建的 `<!doctype html><html><head>…</head><body>…</body></html>` 外层 document，head 里固定放 `<base target="_blank">` + 一段迷你 override `<script>`（DOMContentLoaded 时遍历所有 `<a href>`，把 `target` 强写为 `_blank` 并补 `rel="noopener noreferrer"`）——单 `<base>` 按 HTML 规范会输给链接自带的 `target=`（所以 `<a target="_self">` / `target="_top">` 仍会穿帮），script 层把这两个 case 也盖住。注入走 wrap 模式而不是 regex-inject 进用户已有 `<head>`，避免 `<!-- <head> template --><head>real</head>` 这类 comment decoy 绕过——HTML parser 会把用户自己的 `<html>` / `<head>` / `<body>` 折叠掉，我们的 `<base>` / `<script>` 永远在 `<head>` 最前面，与 `entrypoints/mcp-app.sandbox/main.ts` 的 `wrapWithCspDocument` 同款手法。结果：四种 `target` 写法的链接一致走新 tab、预览本身永远不会被替换、Chrome 不再拦截。注入的 `<base>` / `<script>` 只活在 iframe srcdoc 里，VFS 源文件与 View source 模式都不受影响
- Fixed clicks on links inside the `/vfs` HTML preview being silently blocked by Chrome: both the outer iframe sandbox (in `entrypoints/vfs/ui/FileView.tsx`) and the inner `INNER_SANDBOX` constant (in `entrypoints/html-preview.sandbox/main.ts`) deliberately omitted `allow-popups` when the inline preview shipped in commit `c4fd09c`. The result was three distinct failure modes — `<a target="_blank">` did nothing, `<a target="_top">` did nothing, and `<a>` without a `target` (or with `target="_self"`) navigated the inner iframe in place, which most external sites reject via `X-Frame-Options` / `frame-ancestors` and surfaces Chrome's "This content is blocked. Contact the site owner to fix the issue." page. The fix adds `allow-popups` to both sandbox attribute strings (`allow-top-navigation` stays excluded so the preview can never replace the parent sidepanel tab) and, in `renderHtml`, wraps the user's HTML in a fresh `<!doctype html><html><head>…</head><body>…</body></html>` outer document whose `<head>` carries a `<base target="_blank">` element plus a tiny override `<script>` that walks every `<a href>` at `DOMContentLoaded` and rewrites `target` to `_blank` with `rel="noopener noreferrer"`. The script is required because, per the HTML spec, a link's own `target=` attribute wins over `<base>`, so without the script the `<a target="_self">` / `<a target="_top">` cases would still slip through. The wrap-the-document pattern (rather than regex-injecting into the user's existing `<head>`) is comment-decoy-proof — an HTML comment like `<!-- <head> template --><head>real</head>` would otherwise let the regex match the comment's `<head>` and drop the override where the browser ignores it; the HTML parser collapses any duplicate `<html>` / `<head>` / `<body>` the user includes, so our injected elements always land first in `<head>`. Same pattern as `wrapWithCspDocument` in `entrypoints/mcp-app.sandbox/main.ts`. After the fix: all four `target` variants open in a new tab, the preview itself is never replaced by the linked site, and Chrome stops blocking. The injected `<base>` / `<script>` live only inside the iframe's srcdoc — the VFS source file and the "View source" toggle both show the unmodified text

- skill 沙箱 `vfs.read` 现在能读到同 session 内 agent 早些时候产生的同级文件：`vfs.readFile('../foo.html')`（agent 在 `/workspaces/<sessionId>/foo.html` 写的待部署 HTML）等过去会被 `vfs path escapes skill workspace` 拦下的路径，现在通过 sandbox-rpc 的 writeRoot-first / readRoot-fallback 兜底解析（`resolveReadScopedPath` 把 `..` 视为从 skill 子目录走出、落到 session workspace 根），skill 不必再借 `fs_rename` 把文件物理搬进自己的子目录。`vfs.readFile('cat.png')` 这种直传相对路径仍按既有语义在 skill 子目录里找（writeRoot 优先命中），`vfs.cwd` 仍返回 writeRoot（skill 自己的子目录），markdown 链接模板不受影响；写作用域完全不变（仍只在 skill 子目录内），跨 session 路径依旧被 `resolveReadScopedPath` 在 readRoot 这一关拦下
- Skill sandbox `vfs.read` can now reach sibling files the agent produced earlier in the same session: paths like `vfs.readFile('../foo.html')` (e.g. HTML awaiting deploy at `/workspaces/<sessionId>/foo.html`) used to throw `vfs path escapes skill workspace`. They now resolve through sandbox-rpc's writeRoot-first / readRoot-fallback parser (new `resolveReadScopedPath` helper treats `..` as "walk out of the skill subdir" so it lands inside the session workspace root) so skills no longer have to physically `fs_rename` files into their own subdir. Plain relative paths like `vfs.readFile('cat.png')` still resolve against the skill's own writeRoot first (no behaviour change for skill-self reads); `vfs.cwd` still returns the writeRoot, so existing markdown-link templates are unaffected. Write scope is unchanged (still locked to the skill subdir); cross-session paths remain blocked — `resolveReadScopedPath`'s readRoot boundary still rejects anything escaping `/workspaces/<sessionId>/`
- 修复 v1.7.0 引入的「mid-stream 重新订阅偶发文本重复」bug（"lỗi nhả chữ"）：subscribe 期间 agent 仍在 emit，pi-agent-core 会**先同步**把 `partial.content` 写进 `messages` 再 `await emit` 把 BG handler 排成 microtask——若该 microtask 在 subscribe 的最后一个 await resolve 之后才被调度，对应事件就只进了 `session_state` 快照、还没进缓冲；trailing 帧到期后把同一段 delta 又投了一遍，UI 端因此显示 "ABCC"。新 viewer 在订阅后 210ms 窗口内仅接收快照与控制帧，跳过该窗口内会与快照重叠的 `stream_ops` 帧——`entrypoints/background/chat/viewers.ts` 新增 per-port 抑制表 `suppressedUntil`（`WeakMap<Port, number>`，port GC 时自动回收），`broadcastToViewers` 命中 `stream_ops` 时按 `Date.now()` 懒判定；`client-handlers.ts` 在 `subscribe` 的两个 post 分支末尾调用 `suppressStreamOpsFor(port, FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS * 2 + 50)`，窗长按 `2 × FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS + 50` 算（一个 trailing 周期 × 2 覆盖 subscribe 末尾到下两个 trailing 窗口，+50ms 是 MV3 service worker 下 `setTimeout` 的实测漂移上限）。新增 `entrypoints/background/chat/client-handlers.test.ts` 端到端回归测试断言：豁免窗内新 viewer 不收带重复 delta 的 `stream_ops`、`session_state` 立即到达；豁免窗外照常投递
- Fixed the v1.7.0 "mid-stream re-subscribe causes occasional text duplication" bug ("lỗi nhả chữ"): while a viewer re-subscribes, the agent may still be emitting — pi-agent-core's `message_update` path **synchronously** mutates `partial.content` into `messages` and then `await emit`s the BG handler as a microtask. If that microtask is only scheduled after the subscribe handler's final `await` resolves, the corresponding event lands in the `session_state` snapshot but never reaches the broadcast buffer in time; the trailing frame then ships the same delta again, producing "ABCC" on the UI. New viewers now skip trailing `stream_ops` frames within a 210 ms window after subscribing while still receiving the snapshot and control frames — `entrypoints/background/chat/viewers.ts` adds a per-port `suppressedUntil` map (`WeakMap<Port, number>`, entries auto-evicted when the port is GC'd) and `broadcastToViewers` checks it lazily against `Date.now()` on every `stream_ops` dispatch; `client-handlers.ts` calls `suppressStreamOpsFor(port, FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS * 2 + 50)` at the tail of both `session_state` and `session_loaded` post branches inside `subscribe`. The window is sized at `2 × FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS + 50` — one trailing period doubled to cover the gap between `subscribe`'s tail and the next two trailing windows, plus 50 ms to absorb the worst-case MV3 service-worker `setTimeout` drift observed in production. A new `entrypoints/background/chat/client-handlers.test.ts` end-to-end regression test asserts that within the suppression window a new viewer receives `session_state` immediately but no overlapping-delta `stream_ops`, while outside the window frames flow normally

- 修复 Worker 超时常量文档/运行时不匹配的文档漂移（Subtask 8.10）：早期 Subtask 8.5 CHANGELOG 写「TTFT 45s + idle 20s」是当时理想值，运行时落地后被调整为 `WORKER_TTFT_MS = 120_000` (120s) / `WORKER_IDLE_MS = 180_000` (180s)——3× / 9× 更大，给本地 OpenAI-compatible 代理（Kimi K3 emit gap 227s、Minimax-M3 冷启首字节 30-60s）保留缓冲窗口。`resolvePhaseTimeout` 在 emit 阶段故意用 per-role ceiling 而非 `WORKER_IDLE_MS`（Subtask 7 引入），导致 model 在 emit 阶段 stall（Minimax-M3 emit 1 token 后 hang）只能由 ceiling（frontend_coder 300s）兜底，不会被 idle 早 fire——是显式选的 design trade-off，**非** bug，理由是兼容 buffer 巨型 tool-call args 的本地 proxy。代码层没有任何 timeout 行为变更；只把 `lib/agent/worker-roles.ts:287-289` 的常量上方加了 22 行 drift-fix docstring + `resolvePhaseTimeout` 文档补一段 emit 阶段取舍说明；`worker-roles.test.ts` 新增 2 条 pinning 测试把 `WORKER_TTFT_MS === 120_000` / `WORKER_IDLE_MS === 180_000` 钉在 CI（改字面量必须同步更新 CHANGELOG + plan + docstring）；`worker-runner.test.ts` 7 处 hardcoded `'20000ms'` / `'120000ms'` / `'45000ms'` 字面量改为 `${WORKER_IDLE_MS}` / `${WORKER_TIMEOUT_MS}` / `${WORKER_TTFT_MS}` template literals，让 runner 错误消息 self-updating（runtime 行为不变，只移除 stale literal drift）
- Fixed a documentation/runtime drift in the Worker timeout constants (Subtask 8.10). The earlier Subtask 8.5 CHANGELOG entry listed "TTFT 45s + idle 20s" — that was an aspirational figure at the time; the runtime landed on `WORKER_TTFT_MS = 120_000` (120s) and `WORKER_IDLE_MS = 180_000` (180s), 3× / 9× larger, to keep the buffer window open for local OpenAI-compatible proxies (Kimi K3's 227s emit gap, Minimax-M3's 30–60s cold first-byte). `resolvePhaseTimeout` deliberately uses the per-role ceiling rather than `WORKER_IDLE_MS` during the `emitting` phase (introduced in Subtask 7) — the consequence is that models which stall mid-emit (e.g. Minimax-M3 emits 1 token then hangs) are caught by the per-role ceiling only (frontend_coder: 300s) rather than the 180s idle timer. This is an explicit design trade-off chosen to keep buffering proxies with huge tool-call argument streams alive, **not** a bug. No runtime timeout behavior changes in this subtask; only the documentation is reconciled — a 22-line drift-fix docstring is added above the constants in `lib/agent/worker-roles.ts:287-289`, the `resolvePhaseTimeout` docstring gains a paragraph explaining the emit-phase trade-off (referencing this CHANGELOG entry), `worker-roles.test.ts` gains 2 pinning tests (`WORKER_TTFT_MS === 120_000`, `WORKER_IDLE_MS === 180_000`) so changing the literals will fail CI until CHANGELOG + plan + docstring are updated in lockstep, and 7 hardcoded `'20000ms'` / `'120000ms'` / `'45000ms'` ms-string literals in `worker-runner.test.ts` are switched to `${WORKER_IDLE_MS}` / `${WORKER_TIMEOUT_MS}` / `${WORKER_TTFT_MS}` template literals — runner error messages now self-update against the constants, but the runtime values they render to are unchanged
- 修复 worker stuck-loop detector 在 reviewer 合法分页读取时被误判的 false-positive：Phase 1 旧 dedup key 是单 `path`，把 reviewer 读 60KB HTML 文件通过 `start_line` / `end_line` 增量分页（4 次调用同一个 path 不同 range）误抓成 `same_path_repeat` —— 复现于 `cebian-debug-20260909-211043.json` attempt 4，10.9s 时 abort 误伤。Phase 2 把 dedup key 改成完整 `(path, start_line, end_line)` 签名（`lastReadSignature` + `sameChunkRepeats` 替换 Phase 1 的 `lastReadPath` + `samePathRepeats`），caller 在每次 tool_execution_start 用 `buildReadSignature(args)` 拼签名并对比；签名变化时重置 `sameChunkRepeats` 回 1（即「不同 chunk」），只有真正连读同一 chunk 才递增到 ≥4 触发 Threshold A。Threshold B（post-write ≥6）逻辑未变，给研究型 multi-read 留余地的逻辑也保留；`fs_list` 无 path 仍 reset counter（researcher 浏览目录是合法流程）；reproduce 路径（4 reads paginate 同一文件）现 sameChunkRepeats=1 在 helper 端不再触发。3 条新 boundary pin 钉在 CI：cùng path 4 lần paginate（range tăng）、3 same-chunk + 1 paginate + 1 same-chunk（counter 跨越转换重置）、3 paginate + 1 same-chunk（reverse direction），确保 dedup key 是 chunk-level 而非 path-level
- Fixed a false-positive where the worker stuck-loop detector aborted reviewer workflows during legitimate file pagination. Phase 1's dedup key was the path alone, so a reviewer reading a 60 KB HTML file across four `fs_read_file` calls with increasing `start_line` / `end_line` (e.g. `0–100`, `100–200`, `200–300`, `300–400`) tripped Threshold A at the fourth call even though it was reading four different chunks — reproduced in `cebian-debug-20260909-211043.json`, attempt 4, abort fired at 10.9 s. Phase 2 replaces the dedup key with the full `(path, start_line, end_line)` tuple: caller now builds a `lastReadSignature = ${path}|${start_line ?? ''}|${end_line ?? ''}` per `tool_execution_start` event and only increments `sameChunkRepeats` (renamed from `samePathRepeats`) when the signature matches the previous read; a different signature (i.e. different chunk) resets the counter to 1, so the four legitimate page-reads keep the counter at 1 each and never reach the `≥4` Threshold A boundary. Threshold B (post-write ≥6) is unchanged; the carve-out for researcher / reviewer multi-file reads still applies, and `fs_list` without a path still resets the counter (browsing directories remains legal). Three new boundary pins cover both directions of the chunk transition (4 reads paginated → no trigger; 3 same-chunk + 1 paginate + 1 same-chunk → counter resets across the transition; 3 paginate + 1 same-chunk → reverse direction same outcome) so the dedup key stays chunk-level and not path-level

- 修复合并（merge）模式恢复备份时 Worker Team 的 per-role 模型与超时配置被静默丢弃的问题：`workerModels` / `workerRoleTimeouts` 虽在备份注册表中登记为 settings 分类、备份包里也确实带着它们，但 merge 策略只写声明了 `fillMissing` 的 item，二者未声明 → 恢复后（尤其新 profile / 全新安装）所有 worker role 落回默认（模型继承主模型、超时用 role registry 默认），用户被迫在 Settings → Advanced 里重选。现按 role key 补缺：本地 map 里已有的 role 保留本地、缺失的 role 从备份补入，本地为空时备份整体生效——与 `providerCredentials` 的补缺语义一致。replace 策略行为不变

- Fixed merge-mode backup restore silently dropping Worker Team per-role model and timeout configuration: `workerModels` / `workerRoleTimeouts` were registered in the backup registry under the settings class and were present in the backup payload, but the merge strategy only writes items that declare `fillMissing` and neither did — so after a restore (especially onto a fresh profile / clean install) every worker role fell back to defaults (model inherits the main session model, timeouts use the role registry defaults) and users had to re-pick them in Settings → Advanced. Both now back-fill per role key: roles already present in the local map keep the local value, missing roles are filled from the backup, and an empty local map takes the backup wholesale — matching the `providerCredentials` fill semantics. Replace-strategy behavior is unchanged

- 修复合并（merge）模式恢复备份时 3 个 persona items (`local:personaEnabled` / `local:persona` / `local:personaIdentity`) 被静默丢弃：与 Worker per-role 修复同根因——注册表里登记为 settings 分类、备份包也带着它们，但 merge 只写声明了 `fillMissing` 的 item，三条 persona 都没声明 → 恢复后（尤其新 profile）persona 副本（SOUL 文本 + 4 字段 identity + OpenClaw 开关）整体归零，用户被迫在 Settings → Persona 里重写。现在三条都声明 `fillMissing` 走 merge「默认视为空、用备份值覆盖」语义：personaEnabled OFF / personaSoul 空串 / personaIdentity 4 字段全空视为默认 → 备份补入；本地已配置（非空）则保留本地（merge「只增不减」契约，与 `providerCredentials` 同 shape）。`personaEnabled` 的「false 视为空」 caveat：merge 无法区分「默认未配置」与「用户主动 OFF」，后者也会被备份覆盖——与 workerModels「Off 以 delete key 表达」同 caveat。三条都无 `splitSecret`，不需 credentials 分类联动。Hoist helper `isEmptyValue` 出 `sources/storage.ts` 顶层为新文件 `lib/backup/is-empty-value.ts`，registry 与 sources 双侧 import，避免循环依赖

- Fixed merge-mode backup restore silently dropping the 3 persona items (`local:personaEnabled` / `local:persona` / `local:personaIdentity`) — same root cause as the worker per-role fix one entry above: the items were registered under the settings class and present in the backup payload, but the merge strategy only writes items that declare `fillMissing` and none of the persona items did — so after a restore (especially onto a fresh profile / clean install) the user's persona copy (SOUL text + 4 identity fields + OpenClaw master switch) was zeroed out and users had to re-author it under Settings → Persona. All three now declare `fillMissing` with merge「default-treated-as-empty / back-fills from backup」semantics: personaEnabled OFF / personaSoul empty string / personaIdentity 4 fields all empty count as "default" → back-fills from the backup; already-configured (non-empty) local values are kept (merge's "additive-only" contract, same shape as `providerCredentials`). `personaEnabled` carries an explicit "false treated as empty" caveat: merge cannot distinguish "user toggled OFF" from "default OFF", so the former is also overwritten by the backup — same caveat as workerModels's "Off expressed via delete key". None of the three is a hybrid item, so no `splitSecret` and no credentials-class wiring needed. The merge-time "is the local value default/empty" helper `isEmptyValue` was hoisted from `sources/storage.ts:24-29` into a new top-level `lib/backup/is-empty-value.ts` so both `registry.ts` and `sources/storage.ts` import from there — breaks the latent circular import (registry → BACKUP_REGISTRY ← sources/storage) without touching call semantics

- 修复合并（merge）模式恢复备份时 4 个标量设置项（压缩模型 `compactionModel` / DOM 子代理模型 `domSubAgentModel` / 自定义指引 `userInstructions` / 自动标题 `autoTitleSettings`）被静默丢弃：与前面 Worker / persona 两条修复同根因——item 已登记为 settings 分类、备份包里也确实携带，但 merge 只写声明了 `fillMissing` 的 item，四个都没声明 → 恢复后（尤其新 profile）这些配置落回默认（压缩模型跟随主模型、`delegate_dom` 功能关闭、自定义指引空串、自动标题回到默认开关 + 无标题模型），用户被迫重配。现在四条都声明 `fillMissing`：两个 `ModelIdentity | null` 型以「本地 null = 默认」→ 用备份值补入；`userInstructions` 以「本地空串 = 默认」→ 补入（与 `personaSoul` 同形态，纯空白串视为用户输入保留本地）；`autoTitleSettings` 以「本地等于默认值（enabled=true 且 model=null）」→ 备份整体补入，本地主动关闭（enabled=false）或已配标题模型则保留本地——比 `personaEnabled` 的 `local || backup` 更精确，「主动关闭」能在 merge 下存活。replace 策略行为不变

- Fixed merge-mode backup restore silently dropping 4 scalar settings (compaction model `compactionModel`, DOM sub-agent model `domSubAgentModel`, custom instructions `userInstructions`, auto-title settings `autoTitleSettings`) — same root cause as the Worker / persona fixes above: the items were registered under the settings class and present in the backup payload, but merge only writes items that declare `fillMissing` and none of the four did — so after a restore (especially onto a fresh profile) they all fell back to defaults (compaction follows the main model, `delegate_dom` disabled, custom instructions empty, auto-title back to default switch + no title model) and users had to reconfigure. All four now declare `fillMissing`: the two `ModelIdentity | null` items back-fill from the backup when local is `null` (= default); `userInstructions` back-fills when local is the empty string (same shape as `personaSoul`; whitespace-only counts as user input and is kept); `autoTitleSettings` back-fills wholesale when local equals the default value (`enabled=true` + `model=null`), while a local deliberate OFF (`enabled=false`) or a configured title model keeps local — more precise than `personaEnabled`'s `local || backup`, so deliberately turning auto-title off survives a merge restore. Replace-strategy behavior is unchanged

- 修复 v1.7.1 引入的「session tool 数组中 scheduler 4 件套（scheduler_list / scheduler_create / scheduler_delete / scheduler_run_now）各注册两次」的问题：`schedulerTools` 在 `sharedTools` 里 spread 一次、又在 `buildSessionToolArray` 末尾重复 `push` 一次，导致发往模型端的 tool declarations 翻倍。Anthropic / OpenAI 对重名静默容忍，但 Vertex AI 校验函数名唯一并以 400 `Duplicate function declaration found: scheduler_list` 直接拒绝请求（vertex/gemini 路由完全不可用）。注册点现收敛为 `sharedTools` 唯一处；新增 `lib/tools/index.test.ts` registry guard（断言全数组 tool name 唯一 + scheduler 4 件套各恰好 1 次），此类回归今后在 CI 即刻失败
- Fixed the v1.7.1 regression where every session's tool array registered the 4 scheduler tools (`scheduler_list` / `scheduler_create` / `scheduler_delete` / `scheduler_run_now`) twice: `schedulerTools` was spread once inside `sharedTools` and pushed again at the end of `buildSessionToolArray`, doubling every scheduler tool declaration sent to the model. Anthropic / OpenAI tolerate duplicate names silently, but Vertex AI enforces unique function names and rejects the request outright with 400 `Duplicate function declaration found: scheduler_list`, breaking the vertex/gemini route entirely. The registration point is now the single `sharedTools` spread; a new `lib/tools/index.test.ts` registry guard (asserts tool names are globally unique across the array + each scheduler tool appears exactly once) makes this class of regression fail CI immediately

- 修复 v1.7.1 区域截图（Crop region）在真实页面上裁切偏移的一组问题。scroll-and-stitch 抓取循环此前假设「滚到哪、capture 就锚定在哪」，三种情况都会打破它：文档底部被 Chrome 静默 clamp、页面 CSS `scroll-behavior: smooth` 动画未落定、capture 前后页面仍在滑动（拖拽期 smooth 滚动尾巴 / snap re-snap / scroll anchoring）——表现为 crop 结果整体偏移一个固定条带（实测「上边多一条、下边少一条」或反向）。修复：抓取全程 `behavior: 'instant'` 滚动（含拖拽期 auto-scroll / wheel）；裁剪区完整落在当前 viewport 时走零滚动 fast path；每条 strip 在 capture 前后各读一次 scrollY 校验稳定性，漂移 >0.5px 自动等页面静止、拉回目标位重拍（≤3 次），裁切一律按 capture 后读回的实际锚点换算（顺带修复文档底部 clamp 与 RTL 页面负 scrollX）。另外：截图期间临时隐藏页面 `position: fixed` / `sticky` 元素（导航条、悬浮球等，按 data 标记快照/原样归还内联样式）——此前固定 header 被印进每条 strip 顶部，既让裁切偏移正好等于 header 高度，又在长图拼接里重复出现
- Fixed a set of region-crop (Crop region) misalignment bugs from v1.7.1 on real-world pages. The scroll-and-stitch capture loop assumed "scroll there, and the capture anchors there" — three things break that: Chrome silently clamping scrolls near the document bottom, CSS `scroll-behavior: smooth` animations still in flight, and the page drifting after the anchor read (smooth-scroll tails from drag auto-scroll / snap re-snap / scroll anchoring) — producing a crop shifted by a constant band (observed as "extra at top, missing at bottom" or the reverse). Fixes: all capture-path scrolling is now `behavior: 'instant'` (including drag auto-scroll and wheel); a crop fully inside the current viewport takes a zero-scroll fast path; every strip reads scrollY before AND after its capture to verify stability, and on >0.5px drift waits for the page to settle, re-scrolls and re-captures (≤3 attempts); cropping always uses the actual anchor read back after the capture (also fixing bottom-of-document clamping and negative scrollX on RTL pages). Additionally, `position: fixed` / `sticky` page elements (navbars, floating balls, etc.) are temporarily hidden during capture (snapshot/restore of inline styles via data markers) — a fixed header used to be stamped onto the top of every strip, shifting the crop by exactly the header height and duplicating itself in stitched tall captures

- 修复 Telegram `/tabs` 截图对后台 tab / 被 Memory Saver discard 的 tab 失败的一组问题：capture 从 `captureVisibleTab`（只能拍 focused window 的可见 tab）整体迁移到 `chrome.debugger` + CDP `Page.captureScreenshot`——offscreen 渲染、不 activate、不抢用户焦点、不受 2次/秒 配额限制；后台 tab / 其它窗口的 tab 直接可拍；discarded tab 先 reload 唤醒到加载完成再拍。同时修复「先报错、照片随后才到」的自相矛盾：开始拍摄即把键盘消息改写为「⏳ Đang chụp tab…」（gateway 收到 editMessage 会取消 5s watchdog——此前慢速 capture 会让 watchdog 先打「⚠️ Hết giờ chụp」、照片迟到照样送达）；同 tab 连拍串行化（double-tap / webhook 重投不再互相踩踏）；错误恒为一条消息并说明具体原因（DevTools 冲突 / 系统页禁止 attach / 睡眠唤醒失败 / tab 已关闭），失败绝不发照片；使用完毕 detach 干净，不留「正在被调试」黄色横幅

- Fixed a set of Telegram `/tabs` screenshot failures for background tabs and Memory-Saver-discarded tabs: capture moves wholesale from `captureVisibleTab` (which only captures the visible tab of the focused window) to `chrome.debugger` + CDP `Page.captureScreenshot` — offscreen rendering, no tab activation, no focus stealing, no 2-per-second quota; background tabs and tabs in other windows capture directly; a discarded tab is reloaded and awaited to load-completion before capture. Also fixes the self-contradictory "error first, photo arrives later": the moment a capture starts, the keyboard message is rewritten to "⏳ Đang chụp tab…" (the gateway cancels its 5s watchdog upon receiving that editMessage — previously a slow capture let the watchdog post "⚠️ Hết giờ chụp" first while the late photo still arrived); same-tab rapid captures are serialized (double-taps / webhook redeliveries no longer race each other); errors are always exactly one message stating the specific cause (DevTools conflict / system page refusing attach / discard wake failure / tab closed) and a failed capture never sends a photo; the debugger detaches cleanly afterwards — no lingering "being debugged" yellow bar

- 修复 Chat 渲染：连续 tool-only assistant 回合被拆成 N 个独立消息块的视觉杂乱。当 provider（如 antigravity / gemini-3.8-flash 经 9router-go 网关）每个回合只发一个 tool call 且 `stopReason` 报 `stop` 而非 `toolUse` 时，pi-agent-core 把每回合的 stopReason 都标为 turn-closing，导致 N 个中间回合各自渲染一个 AgentMessage、每块都带 model 名称 + token 计数 + 重试按钮，N 个分隔壁反复出现。现按**内容形态**而非 stopReason 分组：连续的「只含 tool call / thinking、没有面向用户文本」assistant 回合被收成一个 ToolRunBlock 折叠卡（`components/chat/tool-run-groups.ts` pure helper）；header 行显示「调用了 N 个工具」，body 默认展开、按时间顺序渲染每回合的 thinking 块 + 工具卡（让用户看清每一步用了哪个工具），用户点 header 可手动收起。footer（model + token + retry + 分支切换器）仅在该组收尾本轮且后续无 text 回复时渲染一次（避免与 text 回合 footer 重复计数；retry / branch 复用 ChatPage 现有的 turnUserEntryId 扫描与 branchInfo 逻辑）。Delegate task / MCP App（所有 `mcp__*` 工具，流式中结果未回也直接排除）/ ask_user 等显眼工具卡不受影响（继续独立渲染，确保正在等待用户输入 / 实时流 / iframe UI 的内容不被收起）。仅改 presentation 层，state machine / streaming / 工具执行 / 网关均未变更；纯函数分组 + 单元测试（22 用例覆盖 gộp / ranh giới / 透明消息 / MCP 前缀）

- Fixed chat UI fragmentation when a provider (e.g. antigravity / gemini-3.8-flash via the 9router-go gateway) emits one tool call per assistant turn and reports `stopReason: "stop"` rather than `"toolUse"`. Previously every intermediate turn was treated as turn-closing and rendered its own AgentMessage block with a model name + token counter + retry button, producing N separated blocks with N repeated dividers and footers. Grouping is now content-based (not stopReason-based): consecutive assistant turns that contain only tool calls / thinking and no user-facing text are folded into one ToolRunBlock (`components/chat/tool-run-groups.ts`, a pure helper); the header row shows the tool-call count, the body defaults to expanded so every step (thinking + tool card) is visible, and the user can click the header to manually collapse. The model + token + retry + branch-switcher footer renders at most once per group, only when the group closes the turn (no subsequent text reply) — avoiding double-counting against the text reply's own footer; retry and branch reuse ChatPage's existing turnUserEntryId scan and branchInfo plumbing. Delegate task, MCP App, all `mcp__*` tools (excluded by name prefix so the in-flight → iframe transition never causes a visible flash), and ask_user remain standalone as before so live worker streams, iframe UIs and pending user prompts are never hidden. Presentation-only change — the state machine, streaming, tool execution and gateway are untouched. Pure-function grouping is covered by 22 unit tests (membership, boundaries, transparent messages, MCP prefix)

- 修复 Chat 渲染：单 tool 回合的冗余 footer 兜底。某些 provider（如 antigravity / gemini-3.8-flash 经 9router-go 网关）每个回合只发一个 tool call 且 `stopReason` 报 `stop` 而非 `toolUse`，旧 ChatPage 路径（独立 AgentMessage 渲染）在分组未命中的边缘场景下会把单 tool 回合当成收尾回复、加上 model / token / retry footer，紧跟的 text 回合又渲染自己的 footer——同一条 turn 出现两个 footer 重叠。现对单 tool 回合（`toolCalls.length > 0 && getAssistantText.trim() === ''`）且后面有 closing assistant 时显式把 `isTurnClosing` 置 false：copy / retry / fork / meta / branch 一律不出；model + token 信息由后续 closing 回合的 footer 统一聚合展示。分组 helper 正常命中的路径不受影响（ToolRunBlock 已集中渲染 footer），本改动是分组未命中时的安全网，确保用户在任何情形下都看不到重复 footer。同时也消去了 retry 按钮出现在中间 tool 回合的歧义——retry 一直就该只挂在收尾回合上

- Fixed redundant footer on a single tool-only turn when the grouping helper does not catch it (safety net). Some providers (e.g. antigravity / gemini-3.8-flash via the 9router-go gateway) report `stopReason: "stop"` rather than `"toolUse"` on tool-call turns; in the rare case the new `groupToolRuns` helper does not promote such a turn to a `ToolRunBlock` (where the footer is already centralized), the legacy standalone AgentMessage path used to render its own model / token / retry footer, and the subsequent closing message rendered another — two footers stacked on the same turn. We now force `isTurnClosing = false` for tool-only assistant turns (`toolCalls.length > 0 && getAssistantText.trim() === ''`) when a following closing assistant exists: no copy, no retry, no fork, no meta, no branch for the intermediate round. The aggregated model + tokens are surfaced once on the closing message's footer. Normal grouped path is unaffected (`ToolRunBlock` already centralizes the footer); this change is the safety net so the user never sees two stacked footers regardless of path. Also closes the gap where a retry button used to appear on an intermediate tool-only round

- 修复画布 / HTML 预览里点链接第一次落在空白页、第二次才打开网页的问题：此前的防自导航靠 `<base target="_blank">` 加解析时一次性改写 `target`，拦不住 `target="_parent"`（会逃去代理页，外部站点拒被框架后整块变白）和作者脚本事后插入的链接，而浏览器默认的弹窗导航也可能被 opener 文档替换打断、留下空白标签页。现在内层文档里加了一个 capture 阶段的 click 拦截器，click 当下对 http(s) 链接 `preventDefault()` 并用 `window.open` 在真实用户手势里开新标签，`#anchor` 与 `mailto:` 保持原行为；拾取模式激活时拦截器让路（`window.__cebianInspectActive` 标记），避免「既弹标签又误报拾取」。canvas 与 VFS 的 HTML 预览共用这条修复

- Fixed clicking a link in the canvas / HTML preview landing a blank page on the first click and only opening the site on the second: the old anti-self-navigation relied on `<base target="_blank">` plus a one-time parse-time `target` rewrite, which missed `target="_parent"` anchors (these escaped to the proxy page, where the external site refuses framing and the whole pane went white) and links injected later by author scripts — and the browser's default popup navigation could be cancelled by opener-document replacement, leaving an empty tab. The inner document now runs a capture-phase click interceptor that preventDefaults http(s) anchors and `window.open`s them from the real user gesture; `#anchor` and `mailto:` keep their default behavior, and the interceptor stands down while element picking is active (a `window.__cebianInspectActive` flag) so a click can't both open a tab and report a pick. The fix covers the canvas and the VFS HTML preview alike

- 修复在聊天里点 `#/…html` 链接打开的是空画布的问题。三处缺口叠加：① MV3 service worker 空闲挂起会断开端口并顺带清掉 channel 的 session 绑定，点击触发的 `canvas_open` 请求被静默丢弃；② 重连路径只重发了裸 subscribe 消息、并不恢复这个绑定，用户停留在同一会话时它就一直缺失；③ `canvasOpen` 翻转会让 chat 页整棵子树 remount，port 断开重连把刚广播的 `canvas_opened` 随旧端口一起丢掉——新挂载的画布面板只能停在空态，第二次点击时树已稳定才显示出来。现在：重连时**恢复 session 绑定**；就绪窗口里的打开请求**排队**（只保留最近一个）并在绑定恢复后重放；点击本身会立即触发重连（不等最长 30s 的退避计时器）；并补上了**缺失已久的首帧推送**——`canvas_state` 的类型、处理器与测试早已就位但发送端从未实现，现在 port 接入时把当前所有打开中的画布状态推给新连接，重挂载 / 重连 / SW 重启都能自愈
- Fixed clicking a `#/…html` link in chat opening an empty canvas. Three gaps stacked: ① an idle MV3 service worker drops the port and clears the channel's session binding along with it, silently discarding the `canvas_open` request the click triggers; ② the reconnect path only re-sent the raw subscribe message and never restored that binding, which stayed missing while the user remained on the same session; ③ flipping `canvasOpen` remounts the whole chat-page subtree, and the disconnect/reconnect wiped the just-broadcast `canvas_opened` along with the old port — the freshly mounted canvas pane could only sit at its empty state, and content appeared only on the second click once the tree had settled. Now: the reconnect **restores the session binding**; open requests arriving in the not-ready window are **queued** (only the most recent kept) and replayed once the binding is restored; the click itself triggers an **immediate reconnect** (no more waiting out the up-to-30s backoff timer); and the **long-missing first-frame push** is in place — the `canvas_state` type, handler and tests existed but the sender never did, so a newly connected port now receives the current state of every open canvas, letting a remount, a reconnect or a SW restart self-heal

- 修复 composer 的会话级 pin（文件夹 / 文件 / RAG collection）在离开聊天页时丢失的问题：这类 pin 原本是纯 React state，任何导致聊天页 unmount 的操作——最常见的是打开设置页——都会把它们静默清空，pin 指示条消失，被 ghim 的 RAG collection 也随之失去 `rag_search` 工具。现改为按会话 id 持久化进 `session:composerPinnedSessionPins`（WXT session storage）：打开设置再返回、切换会话离开再回来、甚至关闭侧边栏后重新打开，pin 都还在；不同会话的 pin 各自独立、互不泄漏；浏览器会话结束（关闭浏览器）自动清空。在 `/chat/new` 上做的 pin 会在首条消息创建会话时自动迁移到新会话名下
- Fixed session-scoped composer pins (folders / files / RAG collections) being lost whenever the chat page unmounted: these pins lived in plain React state, so any navigation away from the chat route — most commonly opening Settings — silently cleared them. The pin strip went empty and the pinned RAG collection lost its `rag_search` unlock with it. Pins are now persisted per conversation in `session:composerPinnedSessionPins` (WXT session storage): opening Settings and coming back, switching chats away and back, even closing and reopening the sidepanel all keep the pin; each conversation's pins are independent and never leak into another chat; everything clears automatically when the browser session ends. Pins made on `/chat/new` migrate to the new session automatically when the first message creates it

### 移除 / Removed

- 侧边栏抽屉中移除了 Memory 区块：之前 Collections / MCP / Memory / History 四段中的 Memory 段不再渲染；记忆功能的总开关、整理配置、文件浏览 / 编辑仍可通过 `/settings/memory` 设置页完整访问

- Removed the Memory section from the sidebar drawer: the Memory block that previously sat between MCP and History no longer renders. Memory management (master toggle, organize configuration, file browse / edit) remains fully accessible via the `/settings/memory` settings page

- 知识库（RAG）设置页移除「相关性门槛」输入框：相关性分数在两种检索模式下刻度不同（纯向量是 cosine 0–1，hybrid 是 RRF 0–0.033），不存在对两种模式都正确的默认值——旧的 0.35 是 cosine 时代的数字，切到 hybrid 后会静默过滤掉全部结果。过滤职责现在完全交给 Rerank 开关。该设置项在存储中保留（默认 0 = 不过滤），因此已有配置不会报错

- Knowledge (RAG) settings: the relevance-threshold input is gone. Relevance scores are on different scales per mode (cosine 0–1 for vector-only, RRF 0–0.033 for hybrid), so no single default is right — the old 0.35 was a cosine-era number that silently filtered every result under hybrid. Filtering is now entirely Rerank's job. The setting stays in storage (default 0 = no filtering), so existing configurations keep working

## 1.8.0 - 2026-09-29

### 新增 / Added

- 新增「设置 → 对话 → 自动压缩超长对话」：可开关自动压缩，并用滑杆调整触发阈值（占模型上下文窗口的百分比，默认 80%）。此前触发点写死为「窗口 − 16384 token」，换算成百分比会随窗口漂移——128k 模型 87%、1M 模型要到 98.4% 才压，基本等于压不到就已经撑爆 ([#72](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/72))
- 输入框右下角新增上下文用量环：一眼看出当前会话占了模型上下文窗口的多少，点开显示已用 / 总量、自动压缩的触发比例；用量逼近压缩点时环会变色。数字与自动压缩用的是同一套估算，界面上的占用就是压缩判据比较的那个值
- 新增「设置 → 外观」：可调整对话字号（80%–150%）和对话字体（默认 / 衬线 / 等宽，浏览器支持读取本机字体时还可从本机已安装的字体中选择），只作用于消息与输入框，工具卡片等其余界面保持原样，并带实时预览；主题也可在这里直接选择跟随系统、浅色或深色 ([#75](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/75))
- 新增拖拽添加附件：把文件拖到侧边栏的聊天页（消息区或输入框都可以）即可添加，校验规则与点击上传一致，拖入文件夹会提示不支持；同时，在侧边栏其他位置松开文件也不会再被浏览器直接打开、把整个 Cebian 界面替换掉 ([#55](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/55))
- 新增模型：Claude Opus 5.5、Claude Fable 5.1（Anthropic、GitHub Copilot），GPT-6 Sol / Luna / Astra（OpenAI、OpenAI Codex、GitHub Copilot），Grok 4.7（xAI、GitHub Copilot），Gemini 3.8 Flash（Google、GitHub Copilot），DeepSeek V4.1 Flash（DeepSeek）。OpenAI Codex 下的 GPT-6 Sol / Luna 暂不提供「关闭思考」档

- Added Settings → Chat → Auto-compact long chats: a switch for automatic compaction plus a slider for the trigger threshold (a percentage of the model's context window, 80% by default). The trigger used to be hard-coded at "window − 16384 tokens", which drifts with window size — 87% on a 128k model but 98.4% on a 1M one, so large-window models would blow past the limit before compaction ever ran ([#72](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/72))
- Added a context-usage ring next to the composer: see at a glance how much of the model's context window the current chat is using, and click it for the exact amount and the threshold at which earlier history is compacted. The ring changes colour as usage approaches that threshold. It reports the same estimate automatic compaction uses, so the number on screen is exactly the one the threshold is compared against
- Added Settings → Appearance: adjust the chat font size (80%–150%) and chat font (default / serif / monospace, or — where the browser can read installed fonts — any font installed on your device). It applies to messages and the input box only, leaving tool cards and the rest of the interface unchanged, and comes with a live preview. The theme can also be picked here directly: system, light or dark ([#75](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/75))
- Added drag-and-drop attachments: drop files onto the sidepanel chat page — anywhere over the messages or the input box — to attach them, with the same checks as the upload button; dropped folders are reported as unsupported. Dropping a file anywhere else in the sidepanel no longer makes the browser open it in place of the whole Cebian interface ([#55](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/55))
- Added models: Claude Opus 5.5 and Claude Fable 5.1 (Anthropic, GitHub Copilot), GPT-6 Sol / Luna / Astra (OpenAI, OpenAI Codex, GitHub Copilot), Grok 4.7 (xAI, GitHub Copilot), Gemini 3.8 Flash (Google, GitHub Copilot) and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash (DeepSeek). Under OpenAI Codex, GPT-6 Sol and Luna don't offer the "off" thinking level for now

### 变更 / Changed

- 语音输入需要授权时，重复点击麦克风不再叠开多个授权页或 Chrome 设置页，而是切回已经打开的那个；标签页打不开时会给出提示
- 附件数量上限只计入能添加的文件：一次选中的文件里有不支持或超大的，不再占掉后面合格文件的名额；一批文件只能加进去一部分时，会提示有几个没有添加。粘贴图片与上传一样，只接受 PNG、JPEG、GIF、WebP 和 SVG ([#55](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/55))

- When voice input needs permission, clicking the mic again no longer stacks up duplicate permission or Chrome settings tabs — it switches back to the one already open, and shows a message if the tab can't be opened
- The attachment limit now only counts files that can actually be added: unsupported or oversized files in a selection no longer use up slots meant for the valid ones after them, and when only part of a batch fits you're told how many files were left out. Pasted images are accepted in the same formats as uploads — PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP and SVG ([#55](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/55))

### 移除 / Removed

- OpenAI Codex 的模型列表不再显示 GPT-5.4 和 GPT-5.4 mini：ChatGPT 账户已无法使用这两个模型

- GPT-5.4 and GPT-5.4 mini are no longer listed under OpenAI Codex, as they are no longer available to ChatGPT accounts

### 修复 / Fixed

- 修复 Skill 的 `bgFetch:<match-pattern>` 域名权限可被 HTTP 重定向绕过的问题：受限请求现在会在网络层拒绝重定向，避免请求、凭据或响应流向声明范围外的地址；裸 `bgFetch` 仍保留原有的自动跟随行为
- 修复长会话在上下文写满后彻底卡死、已完成的工作全部白费的问题：AI 连续调用工具收集内容时，现在会在轮次中途自动压缩上下文，而不是只在用户发下一条消息前才压；压缩摘要生成失败时也不再原样重发整段超长上下文（那必然再次报错），改为丢弃最早的历史并在对话里标出；确实压不动时 AI 会主动暂停并说明原因，而不是反复报错。另外修正了中文内容的上下文用量估算——此前按英文经验值估算，中文被低估 2.7–4 倍，导致压缩触发过晚 ([#72](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/72))
- 修复在新设备上用「合并」方式恢复备份时，对话自定义指引、主题、模型、思考档位、自动压缩、页面交互等设置全部没有恢复的问题：合并现在会把本地从未设置过的项从备份补上，本地已经设置过的仍保留本地 ([#76](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/76))
- 修复选完文件立即发送时，还没读取完的附件被悄悄漏掉的问题：现在发送会先等所有已选文件读取完成 ([#55](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/55))
- 修复技能授权卡片等待确认期间，技能权限声明或脚本被修改后仍会执行新内容的问题：授权现在绑定当时的权限与脚本快照，内容变化会拒绝执行并要求重新授权；“始终允许”也只保存卡片中实际展示的权限 ([#79](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/79))

- Fixed an HTTP redirect bypass in Skill `bgFetch:<match-pattern>` URL permissions. Scoped requests now reject redirects at the network layer so requests, credentials and responses cannot escape the declared URL scope; unrestricted `bgFetch` keeps its existing automatic-follow behavior
- Fixed long chats wedging permanently once the context filled up, losing all the work already done. While the AI is making back-to-back tool calls, the context is now compacted mid-turn instead of only before the user's next message; when summarization fails, the over-long context is no longer resent verbatim (which could only fail again) — the earliest history is dropped and marked in the transcript instead; and when nothing can be compacted any further the AI pauses itself and explains why instead of failing over and over. Context-usage estimates for Chinese text were also corrected — they previously used an English-tuned heuristic that underestimated Chinese by 2.7–4×, so compaction triggered far too late ([#72](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/72))
- Fixed restoring a backup with "Merge" on a new device leaving custom chat instructions, theme, model, thinking level, auto-compaction, page-interaction and similar settings unrestored. Merge now fills in any setting that was never set locally from the backup, while settings you have already changed locally still keep their local value ([#76](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/76))
- Fixed attachments being silently dropped when you sent a message right after picking files that were still being read: sending now waits until every selected file has finished loading ([#55](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/55))
- Fixed a Skill executing modified permissions or script content when it changed while its permission card was awaiting confirmation. Authorization is now bound to the permissions and script snapshot shown at approval time; changed content is rejected and must be authorized again, and “Always allow” persists only the permissions actually displayed ([#79](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/79))

## 1.7.1 - 2026-09-15

### 新增 / Added

- 新增「设置 → 对话 → 联网搜索」：可调整 Bing、Brave、Google、DuckDuckGo、百度的顺序与启停，修改搜索地址、抽取脚本和适用场景，也可添加自定义引擎（如 Kagi、Startpage、自建 SearXNG）。AI 改用 `web_search` 工具取得标题、链接和摘要，不再自行拼接搜索地址、逐个打开引擎结果页；工具默认按配置顺序尝试，支持优先使用指定引擎，被拦截、没有结果或执行失败时自动尝试其余引擎，都没有结果时汇总各引擎的情况 ([#63](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/63))
- 支持从历史任意一轮的收尾 assistant 回复处分叉出独立的新会话：回复操作行新增「分叉」按钮，新会话保留截至该回复（含）的完整上下文，并复制分叉时的工作区文件，标题、模型、思考档沿用源会话且此后互不影响；历史面板给分叉会话标注来源，点击可回到源会话 ([#60](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/60))
- 支持给会话改名：点击页头标题、或在历史面板的会话菜单里选「重命名」，即可行内编辑（Enter 保存、Esc 取消），各窗口的页头与历史列表同步更新；改名不会改变会话在历史列表里的顺序
- 新增「设置 → 对话 → 自动生成会话标题」（默认开启）：首轮回复结束后，用 AI 生成的简短标题替换「首条消息截断」的默认标题；可关闭，也可指定生成标题所用的模型（默认与对话模型相同）。首轮期间已手动改名的会话不会被覆盖

- Added Settings → Chat → Web search: reorder or enable and disable Bing, Brave, Google, DuckDuckGo and Baidu; edit search URLs, extraction scripts and best-for hints; or add custom engines (Kagi, Startpage, a self-hosted SearXNG, …). The AI now uses the `web_search` tool to get titles, links and snippets instead of constructing search URLs and opening engine result pages itself. The tool follows the configured order by default, can try a specified engine first, automatically moves on after blocked searches, empty results or failures, and reports each engine's outcome when nothing usable was found ([#63](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/63))
- Fork an independent new chat from any turn-closing assistant reply in the history: a Fork button in the reply's action row creates a chat that keeps the full context up to and including that reply and copies the workspace files as they are at fork time, inheriting the source chat's title, model and thinking level while staying independent from then on; the history panel marks forked chats with their source, which opens the source chat on click ([#60](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/60))
- Rename chats: click the title in the header, or pick "Rename" from a chat's menu in the history panel, to edit it inline (Enter saves, Esc cancels); the header and history lists in every window update together, and renaming does not change the chat's position in the history list
- Added Settings → Chat → Auto-generate chat titles (on by default): after the first reply, the default "first message, truncated" title is replaced with a short AI-generated one; it can be turned off, and the model used for titles can be chosen (defaults to the conversation model). Chats renamed by hand during the first turn are left alone

### 变更 / Changed

- 设置页导航从 11 个平行入口收成 3 组 9 项：「连接」（AI 提供商、MCP 服务器）、「定制」（对话、提示词、技能、记忆、页面交互）、「系统」（数据、关于）。原「指引」与「高级」合并为「对话」，原「备份与恢复」与「文件系统」合并为「数据」，各项的具体设置不变。窄屏侧边栏里当前分节的图标会展开显示名称，组与组之间用分隔线区分；宽屏左栏显示分组标题。旧的 `#/instructions`、`#/advanced`、`#/backup`、`#/storage` 链接会自动跳到合并后的新分节
- 新会话的默认标题统一为「首条消息压成一行后取前 50 字」，超长以 … 结尾（此前侧边栏用 `...`、划词「在侧边栏继续」取 48 字）

- The Settings navigation collapses 11 flat entries into 3 groups of 9: Connect (AI providers, MCP servers), Customize (Chat, Prompts, Skills, Memory, Page interaction) and System (Data, About). The former Instructions and Advanced sections merge into Chat, and Backup & Restore plus Filesystem merge into Data; every individual setting stays as it was. In the narrow sidepanel the active section's icon expands to show its name and groups are separated by dividers; the wide layout shows group headings in the sidebar. Old `#/instructions`, `#/advanced`, `#/backup` and `#/storage` links redirect to the merged sections
- The default title of a new chat is now consistently the first message collapsed to one line and cut to 50 characters, ending in … when truncated (previously the sidepanel used `...` and "Continue in sidepanel" cut at 48)

### 修复 / Fixed

- 调用 MCP 工具的对话在回答完成几十秒后「消失」、只剩转圈的工具卡片：MCP 服务端不返回 `structuredContent` 时，工具结果里会多出一个值为 undefined 的字段，导致该结果及本轮之后的所有消息都无法写入会话存储；扩展后台被浏览器回收后重新打开，就只剩下第一次工具调用之前的内容。现已修正字段写法，并在存储层递归剔除这类字段、对仍不合规的消息用 JSON 归一化后再写入，不再让一条消息卡住整轮对话的保存 ([#74](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/74))

- 修复后台运行的「定时任务」工具在 SW 内调用时偶发卡在「The message port closed before a response was received」：MV3 中 `chrome.runtime.sendMessage` 从 SW 调起自身 `onMessage` 不可靠（消息未到 / channel 在 async reply 之前关闭），agent loop 跑的 `scheduler_list` / `scheduler_create` / `scheduler_update` / `scheduler_delete` / `scheduler_run_now` 走 `sendAndReceive` 路径时首屏列表拉不到、创建后无回信。改为双路由：BG 在 `setupSchedulerClientHandlers()` 注册一个 `SchedulerDirectPlugin`，`sendAndReceive` 命中时直接调 `dispatchDirect`（同 handler map、同 synthetic port 捕首条 post），不绕 message port；sidepanel 仍走原有 `chrome.runtime.sendMessage` 桥（extension page → SW 是 Chrome 原生支持的路径）。`entrypoints/background/ipc/client-router.ts` 同步暴露 `_internal.resetForTest()` 让多 case 复用 handler 表

- Replies that used an MCP tool "vanished" tens of seconds after finishing, leaving only spinning tool cards: when the MCP server returned no `structuredContent`, the tool result carried a field whose value was undefined, so that result and every later message of the turn could not be written to chat storage; once the browser suspended the extension's background worker and it came back, only the content before the first tool call was left. The field is now written correctly, the storage layer strips such fields recursively, and a message that still fails the durability check is JSON-normalized before writing, so one message can no longer block saving the rest of the turn ([#74](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/74))

- Fixed "The message port closed before a response was received" on scheduler tools invoked from inside the BG SW (e.g. agent loop calling `scheduler_list` / `scheduler_create` / `scheduler_update` / `scheduler_delete` / `scheduler_run_now`): in MV3, `chrome.runtime.sendMessage` from a SW to its own `onMessage` listener is unreliable (the message may not fire, or the channel closes before the async reply lands). Replaced the single `sendMessage` path with two routes: BG installs a `SchedulerDirectPlugin` in `setupSchedulerClientHandlers()`, and `sendAndReceive` calls `dispatchDirect` (same handler map, same synthetic-port reply capture) when the plugin is present — no message-port round trip. Sidepanel / Settings UI still go through the original `chrome.runtime.sendMessage` bridge because extension-page → SW is the path Chrome supports natively. `entrypoints/background/ipc/client-router.ts` exposes `_internal.resetForTest()` so multiple test cases can reset the module-level handler table without re-imports

## 1.7.0 - 2026-09-05

## 1.5.1 - 2026-08-22

### 新增 / Added

- 官网首页与安装文档新增 Firefox 附加组件商店入口
- AI 回复中的 LaTeX 数学公式现在渲染为真正的公式（支持行内 `$...$`、`\(...\)` 与块级 `$$...$$`、`\[...\]`），深浅色主题下均清晰可读；块级公式悬停可复制 LaTeX 源码；朗读时公式读作其 LaTeX 源码，货币金额（如 $5）不会被误认成公式 ([#61](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/61))
- 悬浮球、划词工具条与每个工具条动作现在都能限定生效页面：在「设置 → 页面交互」里各自配置「仅在这些页面生效」与「但排除这些页面」（Chrome match pattern，如 `*://*.example.com/*`；留空即所有页面，排除优先）
- 页面匹配规则说明新增「查看配置规则」入口，可直接打开 MDN 的完整语法文档
- 划词工具条的动作现在可以自己配置：内置的解释 / 翻译 / 总结可改系统提示词、限定生效页面、调整顺序或关掉；内置提示词会按界面语言提供默认内容，未覆盖时自动回落，编辑页中的设置也可一键重置。也可以新建自己的动作，自定义按钮名称，并用 `{{selected_text}}` 等变量写提示词模板（可用变量：选中文本、选区周边文本、页面 URL、页面标题、当前日期、界面语言）。动作列表集中展示名称、范围与启用状态，排序和删除等低频操作收在「更多操作」菜单
- 划词动作支持输出后处理脚本：写一个 `transform(text, vars)` 函数对 AI 的输出做加工，返回要显示的字符串；`vars` 与提示词模板同一套环境变量。脚本在一次性沙箱中执行——拿不到任何浏览器扩展 API，跑完或超时（5 秒）即销毁，不会影响其它功能；脚本出错时原样显示原始输出并附上错误原因，方便排查。脚本仍能访问网络，故只运行你自己信得过的脚本

- Added a Firefox Add-ons store link to the website homepage and installation guide
- LaTeX math in AI responses now renders as real formulas (inline `$...$` / `\(...\)` and block `$$...$$` / `\[...\]`), clearly readable in both light and dark themes; hover a block formula to copy its LaTeX source; read-aloud speaks the LaTeX source, and currency amounts (e.g. $5) are no longer mistaken for math ([#61](https://github.com/maotoumao/Cebian/issues/61))
- The floating ball, the selection toolbar and every toolbar action can now be scoped to specific pages: each has its own "show only on these pages" and "except on these pages" lists under Settings → Page interaction (Chrome match patterns such as `*://*.example.com/*`; empty means every page, and exceptions win)
